#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Tests unitaires pour le système de navigation drone.

Usage:
    python3 -m pytest test_navigation_node.py
    python3 -m pytest test_navigation_node.py::TestNavigationNode::test_node_initialization
"""

import unittest
import pytest
import rclpy
from rclpy.node import Node
from rclpy.executors import SingleThreadedExecutor
from geometry_msgs.msg import Point, PoseStamped
from std_msgs.msg import String
import json
import time
import threading
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch, MagicMock

# Import des modules à tester
import sys
import os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', 'drone_navigation'))

from navigation_node import NavigationNode
from trajectory_planner import TrajectoryPlanner
from position_controller import PositionController


class TestNavigationNode(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests pour le nœud principal de navigation."""
    
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        """Setup de la classe de tests."""
        rclpy.init()
    
    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        """Teardown de la classe de tests."""
        rclpy.shutdown()
    
    def setUp(self):
        """Setup avant chaque test."""
        self.node = NavigationNode()
        self.executor = SingleThreadedExecutor()
        self.executor.add_node(self.node)
        
        # Thread pour l'exécuteur
        self.executor_thread = threading.Thread(target=self.executor.spin)
        self.executor_thread.daemon = True
        self.executor_thread.start()
        
        time.sleep(0.1)  # Attendre l'initialisation
    
    def tearDown(self):
        """Teardown après chaque test."""
        self.node.destroy_node()
        self.executor.shutdown()
        if self.executor_thread.is_alive():
            self.executor_thread.join(timeout=1.0)
    
    def test_node_initialization(self):
        """Test l'initialisation du nœud."""
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.node)
        self.assertEqual(self.node.get_name(), 'navigation_node')
        
        # Vérifier que les composants sont initialisés
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.node._trajectory_planner)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.node._position_controller)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.node._coverage_patterns)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.node._obstacle_avoidance)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.node._geofence_manager)
    
    def test_lifecycle_transitions(self):
        """Test les transitions de lifecycle."""
        # Test configure
        result = self.node.on_configure(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.assertEqual(result.id, 2)  # TRANSITION_CALLBACK_SUCCESS
        
        # Test activate
        result = self.node.on_activate(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.assertEqual(result.id, 2)
        
        # Test deactivate
        result = self.node.on_deactivate(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.assertEqual(result.id, 2)
        
        # Test cleanup
        result = self.node.on_cleanup(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.assertEqual(result.id, 2)
    
    def test_status_publishing(self):
        """Test la publication du statut."""
        # Activer le nœud
        self.node.on_configure(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.node.on_activate(self.node.get_current_state())
        
        # Créer un subscriber pour capturer le statut
        status_messages = []
        
        def status_callback(msg):
            status_messages.append(msg.data)
        
        status_sub = self.node.create_subscription(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/status',
            status_callback,
            10
        )
        
        # Attendre quelques publications
        time.sleep(2.0)
        
        # Vérifier qu'on a reçu des messages
        self.assertGreater(len(status_messages), 0)
        
        # Vérifier le format JSON
        for status_str in status_messages:
            try:
                status_data = json.loads(status_str)
                self.assertIn('state', status_data)
                self.assertIn('timestamp', status_data)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                self.fail("Status message is not valid JSON")
    
    @patch('drone_navigation.navigation_node.NavigationNode._update_mavros_setpoint')
    def test_goto_position_service(self, mock_mavros):
        """Test le service goto_position."""
        # Activer le nœud
        self.node.on_configure(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.node.on_activate(self.node.get_current_state())
        
        # Créer un client pour le service
        from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan
        
        client = self.node.create_client(GetPlan, '/drone_nav/goto_position')
        self.assertTrue(client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0))
        
        # Créer une requête
        request = GetPlan.Request()
        request.goal.pose.position.x = 10.0
        request.goal.pose.position.y = 20.0
        request.goal.pose.position.z = 15.0
        
        # Envoyer la requête
        future = client.call_async(request)
        
        # Attendre la réponse
        start_time = time.time()
        while not future.done() and (time.time() - start_time) < 5.0:
            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        self.assertTrue(future.done())
        response = future.result()
        self.assertIsNotNone(response)
        self.assertGreater(len(response.plan.poses), 0)
    
    def test_emergency_stop(self):
        """Test l'arrêt d'urgence."""
        # Activer le nœud
        self.node.on_configure(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.node.on_activate(self.node.get_current_state())
        
        # Déclencher un arrêt d'urgence
        self.node._emergency_stop("Test emergency")
        
        # Vérifier que l'état a changé
        self.assertEqual(self.node._current_state, "emergency")
        self.assertEqual(self.node._current_mode, "emergency_stop")


class TestTrajectoryPlanner(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests pour le planificateur de trajectoire."""
    
    def setUp(self):
        """Setup avant chaque test."""
        rclpy.init()
        self.planner = TrajectoryPlanner()
    
    def tearDown(self):
        """Teardown après chaque test."""
        rclpy.shutdown()
    
    def test_a_star_planning(self):
        """Test la planification A*."""
        start = Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=15.0)
        goal = Point(x=10.0, y=10.0, z=15.0)
        
        path = self.planner.plan_a_star(start, goal)
        
        self.assertIsNotNone(path)
        self.assertGreater(len(path), 0)
        
        # Vérifier que le chemin commence au point de départ
        first_point = path[0]
        self.assertAlmostEqual(first_point.x, start.x, places=1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(first_point.y, start.y, places=1)
        
        # Vérifier que le chemin finit au point d'arrivée
        last_point = path[-1]
        self.assertAlmostEqual(last_point.x, goal.x, places=1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(last_point.y, goal.y, places=1)
    
    def test_rrt_star_planning(self):
        """Test la planification RRT*."""
        start = Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=15.0)
        goal = Point(x=15.0, y=15.0, z=15.0)
        
        path = self.planner.plan_rrt_star(start, goal)
        
        self.assertIsNotNone(path)
        self.assertGreater(len(path), 0)
        
        # Vérifier la continuité du chemin
        for i in range(1, len(path)):
            distance = ((path[i].x - path[i-1].x)**2 + 
                       (path[i].y - path[i-1].y)**2 + 
                       (path[i].z - path[i-1].z)**2)**0.5
            self.assertLess(distance, 10.0)  # Pas de saut trop grand
    
    def test_bezier_smoothing(self):
        """Test le lissage par courbes de Bézier."""
        # Créer un chemin avec des angles vifs
        waypoints = [
            Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=15.0),
            Point(x=10.0, y=0.0, z=15.0),
            Point(x=10.0, y=10.0, z=15.0),
            Point(x=0.0, y=10.0, z=15.0)
        ]
        
        smoothed_path = self.planner.smooth_path_bezier(waypoints)
        
        self.assertIsNotNone(smoothed_path)
        self.assertGreater(len(smoothed_path), len(waypoints))
        
        # Vérifier que le chemin lissé respecte les points de départ et d'arrivée
        self.assertAlmostEqual(smoothed_path[0].x, waypoints[0].x, places=1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(smoothed_path[0].y, waypoints[0].y, places=1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(smoothed_path[-1].x, waypoints[-1].x, places=1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(smoothed_path[-1].y, waypoints[-1].y, places=1)
    
    def test_path_validation(self):
        """Test la validation de chemin."""
        # Chemin valide
        valid_path = [
            Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=15.0),
            Point(x=5.0, y=5.0, z=15.0),
            Point(x=10.0, y=10.0, z=15.0)
        ]
        
        self.assertTrue(self.planner.validate_path(valid_path))
        
        # Chemin invalide (saut trop grand)
        invalid_path = [
            Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=15.0),
            Point(x=100.0, y=100.0, z=15.0)  # Saut trop grand
        ]
        
        self.assertFalse(self.planner.validate_path(invalid_path))
    
    def test_obstacle_aware_planning(self):
        """Test la planification avec évitement d'obstacles."""
        start = Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=15.0)
        goal = Point(x=10.0, y=10.0, z=15.0)
        
        # Ajouter un obstacle
        obstacles = [
            {'center': Point(x=5.0, y=5.0, z=15.0), 'radius': 2.0}
        ]
        
        path = self.planner.plan_with_obstacles(start, goal, obstacles)
        
        self.assertIsNotNone(path)
        self.assertGreater(len(path), 0)
        
        # Vérifier que le chemin évite l'obstacle
        for point in path:
            distance_to_obstacle = ((point.x - 5.0)**2 + 
                                  (point.y - 5.0)**2 + 
                                  (point.z - 15.0)**2)**0.5
            self.assertGreaterEqual(distance_to_obstacle, 1.8)  # Marge de sécurité


class TestPositionController(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests pour le contrôleur de position."""
    
    def setUp(self):
        """Setup avant chaque test."""
        rclpy.init()
        node = Node('test_node')
        self.controller = PositionController(node)
    
    def tearDown(self):
        """Teardown après chaque test."""
        rclpy.shutdown()
    
    def test_pid_controller_initialization(self):
        """Test l'initialisation des contrôleurs PID."""
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.controller._position_pid_x)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.controller._position_pid_y)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.controller._position_pid_z)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.controller._attitude_pid_roll)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.controller._attitude_pid_pitch)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.controller._attitude_pid_yaw)
    
    def test_position_control(self):
        """Test le contrôle de position."""
        # Position actuelle
        current_pos = Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=10.0)
        
        # Position cible
        target_pos = Point(x=5.0, y=5.0, z=15.0)
        
        # Calculer la commande
        control_output = self.controller.compute_position_control(
            current_pos, target_pos, dt=0.02
        )
        
        self.assertIsNotNone(control_output)
        
        # Vérifier que la commande va dans la bonne direction
        self.assertGreater(control_output.linear.x, 0)  # Mouvement vers +x
        self.assertGreater(control_output.linear.y, 0)  # Mouvement vers +y
        self.assertGreater(control_output.linear.z, 0)  # Mouvement vers +z
    
    def test_pid_limits(self):
        """Test les limites des contrôleurs PID."""
        # Position très éloignée pour tester les limites
        current_pos = Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=10.0)
        target_pos = Point(x=1000.0, y=1000.0, z=100.0)
        
        control_output = self.controller.compute_position_control(
            current_pos, target_pos, dt=0.02
        )
        
        # Vérifier que les limites sont respectées
        max_velocity = 10.0  # m/s
        self.assertLessEqual(abs(control_output.linear.x), max_velocity)
        self.assertLessEqual(abs(control_output.linear.y), max_velocity)
        self.assertLessEqual(abs(control_output.linear.z), max_velocity)
    
    def test_convergence_detection(self):
        """Test la détection de convergence."""
        # Position proche de la cible
        current_pos = Point(x=9.9, y=9.9, z=14.9)
        target_pos = Point(x=10.0, y=10.0, z=15.0)
        
        # Vitesse faible
        current_vel = Point(x=0.1, y=0.1, z=0.1)
        
        is_converged = self.controller.check_convergence(
            current_pos, target_pos, current_vel
        )
        
        self.assertTrue(is_converged)
        
        # Position éloignée
        current_pos = Point(x=5.0, y=5.0, z=10.0)
        
        is_converged = self.controller.check_convergence(
            current_pos, target_pos, current_vel
        )
        
        self.assertFalse(is_converged)
    
    def test_control_mode_switching(self):
        """Test le changement de mode de contrôle."""
        # Test mode position_hold
        self.controller.set_control_mode("position_hold")
        self.assertEqual(self.controller._current_mode, "position_hold")
        
        # Test mode guided
        self.controller.set_control_mode("guided")
        self.assertEqual(self.controller._current_mode, "guided")
        
        # Test mode invalide
        result = self.controller.set_control_mode("invalid_mode")
        self.assertFalse(result)


class TestIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests d'intégration du système complet."""
    
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        """Setup de la classe de tests."""
        rclpy.init()
    
    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        """Teardown de la classe de tests."""
        rclpy.shutdown()
    
    def setUp(self):
        """Setup avant chaque test."""
        self.node = NavigationNode()
        self.executor = SingleThreadedExecutor()
        self.executor.add_node(self.node)
        
        # Thread pour l'exécuteur
        self.executor_thread = threading.Thread(target=self.executor.spin)
        self.executor_thread.daemon = True
        self.executor_thread.start()
        
        # Activer le nœud
        self.node.on_configure(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.node.on_activate(self.node.get_current_state())
        
        time.sleep(0.1)
    
    def tearDown(self):
        """Teardown après chaque test."""
        self.node.destroy_node()
        self.executor.shutdown()
        if self.executor_thread.is_alive():
            self.executor_thread.join(timeout=1.0)
    
    @patch('drone_navigation.navigation_node.NavigationNode._update_mavros_setpoint')
    def test_complete_navigation_workflow(self, mock_mavros):
        """Test le workflow complet de navigation."""
        # 1. Planifier une trajectoire
        from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan
        
        client = self.node.create_client(GetPlan, '/drone_nav/goto_position')
        self.assertTrue(client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0))
        
        request = GetPlan.Request()
        request.goal.pose.position.x = 10.0
        request.goal.pose.position.y = 10.0
        request.goal.pose.position.z = 15.0
        
        future = client.call_async(request)
        
        # Attendre la planification
        start_time = time.time()
        while not future.done() and (time.time() - start_time) < 10.0:
            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        self.assertTrue(future.done())
        response = future.result()
        self.assertIsNotNone(response)
        self.assertGreater(len(response.plan.poses), 0)
        
        # 2. Vérifier que l'exécution commence
        time.sleep(1.0)
        
        # Le mock MAVROS devrait avoir été appelé
        self.assertTrue(mock_mavros.called)
    
    def test_error_handling(self):
        """Test la gestion d'erreurs."""
        # Test avec une position invalide (hors limites)
        from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan
        
        client = self.node.create_client(GetPlan, '/drone_nav/goto_position')
        self.assertTrue(client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0))
        
        request = GetPlan.Request()
        request.goal.pose.position.x = 1000.0  # Position très éloignée
        request.goal.pose.position.y = 1000.0
        request.goal.pose.position.z = 1000.0
        
        future = client.call_async(request)
        
        start_time = time.time()
        while not future.done() and (time.time() - start_time) < 10.0:
            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        self.assertTrue(future.done())
        response = future.result()
        
        # La planification devrait échouer ou retourner un chemin vide
        # selon la configuration de sécurité
        self.assertIsNotNone(response)


class TestPerformance(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests de performance du système."""
    
    def setUp(self):
        """Setup avant chaque test."""
        rclpy.init()
        self.planner = TrajectoryPlanner()
    
    def tearDown(self):
        """Teardown après chaque test."""
        rclpy.shutdown()
    
    def test_planning_performance(self):
        """Test les performances de planification."""
        start = Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=15.0)
        goal = Point(x=50.0, y=50.0, z=15.0)
        
        # Test A*
        start_time = time.time()
        path_astar = self.planner.plan_a_star(start, goal)
        astar_time = time.time() - start_time
        
        self.assertLess(astar_time, 1.0)  # Moins d'une seconde
        self.assertIsNotNone(path_astar)
        
        # Test RRT*
        start_time = time.time()
        path_rrt = self.planner.plan_rrt_star(start, goal)
        rrt_time = time.time() - start_time
        
        self.assertLess(rrt_time, 2.0)  # Moins de deux secondes
        self.assertIsNotNone(path_rrt)
        
        print(f"A* time: {astar_time:.3f}s, RRT* time: {rrt_time:.3f}s")
    
    def test_memory_usage(self):
        """Test l'utilisation mémoire."""
        import psutil
        import os
        
        process = psutil.Process(os.getpid())
        initial_memory = process.memory_info().rss / 1024 / 1024  # MB
        
        # Effectuer plusieurs planifications
        start = Point(x=0.0, y=0.0, z=15.0)
        
        for i in range(100):
            goal = Point(x=float(i), y=float(i), z=15.0)
            path = self.planner.plan_a_star(start, goal)
            self.assertIsNotNone(path)
        
        final_memory = process.memory_info().rss / 1024 / 1024  # MB
        memory_increase = final_memory - initial_memory
        
        # La consommation mémoire ne devrait pas augmenter de façon excessive
        self.assertLess(memory_increase, 100)  # Moins de 100 MB
        
        print(f"Memory increase: {memory_increase:.2f} MB")


if __name__ == '__main__':
    # Configuration des tests
    pytest.main([
        __file__,
        '-v',
        '--tb=short',
        '--color=yes'
    ])

"""
Tests d'intégration pour le système de navigation.

class TestNavigationIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests d'intégration avec les systèmes externes."""
    
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        """Setup de la classe de tests."""
        rclpy.init()
    
    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        """Teardown de la classe de tests."""
        rclpy.shutdown()
    
    def setUp(self):
        """Setup avant chaque test."""
        self.node = NavigationNode()
        self.test_node = Node('test_integration_node')
        
        self.executor = MultiThreadedExecutor(num_threads=4)
        self.executor.add_node(self.node)
        self.executor.add_node(self.test_node)
        
        # Thread pour l'exécuteur
        self.executor_thread = threading.Thread(target=self.executor.spin)
        self.executor_thread.daemon = True
        self.executor_thread.start()
        
        # Activer le système de navigation
        self.node.on_configure(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.node.on_activate(self.node.get_current_state())
        
        time.sleep(0.5)  # Laisser le temps à l'initialisation
    
    def tearDown(self):
        """Teardown après chaque test."""
        self.node.destroy_node()
        self.test_node.destroy_node()
        self.executor.shutdown()
        if self.executor_thread.is_alive():
            self.executor_thread.join(timeout=2.0)
    
    def test_mavros_position_integration(self):
        """Test l'intégration avec les données de position MAVROS."""
        # Simuler des données de position MAVROS
        position_data = []
        
        def position_callback(msg):
            position_data.append(msg)
        
        # Subscriber pour capturer les setpoints envoyés à MAVROS
        setpoint_sub = self.test_node.create_subscription(
            PoseStamped,
            '/mavros/setpoint_position/local',
            position_callback,
            10
        )
        
        # Publisher pour simuler la position actuelle
        position_pub = self.test_node.create_publisher(
            PoseStamped,
            '/mavros/local_position/pose',
            10
        )
        
        # Publier une position actuelle
        current_pose = PoseStamped()
        current_pose.header.frame_id = "map"
        current_pose.pose.position.x = 0.0
        current_pose.pose.position.y = 0.0
        current_pose.pose.position.z = 10.0
        
        # Publier la position plusieurs fois
        for _ in range(5):
            position_pub.publish(current_pose)
            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        # Envoyer une commande de navigation
        from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan
        
        client = self.test_node.create_client(GetPlan, '/drone_nav/goto_position')
        self.assertTrue(client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0))
        
        request = GetPlan.Request()
        request.goal.pose.position.x = 10.0
        request.goal.pose.position.y = 10.0
        request.goal.pose.position.z = 15.0
        
        future = client.call_async(request)
        
        # Attendre la réponse
        start_time = time.time()
        while not future.done() and (time.time() - start_time) < 10.0:
            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        self.assertTrue(future.done())
        
        # Attendre que des setpoints soient envoyés
        time.sleep(2.0)
        
        # Vérifier qu'on a reçu des setpoints
        self.assertGreater(len(position_data), 0)
        
        # Vérifier que les setpoints sont cohérents
        for setpoint in position_data:
            self.assertIsInstance(setpoint.pose.position.x, float)
            self.assertIsInstance(setpoint.pose.position.y, float)
            self.assertIsInstance(setpoint.pose.position.z, float)
    
    def test_mavros_velocity_integration(self):
        """Test l'intégration avec les données de vélocité."""
        velocity_data = []

        current_vel.twist.linear.y = 0.5
        current_vel.twist.linear.z = 0.0
        
        for _ in range(5):

        request.goal.pose.position.x = 5.0
        request.goal.pose.position.y = 5.0
        request.goal.pose.position.z = 15.0

        start_time = time.time()
        while not future.done() and (time.time() - start_time) < 10.0:
            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        time.sleep(2.0)
        
        # Vérifier la réception de commandes de vélocité
        # (Dépend de la configuration du contrôleur)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(velocity_data), 0)

            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        # Vérifier que le système de navigation peut utiliser les données GPS
        # (Test basique - l'implémentation complète nécessiterait des transformations GPS)
        time.sleep(1.0)
        
        # Le test vérifie simplement que les données sont reçues sans erreur
        self.assertTrue(True)  # Placeholder - à étendre selon l'implémentation

    def test_status_publishing_integration(self):
        """Test la publication du statut en intégration."""
        status_messages = []
        
        def status_callback(msg):
            try:
                status_data = json.loads(msg.data)
                status_messages.append(status_data)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                pass
        
        # Subscriber pour le statut
        status_sub = self.test_node.create_subscription(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/status',
            status_callback,
            10
        )
        
        # Simuler des données d'entrée
        position_pub = self.test_node.create_publisher(
            PoseStamped,
            '/mavros/local_position/pose',
            10
        )
        
        velocity_pub = self.test_node.create_publisher(
            TwistStamped,
            '/mavros/local_position/velocity_local',
            10
        )
        
        # Publier des données
        for i in range(10):
            pose = PoseStamped()
            pose.pose.position.x = float(i)
            pose.pose.position.y = float(i)
            pose.pose.position.z = 15.0
            position_pub.publish(pose)
            
            vel = TwistStamped()
            vel.twist.linear.x = 1.0
            velocity_pub.publish(vel)
            
            time.sleep(0.2)
        
        # Vérifier qu'on reçoit des messages de statut
        self.assertGreater(len(status_messages), 0)
        
        # Vérifier le contenu des messages
        for status in status_messages:
            self.assertIn('state', status)
            self.assertIn('timestamp', status)
            self.assertIn('current_position', status)

    def test_coverage_pattern_generation(self):
        """Test la génération de motifs de couverture avec services standards."""
        from std_srvs.srv import SetBool
        from geometry_msgs.msg import Polygon, Point32
        
        # Créer un client pour le service de waypoints
        client = self.test_node.create_client(
            SetBool,
            '/drone_nav/set_waypoints'
        )
        
        self.assertTrue(client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0))
        
        # Test avec waypoints activés
        request = SetBool.Request()
        request.data = True
        
        # Envoyer la requête
        future = client.call_async(request)
        
        # Attendre la réponse
        rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(self.test_node, future, timeout_sec=5.0)
        
        # Vérifier la réponse
        self.assertIsNotNone(future.result())
        response = future.result()
        self.assertTrue(response.success)
        
        # Test désactivation
        request.data = False
        future = client.call_async(request)
        rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(self.test_node, future, timeout_sec=5.0)
        
        self.assertIsNotNone(future.result())
        response = future.result()
        self.assertTrue(response.success)
        future = client.call_async(request)
        
        start_time = time.time()
        while not future.done() and (time.time() - start_time) < 10.0:
            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        self.assertTrue(future.done())
        response = future.result()
        
        self.assertIsNotNone(response)
        self.assertTrue(response.success)
        self.assertGreater(len(response.path.poses), 0)
        
        # Vérifier que tous les points sont à la bonne altitude
        for pose in response.path.poses:
            self.assertAlmostEqual(pose.pose.position.z, 15.0, places=1)
        
        # Vérifier que le motif couvre la zone
        x_coords = [p.pose.position.x for p in response.path.poses]
        y_coords = [p.pose.position.y for p in response.path.poses]
        
        self.assertGreaterEqual(min(x_coords), -1.0)  # Avec une petite marge
        self.assertLessEqual(max(x_coords), 21.0)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(min(y_coords), -1.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(max(y_coords), 21.0)
    
    def test_multiple_pattern_types(self):
        """Test différents types de services."""
        from std_srvs.srv import Trigger
        from geometry_msgs.msg import Polygon, Point32
        
        # Test des différents services de navigation
        services_to_test = [
            '/drone_nav/start_mission',
            '/drone_nav/pause_mission',
            '/drone_nav/resume_mission'
        ]
        
        for service_name in services_to_test:
            with self.subTest(service=service_name):
                client = self.test_node.create_client(Trigger, service_name)
                
                if client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=2.0):
                    request = Trigger.Request()
                    future = client.call_async(request)
                    rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(self.test_node, future, timeout_sec=3.0)
                    
                    if future.result() is not None:
                        response = future.result()
                        # Les services peuvent retourner succès ou échec selon l'état
                        self.assertIsInstance(response.success, bool)
                        self.assertIsInstance(response.message, str)
                
                future = client.call_async(request)
                
                start_time = time.time()
                while not future.done() and (time.time() - start_time) < 15.0:
                    time.sleep(0.1)
                
                self.assertTrue(future.done(), f"Timeout for pattern {pattern}")
                response = future.result()
                
                self.assertIsNotNone(response, f"No response for pattern {pattern}")
                self.assertTrue(response.success, f"Failed to generate pattern {pattern}")
                self.assertGreater(len(response.path.poses), 0, f"Empty path for pattern {pattern}")

    def test_obstacle_detection_integration(self):

        from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan
        
        client = self.test_node.create_client(GetPlan, '/drone_nav/goto_position')
        self.assertTrue(client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0))

        request.goal.pose.position.y = 15.0
        request.goal.pose.position.z = 15.0
        
        future = client.call_async(request)
        
        start_time = time.time()
        while not future.done() and (time.time() - start_time) < 10.0:
            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        self.assertTrue(future.done())
        response = future.result()
        
        # Le système devrait pouvoir planifier malgré les obstacles
        self.assertIsNotNone(response)


class TestGeofenceIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests d'intégration pour le système de geofencing."""
    
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        """Setup de la classe de tests."""
        rclpy.init()
    
    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        """Teardown de la classe de tests."""
        rclpy.shutdown()
    
    def setUp(self):
        """Setup avant chaque test."""
        self.node = NavigationNode()
        self.test_node = Node('test_geofence_node')
        
        self.executor = MultiThreadedExecutor(num_threads=2)
        self.executor.add_node(self.node)
        self.executor.add_node(self.test_node)
        
        self.executor_thread = threading.Thread(target=self.executor.spin)
        self.executor_thread.daemon = True
        self.executor_thread.start()
        
        self.node.on_configure(self.node.get_current_state())
        self.node.on_activate(self.node.get_current_state())

        self.executor.shutdown()
        if self.executor_thread.is_alive():
            self.executor_thread.join(timeout=2.0)
    
    def test_geofence_violation_handling(self):
        """Test la gestion des violations de geofence."""
        # Tenter une navigation vers une zone interdite
        from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan
        
        client = self.test_node.create_client(GetPlan, '/drone_nav/goto_position')
        self.assertTrue(client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0))
        
        # Position dans une zone d'exclusion (selon config par défaut)
        request = GetPlan.Request()
        request.goal.pose.position.x = 40.0  # Zone building_exclusion
        request.goal.pose.position.y = 40.0
        request.goal.pose.position.z = 15.0
        
        future = client.call_async(request)
        
        start_time = time.time()
        while not future.done() and (time.time() - start_time) < 10.0:
            time.sleep(0.1)
        
        self.assertTrue(future.done())
        response = future.result()
        
        # Le système devrait soit rejeter la demande, soit planifier un contournement
        self.assertIsNotNone(response)
        
        # Si un chemin est retourné, il ne devrait pas passer par la zone interdite
        if len(response.plan.poses) > 0:
            for pose in response.plan.poses:
                x, y = pose.pose.position.x, pose.pose.position.y
                # Vérifier qu'on n'est pas dans la zone interdite [30-50, 30-50]
                if 30.0 <= x <= 50.0 and 30.0 <= y <= 50.0:
                    self.fail(f"Path goes through forbidden zone at ({x}, {y})")


if __name__ == '__main__':

    ])

# Package drone_navigation

## Vue d'ensemble

Le package `drone_navigation` est un système complet de navigation autonome pour drones, conçu pour ROS2 Humble. Il offre des capacités avancées de planification de trajectoire, contrôle de position, évitement d'obstacles, gestion de geofencing et génération de motifs de couverture pour des missions de pollinisation automatisée.

## Architecture

### Composants principaux

1. **Navigation Node** (`navigation_node.py`)
   - Nœud principal avec gestion du cycle de vie (LifecycleNode)
   - Orchestration de tous les composants
   - Interface avec MAVROS
   - Threading pour performance temps réel

2. **Trajectory Planner** (`trajectory_planner.py`)
   - Algorithmes A*, RRT*, Dijkstra
   - Courbes de Bézier et chemins de Dubins
   - Optimisation multi-critères

3. **Position Controller** (`position_controller.py`)
   - Contrôleurs PID cascade
   - Modes de contrôle multiples
   - Anti-windup et filtrage

4. **Coverage Patterns** (`coverage_patterns.py`)
   - Motifs zigzag, spirale, tondeuse
   - Optimisation de couverture
   - Adaptation au terrain

5. **Path Optimizer** (`path_optimizer.py`)
   - Algorithmes génétiques
   - Optimisation SciPy
   - Coûts multi-objectifs

6. **Obstacle Avoidance** (`obstacle_avoidance.py`)
   - Champs de potentiel
   - Dynamic Window Approach
   - Base de données d'obstacles

7. **Geofence Manager** (`geofence_manager.py`)
   - Zones polygonales
   - Actions automatiques
   - Gestion des violations

## Installation

### Prérequis

```bash
# Dépendances système
sudo apt install python3-pip python3-dev

# Dépendances Python
pip3 install numpy scipy shapely matplotlib

# Packages ROS2 requis
sudo apt install ros-humble-mavros ros-humble-mavros-extras
sudo apt install ros-humble-lifecycle-msgs ros-humble-diagnostic-updater
```

### Compilation

```bash
# Dans le workspace ROS2
cd ~/ros2_ws
colcon build --packages-select drone_navigation
source install/setup.bash
```

## Configuration

### Fichiers de configuration

Le package utilise plusieurs fichiers YAML pour la configuration :

- `config/navigation_params.yaml` - Configuration principale
- `config/pid_tuning.yaml` - Paramètres des contrôleurs PID
- `config/geofence_zones.yaml` - Définition des zones de sécurité
- `config/coverage_patterns.yaml` - Paramètres des motifs de couverture

### Paramètres principaux

```yaml
navigation_node:
  ros__parameters:
    update_frequency: 20.0  # Hz
    control_frequency: 50.0  # Hz
    world_frame: "map"
    robot_frame: "base_link"
```

## Utilisation

### Lancement du système

```bash
# Lancement basique
ros2 launch drone_navigation navigation.launch.py

# Avec configuration personnalisée
ros2 launch drone_navigation navigation.launch.py config_file:=custom_config.yaml

# Mode debug
ros2 launch drone_navigation navigation.launch.py debug:=true enable_diagnostics:=true
```

### Services disponibles

#### Navigation vers position
```bash
ros2 service call /drone_nav/goto_position geometry_msgs/srv/GetPlan \
  "start: {header: {frame_id: 'map'}, pose: {position: {x: 0, y: 0, z: 15}}}
   goal: {header: {frame_id: 'map'}, pose: {position: {x: 10, y: 10, z: 15}}}"
```

#### Planification de mission
```bash
ros2 service call /drone_nav/plan_mission drone_msgs/srv/PlanMission \
  "mission_type: 'waypoint'
   parameters: '{\"waypoints\": [[10,10,15], [20,20,15]]}'"
```

#### Génération de motif de couverture
```bash
ros2 service call /drone_nav/get_coverage_pattern drone_msgs/srv/GetCoveragePattern \
  "area: {points: [{x: 0, y: 0}, {x: 20, y: 0}, {x: 20, y: 20}, {x: 0, y: 20}]}
   pattern_type: 'zigzag'
   line_spacing: 5.0
   altitude: 15.0"
```

### Outils CLI

Le package fournit plusieurs outils en ligne de commande :

#### Navigation simple
```bash
# Navigation vers position
goto_position --x 10.0 --y 20.0 --z 15.0

# Mode interactif
goto_position --interactive

# Coordonnées GPS
goto_position --lat 45.123456 --lon 2.654321 --alt 15.0
```

#### Planification de mission
```bash
# Mission waypoints
plan_mission --waypoints "10,20,15" "30,40,25" "50,10,20"

# Mission de couverture
plan_mission --coverage --polygon "0,0 10,0 10,10 0,10" --pattern zigzag

# Depuis fichier
plan_mission --file mission.yaml
```

#### Monitoring
```bash
# Statut en temps réel
nav_status

# Monitoring continu
nav_status --watch

# Format JSON
nav_status --json
```

#### Diagnostics
```bash
# Diagnostic standard
nav_diagnostics

# Rapport détaillé
nav_diagnostics --detailed --duration 30

# Export JSON
nav_diagnostics --export report.json
```

#### Tests
```bash
# Test preset
test_waypoints --preset square

# Test personnalisé
test_waypoints --custom "10,10,15" "20,10,15" "20,20,15"

# Test de stress
test_waypoints --preset stress_test --detailed
```

## API des composants

### NavigationNode

```python
class NavigationNode(LifecycleNode):
    def goto_position(self, target_position: Point) -> bool
    def plan_mission(self, waypoints: List[Point]) -> str
    def execute_mission(self, mission_id: str) -> bool
    def get_status(self) -> Dict
    def emergency_stop(self, reason: str) -> None
```

### TrajectoryPlanner

```python
class TrajectoryPlanner:
    def plan_a_star(self, start: Point, goal: Point) -> List[Point]
    def plan_rrt_star(self, start: Point, goal: Point) -> List[Point]
    def smooth_path_bezier(self, waypoints: List[Point]) -> List[Point]
    def validate_path(self, path: List[Point]) -> bool
```

### PositionController

```python
class PositionController:
    def compute_position_control(self, current: Point, target: Point, dt: float) -> Twist
    def set_control_mode(self, mode: str) -> bool
    def check_convergence(self, current: Point, target: Point, velocity: Point) -> bool
```

## Intégration MAVROS

### Topics utilisés

**Subscription:**
- `/mavros/local_position/pose` - Position locale
- `/mavros/local_position/velocity_local` - Vélocité locale
- `/mavros/state` - État du système
- `/mavros/global_position/global` - Position GPS

**Publication:**
- `/mavros/setpoint_position/local` - Consignes de position
- `/mavros/setpoint_velocity/cmd_vel` - Consignes de vélocité

**Services:**
- `/mavros/cmd/arming` - Armement
- `/mavros/set_mode` - Changement de mode
- `/mavros/cmd/takeoff` - Décollage
- `/mavros/cmd/land` - Atterrissage

## Algorithmes implémentés

### Planification de trajectoire

1. **A*** - Recherche de chemin optimal avec heuristique
2. **RRT*** - Rapidly-exploring Random Tree avec optimisation
3. **Dijkstra** - Algorithme de plus court chemin
4. **Courbes de Bézier** - Lissage de trajectoire
5. **Chemins de Dubins** - Trajectoires avec contraintes de courbure

### Contrôle

1. **PID Cascade** - Contrôle position → vélocité → attitude
2. **Anti-windup** - Prévention de la saturation intégrale
3. **Filtrage dérivé** - Réduction du bruit sur la dérivée
4. **Compensation de perturbations** - Rejet actif des perturbations

### Évitement d'obstacles

1. **Champs de potentiel** - Forces répulsives/attractives
2. **Dynamic Window Approach** - Optimisation locale de trajectoire
3. **Évaluation de menaces** - Classification des obstacles

### Motifs de couverture

1. **Zigzag** - Lignes parallèles avec virages
2. **Spirale** - Couverture en spirale depuis le centre
3. **Tondeuse** - Motif de tondeuse à gazon
4. **Boustrophédon** - Décomposition cellulaire
5. **Adaptatif** - Adaptation à la densité locale

## Sécurité

### Geofencing

- **Zones d'inclusion** - Zones autorisées
- **Zones d'exclusion** - Zones interdites
- **Zones d'avertissement** - Zones de vigilance
- **Actions automatiques** - Réponses aux violations

### Limitations de sécurité

```yaml
safety_limits:
  max_velocity: [15.0, 15.0, 10.0]  # m/s [x, y, z]
  max_acceleration: [5.0, 5.0, 3.0]  # m/s² [x, y, z]
  max_altitude: 50.0  # mètres
  max_distance_from_home: 100.0  # mètres
```

### Actions d'urgence

- **Return to Launch (RTL)** - Retour automatique
- **Emergency Land** - Atterrissage d'urgence
- **Hover in Place** - Maintien en position
- **Disarm** - Désarmement automatique


## Performance

### Optimisations

- **Threading** - Traitement parallèle des composants
- **Caching** - Mise en cache des calculs coûteux
- **Lazy evaluation** - Calcul à la demande
- **Memory pooling** - Réutilisation de la mémoire

### Métriques

- **Fréquence de boucle** - 20-50 Hz selon configuration
- **Latence de contrôle** - < 50ms
- **Utilisation CPU** - < 80% recommandé
- **Utilisation mémoire** - < 512MB typique

## Dépannage

### Problèmes courants

1. **Connection MAVROS échouée**
   ```bash
   # Vérifier MAVROS
   ros2 topic echo /mavros/state
   
   # Relancer MAVROS
   ros2 launch mavros apm.launch fcu_url:=udp://127.0.0.1:14550@14555
   ```

2. **Planification échouée**
   ```bash
   # Vérifier les paramètres
   ros2 param get /navigation_node trajectory_planner
   
   # Logs détaillés
   ros2 launch drone_navigation navigation.launch.py log_level:=debug
   ```

3. **Performance dégradée**
   ```bash
   # Diagnostics
   nav_diagnostics --detailed
   
   # Monitoring
   nav_status --watch
   ```

### Logs importants

```bash
# Erreurs de planification
grep "Planning failed" ~/.ros/log/latest/navigation_node*

# Violations de geofence
grep "Geofence violation" ~/.ros/log/latest/navigation_node*

# Erreurs de contrôle
grep "Control error" ~/.ros/log/latest/navigation_node*
```

## Exemples d'utilisation

### Mission de pollinisation complète

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import rclpy
from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan
from drone_msgs.srv import GetCoveragePattern, PlanMission

def main():
    rclpy.init()
    node = rclpy.create_node('pollination_mission')
    
    # 1. Générer motif de couverture
    coverage_client = node.create_client(GetCoveragePattern, '/drone_nav/get_coverage_pattern')
    # ... configure polygon and pattern
    
    # 2. Planifier mission
    plan_client = node.create_client(PlanMission, '/drone_nav/plan_mission')
    # ... configure mission parameters
    
    # 3. Exécuter mission
    # ... execute and monitor
    
    rclpy.shutdown()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### Navigation avec évitement d'obstacles

```bash
# 1. Activer évitement d'obstacles
ros2 param set /navigation_node obstacle_avoidance.enable true

# 2. Navigation vers position
goto_position --x 20.0 --y 20.0 --z 15.0

# 3. Monitoring en temps réel
nav_status --watch
```

## Contribuer

### Structure du code

```
drone_navigation/
├── drone_navigation/          # Code Python principal
│   ├── __init__.py
│   ├── navigation_node.py     # Nœud principal
│   ├── trajectory_planner.py  # Planification
│   ├── position_controller.py # Contrôle
│   ├── coverage_patterns.py   # Motifs de couverture
│   ├── path_optimizer.py      # Optimisation
│   ├── obstacle_avoidance.py  # Évitement d'obstacles
│   ├── geofence_manager.py    # Geofencing
│   └── tools/                 # Outils CLI
├── config/                    # Fichiers de configuration
├── launch/                    # Fichiers de lancement
├── test/                      # Tests
├── docs/                      # Documentation
└── package.xml               # Métadonnées du package
```

### Guidelines de développement

1. **Suivre PEP 8** pour le style Python
2. **Documenter** toutes les fonctions publiques
3. **Tester** le code avec pytest
4. **Utiliser** le logging ROS2 standard
5. **Respecter** les conventions ROS2

### Soumettre des modifications

1. Fork du repository
2. Création d'une branche feature
3. Implémentation et tests
4. Pull request avec description détaillée

# Configuration des motifs de couverture
# Package: drone_navigation
# Description: Paramètres pour génération de motifs de couverture automatisés

coverage_patterns:
  ros__parameters:
    # === PARAMÈTRES GÉNÉRAUX ===
    general:
      default_altitude: 15.0      # mètres
      default_speed: 5.0          # m/s
      default_pattern: "zigzag"
      coordinate_frame: "map"
      
      # Sécurité
      safety_margins:
        polygon_buffer: 2.0       # mètres - marge intérieure du polygone
        obstacle_buffer: 3.0      # mètres - marge autour des obstacles
        boundary_buffer: 1.0      # mètres - marge aux limites
      
      # Qualité de couverture
      coverage_quality:
        min_overlap: 0.1          # 10% minimum
        max_overlap: 0.3          # 30% maximum
        uniformity_threshold: 0.9  # 90% d'uniformité
    
    # === MOTIF ZIGZAG ===
    zigzag:
      # Paramètres de base
      line_spacing: 5.0           # mètres entre lignes
      overlap_ratio: 0.15         # 15% de recouvrement
      
      # Direction
      direction_angle: 0.0        # radians (0 = horizontal)
      auto_optimize_direction: true
      
      # Virages
      turn_style: "smooth"        # smooth, sharp, arc
      turn_radius: 3.0           # mètres pour virages arc
      turn_speed_reduction: 0.7   # facteur de réduction vitesse en virage
      
      # Optimisations
      minimize_turns: true
      start_from_corner: true
      prefer_long_lines: true
      
      # Paramètres avancés
      line_generation:
        method: "parallel_lines"   # parallel_lines, offset_polygon
        precision: 0.1            # mètres
        smooth_endpoints: true
        extend_lines: false
      
      # Gestion des obstacles
      obstacle_handling:
        method: "split_lines"     # split_lines, detour, skip
        min_segment_length: 2.0   # mètres
        reconnect_segments: true
    
    # === MOTIF SPIRALE ===
    spiral:
      # Type de spirale
      spiral_type: "archimedean"  # archimedean, logarithmic, fermat
      
      # Paramètres géométriques
      center_method: "centroid"   # centroid, user_defined, optimal
      radius_increment: 3.0       # mètres par tour
      angular_resolution: 0.1     # radians entre points
      
      # Limites
      max_radius: 50.0           # mètres
      min_radius: 2.0            # mètres
      max_turns: 20              # nombre maximum de tours
      
      # Direction
      clockwise: true
      start_angle: 0.0           # radians
      
      # Vitesse variable
      variable_speed: true
      speed_profile: "constant_linear"  # constant_linear, constant_angular, adaptive
      
      # Optimisations
      adaptive_spacing: true
      density_adjustment: true
      boundary_conformance: 0.9   # facteur de conformité aux limites
    
    # === MOTIF TONDEUSE (LAWN MOWER) ===
    lawn_mower:
      # Paramètres de base
      line_spacing: 4.0          # mètres
      overlap_ratio: 0.12        # 12% de recouvrement
      
      # Direction des lignes
      direction: "auto"          # auto, horizontal, vertical, diagonal, optimal
      direction_angle: 0.0       # radians (si direction = diagonal)
      
      # Types de virages
      turn_type: "u_turn"        # u_turn, square_turn, spiral_turn
      turn_radius: 2.5           # mètres
      
      # Stratégie de couverture
      coverage_strategy: "back_and_forth"  # back_and_forth, all_same_direction
      start_edge: "shortest"     # shortest, longest, left, right, top, bottom
      
      # Optimisations
      optimize_for: "time"       # time, fuel, coverage_quality, path_length
      minimize_repositioning: true
      
      # Gestion des bords
      edge_handling:
        follow_boundary: true
        boundary_passes: 1        # nombre de passes le long du bord
        inward_offset: 1.0       # mètres depuis le bord
    
    # === MOTIF BOUSTROPHÉDON ===
    boustrophedon:
      # Décomposition cellulaire
      cell_decomposition:
        method: "trapezoidal"     # trapezoidal, triangular, morse
        cell_size_preference: "uniform"  # uniform, adaptive, obstacle_aware
        
      # Paramètres de ligne
      line_spacing: 5.0
      overlap_ratio: 0.1
      
      # Connexion entre cellules
      inter_cell_connection: "shortest_path"  # shortest_path, smooth_curve, straight_line
      connection_optimization: true
      
      # Gestion des obstacles
      obstacle_decomposition: true
      create_buffer_cells: true
      buffer_cell_size: 2.0      # mètres
      
      # Direction adaptative
      adaptive_direction: true
      direction_per_cell: true
      
      # Optimisations
      cell_order_optimization: "nearest_neighbor"  # nearest_neighbor, genetic, optimal
      minimize_total_path: true
    
    # === MOTIF ADAPTATIF ===
    adaptive:
      # Méthode d'adaptation
      adaptation_method: "density_based"  # density_based, terrain_following, sensor_guided
      
      # Paramètres de densité
      density_sensing:
        sensor_range: 10.0        # mètres
        density_threshold: 0.5    # seuil de densité (0-1)
        sampling_resolution: 1.0  # mètres
      
      # Espacement variable
      spacing_range:
        min_spacing: 2.0         # mètres
        max_spacing: 10.0        # mètres
        adaptation_rate: 0.3     # taux d'adaptation
      
      # Réévaluation en temps réel
      realtime_adaptation: true
      reevaluation_interval: 30.0  # secondes
      
      # Machine learning
      learning_enabled: false
      model_update_frequency: 100  # missions
      
      # Paramètres prédictifs
      predictive_adaptation: true
      prediction_horizon: 5       # points ahead
      confidence_threshold: 0.8
    
    # === MOTIF CONTOUR ===
    contour:
      # Suivre les contours
      follow_boundary: true
      inward_offset: 2.0         # mètres depuis le bord
      
      # Nombre de passes
      num_passes: 3
      pass_spacing: 3.0          # mètres entre passes
      
      # Direction
      clockwise: true
      start_from: "closest_point"  # closest_point, furthest_point, user_defined
      
      # Lissage du contour
      contour_smoothing: true
      smoothing_factor: 0.5
      
      # Gestion des concavités
      handle_concavities: true
      min_concavity_size: 2.0    # mètres
    
    # === MOTIF GRILLE ===
    grid:
      # Type de grille
      grid_type: "rectangular"   # rectangular, hexagonal, triangular
      
      # Paramètres de la grille
      grid_spacing: [5.0, 5.0]  # [x, y] mètres
      grid_angle: 0.0           # radians
      
      # Points de visite
      visit_all_points: true
      visit_pattern: "row_by_row"  # row_by_row, column_by_column, nearest_neighbor
      
      # Optimisation du chemin
      optimize_path: true
      path_algorithm: "tsp"      # tsp, nearest_neighbor, christofides
    
    # === MOTIF SECTEUR ===
    sector:
      # Division en secteurs
      num_sectors: 4
      sector_method: "radial"    # radial, grid_based, adaptive
      
      # Couverture par secteur
      sector_pattern: "spiral"   # spiral, zigzag, contour
      inter_sector_transition: "smooth"  # smooth, direct, optimized
      
      # Ordonnancement des secteurs
      sector_order: "clockwise"  # clockwise, counterclockwise, optimized
      
      # Recouvrement entre secteurs
      sector_overlap: 1.0        # mètres
    
    # === OPTIMISATIONS GLOBALES ===
    optimization:
      # Algorithmes d'optimisation
      path_optimization: true
      optimization_algorithm: "genetic"  # genetic, simulated_annealing, ant_colony
      
      # Paramètres génétiques
      genetic_algorithm:
        population_size: 50
        generations: 100
        mutation_rate: 0.1
        crossover_rate: 0.8
      
      # Critères d'optimisation
      optimization_criteria:
        path_length: 0.4         # poids
        coverage_quality: 0.3    # poids
        energy_efficiency: 0.2   # poids
        time_efficiency: 0.1     # poids
      
      # Contraintes
      constraints:
        max_path_length: 1000.0  # mètres
        max_mission_time: 3600   # secondes
        min_coverage_ratio: 0.95 # 95%
        max_energy_consumption: 80.0  # pourcentage
    
    # === VALIDATION ET QUALITÉ ===
    validation:
      # Validation automatique
      auto_validate: true
      
      # Critères de validation
      validation_criteria:
        coverage_completeness: 0.95  # 95% minimum
        path_feasibility: true
        safety_compliance: true
        resource_constraints: true
      
      # Métriques de qualité
      quality_metrics:
        calculate_coverage_ratio: true
        calculate_overlap_ratio: true
        calculate_path_efficiency: true
        calculate_time_estimate: true
      
      # Simulation
      simulation_enabled: true
      simulation_resolution: 0.5   # mètres
      simulation_timestep: 0.1     # secondes
    
    # === GESTION DES OBSTACLES ===
    obstacle_management:
      # Détection d'obstacles
      obstacle_detection: true
      detection_method: "polygon_intersection"  # polygon_intersection, distance_based
      
      # Types d'obstacles
      obstacle_types:
        static: true             # bâtiments, arbres
        dynamic: false           # véhicules, personnes
        temporary: true          # équipements temporaires
      
      # Stratégies d'évitement
      avoidance_strategies:
        small_obstacles: "go_around"     # go_around, go_over, split_path
        large_obstacles: "split_path"    # split_path, go_around, exclude_area
        linear_obstacles: "perpendicular_crossing"  # perpendicular_crossing, parallel_follow
      
      # Paramètres d'évitement
      avoidance_parameters:
        min_clearance: 3.0       # mètres
        detour_preference: "shortest"  # shortest, safest, smoothest
        max_detour_ratio: 1.5    # rapport détour/chemin direct
    
    # === PROFILS DE MISSION ===
    mission_profiles:
      # Profil pollinisation
      pollination:
        optimal_altitude: 8.0
        line_spacing: 3.0
        overlap_ratio: 0.2
        pattern_preference: ["zigzag", "lawn_mower"]
        speed: 3.0
        
      # Profil surveillance
      surveillance:
        optimal_altitude: 25.0
        line_spacing: 8.0
        overlap_ratio: 0.1
        pattern_preference: ["spiral", "contour"]

# Configuration principale du système de navigation
# Package: drone_navigation
# Description: Paramètres pour tous les composants de navigation

navigation_node:
  ros__parameters:
    # === PARAMÈTRES GÉNÉRAUX ===
    update_frequency: 20.0  # Hz - Fréquence de la boucle principale
    control_frequency: 50.0  # Hz - Fréquence du contrôleur
    diagnostic_frequency: 1.0  # Hz - Fréquence des diagnostics
    
    # Frame de référence
    world_frame: "map"
    robot_frame: "base_link"
    
    # === PLANIFICATION DE TRAJECTOIRE ===
    trajectory_planner:
      # Algorithme par défaut
      default_algorithm: "a_star"  # a_star, rrt_star, dijkstra, bezier, dubins
      
      # Paramètres A*
      a_star:
        heuristic_weight: 1.2
        max_iterations: 10000
        grid_resolution: 0.5  # mètres
        allow_diagonal: true
        tie_breaker: 0.001
      
      # Paramètres RRT*
      rrt_star:
        max_iterations: 5000
        step_size: 2.0  # mètres
        goal_tolerance: 1.0  # mètres
        rewire_radius: 5.0  # mètres
        goal_bias: 0.1  # probabilité de se diriger vers l'objectif
      
      # Paramètres Dijkstra
      dijkstra:
        grid_resolution: 0.5  # mètres
        max_cost: 1000.0
      
      # Paramètres courbes de Bézier
      bezier:
        num_control_points: 4
        resolution: 0.1  # mètres entre points
        max_curvature: 0.5  # rad/m
      
      # Paramètres chemins de Dubins
      dubins:
        turning_radius: 3.0  # mètres
        resolution: 0.1  # mètres
    
    # === CONTRÔLE DE POSITION ===
    position_controller:
      # Gains PID pour position
      position_pid:
        kp: [2.0, 2.0, 3.0]  # [x, y, z]
        ki: [0.1, 0.1, 0.2]  # [x, y, z]
        kd: [0.5, 0.5, 0.8]  # [x, y, z]
        
        # Limites anti-windup
        integral_limit: [5.0, 5.0, 5.0]  # [x, y, z]
        
        # Limites de sortie
        output_limit: [10.0, 10.0, 5.0]  # [x, y, z] m/s
      
      # Gains PID pour attitude
      attitude_pid:
        kp: [4.0, 4.0, 2.0]  # [roll, pitch, yaw]
        ki: [0.2, 0.2, 0.1]  # [roll, pitch, yaw]
        kd: [1.0, 1.0, 0.5]  # [roll, pitch, yaw]
        
        integral_limit: [0.5, 0.5, 0.5]  # [roll, pitch, yaw]
        output_limit: [1.0, 1.0, 0.5]  # [roll, pitch, yaw] rad/s
      
      # Tolérance de convergence
      convergence_tolerance:
        position: 1.0  # mètres
        velocity: 0.5  # m/s
        attitude: 0.1  # radians
      
      # Modes de contrôle
      control_modes:
        default_mode: "position_hold"
        available_modes:
          - "position_hold"
          - "guided"
          - "trajectory_following"
          - "precision_approach"
      
      # Limites de sécurité
      safety_limits:
        max_velocity: [15.0, 15.0, 10.0]  # [x, y, z] m/s
        max_acceleration: [5.0, 5.0, 3.0]  # [x, y, z] m/s²
        max_tilt_angle: 0.5  # radians (≈30°)
    
    # === MOTIFS DE COUVERTURE ===
    coverage_patterns:
      # Paramètres généraux
      default_pattern: "zigzag"
      default_altitude: 15.0  # mètres
      default_speed: 5.0  # m/s
      
      # Motif zigzag
      zigzag:
        line_spacing: 5.0  # mètres
        turn_radius: 2.0  # mètres
        overlap_ratio: 0.1  # 10% de recouvrement
      
      # Motif spirale
      spiral:
        radius_increment: 3.0  # mètres par tour
        angular_resolution: 0.1  # radians
        start_radius: 2.0  # mètres
      
      # Motif tondeuse
      lawn_mower:
        line_spacing: 4.0  # mètres
        turn_type: "square"  # square, round
        direction: "horizontal"  # horizontal, vertical
      
      # Motif boustrophédon
      boustrophedon:
        line_spacing: 5.0  # mètres
        cell_decomposition: true
        obstacle_avoidance: true
      
      # Motif adaptatif
      adaptive:
        initial_spacing: 5.0  # mètres
        min_spacing: 2.0  # mètres
        max_spacing: 10.0  # mètres
        density_threshold: 0.5
    
    # === OPTIMISATION DE CHEMIN ===
    path_optimizer:
      # Algorithme génétique
      genetic_algorithm:
        population_size: 50
        generations: 100
        mutation_rate: 0.1
        crossover_rate: 0.8
        elite_ratio: 0.1
      
      # Optimisation SciPy
      scipy_optimizer:
        method: "SLSQP"  # SLSQP, L-BFGS-B, COBYLA
        max_iterations: 1000
        tolerance: 1e-6
      
      # Poids des coûts
      cost_weights:
        distance: 1.0
        smoothness: 0.5
        safety: 2.0
        energy: 0.3
      
      # Contraintes
      constraints:
        max_curvature: 0.5  # rad/m
        max_acceleration: 3.0  # m/s²
        min_segment_length: 1.0  # mètres
    
    # === ÉVITEMENT D'OBSTACLES ===
    obstacle_avoidance:
      # Champs de potentiel
      potential_fields:
        attractive_gain: 1.0
        repulsive_gain: 10.0
        influence_distance: 5.0  # mètres
        max_force: 5.0  # N
      
      # Approche fenêtre dynamique
      dynamic_window:
        velocity_resolution: 0.1  # m/s
        angular_resolution: 0.1  # rad/s
        prediction_time: 2.0  # secondes
        heading_weight: 0.6
        velocity_weight: 0.2
        distance_weight: 0.2
      
      # Base de données d'obstacles
      obstacle_database:
        max_obstacles: 100
        expiry_time: 30.0  # secondes
        min_size: 0.5  # mètres
        clustering_distance: 2.0  # mètres
      
      # Évaluation des menaces
      threat_assessment:
        collision_time_threshold: 5.0  # secondes
        distance_threshold: 3.0  # mètres
        velocity_threshold: 2.0  # m/s
    
    # === GESTION DES GEOFENCES ===
    geofence_manager:
      # Paramètres généraux
      check_frequency: 5.0  # Hz
      buffer_distance: 2.0  # mètres - marge de sécurité
      
      # Actions automatiques
      auto_actions:
        warning_distance: 5.0  # mètres - distance d'avertissement
        stop_distance: 2.0  # mètres - distance d'arrêt
        return_to_safe_zone: true
        emergency_land: false
      
      # Types de zones supportées
      zone_types:
        - "inclusion"  # Zone autorisée
        - "exclusion"  # Zone interdite
        - "warning"    # Zone d'avertissement
        - "emergency"  # Zone d'urgence
    
    # === PERFORMANCES ET MONITORING ===
    performance:
      # Monitoring système
      enable_performance_monitoring: true
      log_performance_data: true
      performance_log_file: "/tmp/drone_nav_performance.log"
      
      # Seuils d'alerte

        cpu_usage: 80.0  # pourcentage

    # === INTÉGRATION MAVROS ===
