#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Outil CLI pour tester la navigation avec une série de waypoints de test.

Usage:
    test_waypoints --preset square
    test_waypoints --custom "10,10,15" "20,10,15" "20,20,15" "10,20,15"
    test_waypoints --stress-test
"""

import argparse
import sys
import json
import time
import math
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Any, Optional

import rclpy
from rclpy.node import Node
from geometry_msgs.msg import Point, PoseStamped
from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan
from std_msgs.msg import String
from nav_msgs.msg import Path


class TestWaypointsCLI(Node):
    """Client CLI pour tests de navigation par waypoints."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__('test_waypoints_cli')
        
        # Services clients
        self._goto_service = self.create_client(
            GetPlan,
            '/drone_nav/goto_position'
        )
        
        # Subscriber pour le statut
        self._status_sub = self.create_subscription(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/status',
            self._status_callback,
            10
        )
        
        # Données de test
        self._current_status = None
        self._test_results = []
        self._test_start_time = None
        
        # Métriques de test
        self._position_errors = []
        self._timing_data = []
        
    def _status_callback(self, msg: String):
        """Callback pour mise à jour du statut."""
        try:
            self._current_status = json.loads(msg.data)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            self._current_status = {'raw_message': msg.data}
    
    def get_preset_waypoints(self, preset_name: str) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
        """Retourne des waypoints prédéfinis selon le preset."""
        presets = {
            'square': [
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (10.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (10.0, 10.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 10.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0)
            ],
            'triangle': [
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (15.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (7.5, 13.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0)
            ],
            'circle': [],  # Sera généré dynamiquement
            'figure8': [
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (5.0, 5.0, 15.0),
                (10.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (15.0, -5.0, 15.0),
                (10.0, -10.0, 15.0),
                (5.0, -5.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (-5.0, 5.0, 15.0),
                (-10.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (-5.0, -5.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0)
            ],
            'altitude_test': [
                (0.0, 0.0, 10.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 20.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 25.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 20.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 10.0)
            ],
            'precision_test': [
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (1.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (1.0, 1.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 1.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0)
            ],
            'stress_test': [],  # Sera généré avec beaucoup de points
            'long_distance': [
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (50.0, 0.0, 15.0),
                (50.0, 50.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 50.0, 15.0),
                (0.0, 0.0, 15.0)
            ]
        }
        
        if preset_name == 'circle':
            # Génération d'un cercle
            radius = 10.0
            altitude = 15.0
            num_points = 12
            waypoints = []
            for i in range(num_points + 1):
                angle = 2 * math.pi * i / num_points
                x = radius * math.cos(angle)
                y = radius * math.sin(angle)
                waypoints.append((x, y, altitude))
            return waypoints
        
        elif preset_name == 'stress_test':
            # Test de stress avec beaucoup de waypoints
            waypoints = []
            for i in range(20):
                for j in range(20):
                    x = i * 2.0
                    y = j * 2.0
                    z = 15.0 + (i + j) % 3  # Variation d'altitude
                    waypoints.append((x, y, z))
            return waypoints
        
        return presets.get(preset_name, [])
    
    def goto_waypoint(self, x: float, y: float, z: float, timeout: float = 60.0) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Navigue vers un waypoint et mesure les performances.
        
        Returns:
            Dictionnaire avec les résultats du test
        """
        start_time = time.time()
        
        # Vérification du service
        if not self._goto_service.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0):
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Service de navigation non disponible',
                'waypoint': (x, y, z)
            }
        
        # Création de la requête
        request = GetPlan.Request()
        request.start.header.frame_id = "map"
        request.goal.header.frame_id = "map"
        request.goal.pose.position.x = x
        request.goal.pose.position.y = y
        request.goal.pose.position.z = z
        
        # Envoi de la requête
        try:
            future = self._goto_service.call_async(request)
            rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(self, future, timeout_sec=10.0)
            
            if future.result() is None:
                return {
                    'success': False,
                    'error': 'Pas de réponse du service',
                    'waypoint': (x, y, z)
                }
            
            response = future.result()
            if len(response.plan.poses) == 0:
                return {
                    'success': False,
                    'error': 'Échec de planification',
                    'waypoint': (x, y, z)
                }
            
            # Attente de l'achèvement
            success, metrics = self._wait_for_waypoint_completion(x, y, z, timeout)
            
            end_time = time.time()
            total_time = end_time - start_time
            
            result = {
                'success': success,
                'waypoint': (x, y, z),
                'total_time': total_time,
                'planning_time': 10.0,  # Approximation
                'execution_time': total_time - 10.0,
                'metrics': metrics
            }
            
            if not success:
                result['error'] = 'Timeout ou échec de navigation'
            
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': f'Exception: {str(e)}',
                'waypoint': (x, y, z)
            }
    
    def _wait_for_waypoint_completion(self, target_x: float, target_y: float, 
                                    target_z: float, timeout: float) -> Tuple[bool, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Attend que le drone atteigne le waypoint cible."""
        start_time = time.time()
        convergence_threshold = 2.0  # Seuil de convergence en mètres
        
        metrics = {
            'max_error': 0.0,
            'convergence_time': None,
            'trajectory_length': 0.0,
            'average_speed': 0.0,
            'position_samples': []
        }
        
        last_position = None
        converged = False
        
        rate = self.create_rate(5.0)  # 5 Hz
        
        while rclpy.ok() and (time.time() - start_time) < timeout:
            rclpy.spin_once(self, timeout_sec=0.1)
            
            # Vérification du statut
            if self._current_status:
                try:
                    # Extraction position actuelle
                    if 'current_position' in self._current_status:
                        pos = self._current_status['current_position']
                        current_x = pos.get('x', 0)
                        current_y = pos.get('y', 0)
                        current_z = pos.get('z', 0)
                        
                        # Calcul de l'erreur
                        error = math.sqrt(
                            (current_x - target_x)**2 + 
                            (current_y - target_y)**2 + 
                            (current_z - target_z)**2
                        )
                        
                        metrics['max_error'] = max(metrics['max_error'], error)
                        metrics['position_samples'].append({
                            'time': time.time() - start_time,
                            'position': (current_x, current_y, current_z),
                            'error': error
                        })
                        
                        # Calcul de la trajectoire
                        if last_position:
                            dx = current_x - last_position[0]
                            dy = current_y - last_position[1]
                            dz = current_z - last_position[2]
                            segment_length = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz)
                            metrics['trajectory_length'] += segment_length
                        
                        last_position = (current_x, current_y, current_z)
                        
                        # Vérification de convergence
                        if error < convergence_threshold and not converged:
                            converged = True
                            metrics['convergence_time'] = time.time() - start_time
                    
                    # Vérification si la mission est terminée
                    if 'state' in self._current_status and self._current_status['state'] == 'idle':
                        if converged:
                            break
                    
                except (KeyError, TypeError):
                    pass  # Ignore les erreurs de parsing
            
            rate.sleep()
        
        # Calcul de la vitesse moyenne
        if metrics['convergence_time'] and metrics['trajectory_length'] > 0:
            metrics['average_speed'] = metrics['trajectory_length'] / metrics['convergence_time']
        
        return converged and (time.time() - start_time) < timeout, metrics
    
    def run_waypoint_test(self, waypoints: List[Tuple[float, float, float]], 
                         test_name: str = "Custom Test") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Execute un test complet avec une série de waypoints."""
        self.get_logger().info(f"Début du test: {test_name}")
        self.get_logger().info(f"Nombre de waypoints: {len(waypoints)}")
        
        test_start_time = time.time()
        results = []
        
        for i, (x, y, z) in enumerate(waypoints):
            self.get_logger().info(f"Waypoint {i+1}/{len(waypoints)}: ({x:.1f}, {y:.1f}, {z:.1f})")
            
            waypoint_result = self.goto_waypoint(x, y, z)
            results.append(waypoint_result)
            
            if waypoint_result['success']:
                self.get_logger().info(f"✅ Waypoint atteint en {waypoint_result['total_time']:.1f}s")
            else:
                self.get_logger().error(f"❌ Échec: {waypoint_result.get('error', 'Erreur inconnue')}")
                
                # Option d'arrêt sur échec
                user_input = input("Continuer malgré l'échec? (o/N): ").lower()
                if user_input != 'o':
                    break
            
            # Pause entre waypoints
            time.sleep(2.0)
        
        test_end_time = time.time()
        total_test_time = test_end_time - test_start_time
        
        # Compilation des résultats
        successful_waypoints = [r for r in results if r['success']]
        failed_waypoints = [r for r in results if not r['success']]
        
        summary = {
            'test_name': test_name,
            'total_waypoints': len(waypoints),
            'successful_waypoints': len(successful_waypoints),
            'failed_waypoints': len(failed_waypoints),
            'success_rate': len(successful_waypoints) / len(waypoints) * 100 if waypoints else 0,
            'total_test_time': total_test_time,
            'results': results
        }
        
        # Statistiques des waypoints réussis
        if successful_waypoints:
            times = [r['total_time'] for r in successful_waypoints]
            errors = [r['metrics']['max_error'] for r in successful_waypoints if 'max_error' in r['metrics']]
            
            summary['statistics'] = {
                'average_time_per_waypoint': sum(times) / len(times),
                'max_time_per_waypoint': max(times),
                'min_time_per_waypoint': min(times),
                'average_max_error': sum(errors) / len(errors) if errors else 0,
                'max_error_encountered': max(errors) if errors else 0
            }
        
        return summary
    
    def format_test_report(self, test_summary: Dict[str, Any], detailed: bool = False) -> str:
        """Formate le rapport de test."""
        lines = []
        
        # En-tête
        lines.append("=" * 60)
        lines.append(f"RAPPORT DE TEST - {test_summary['test_name'].upper()}")
        lines.append("=" * 60)
        
        # Résumé
        success_rate = test_summary['success_rate']
        total_time = test_summary['total_test_time']
        
        lines.append(f"\n📊 RÉSUMÉ:")
        lines.append(f"   Waypoints total: {test_summary['total_waypoints']}")
        lines.append(f"   Réussis: {test_summary['successful_waypoints']}")
        lines.append(f"   Échoués: {test_summary['failed_waypoints']}")
        lines.append(f"   Taux de réussite: {success_rate:.1f}%")
        lines.append(f"   Temps total: {total_time:.1f}s")
        
        # Évaluation
        if success_rate >= 95:
            lines.append(f"   🏆 Évaluation: EXCELLENT")
        elif success_rate >= 80:
            lines.append(f"   ✅ Évaluation: BON")
        elif success_rate >= 60:
            lines.append(f"   ⚠️  Évaluation: MOYEN")
        else:
            lines.append(f"   ❌ Évaluation: INSUFFISANT")
        
        # Statistiques
        if 'statistics' in test_summary:
            stats = test_summary['statistics']
            lines.append(f"\n📈 STATISTIQUES:")
            lines.append(f"   Temps moyen par waypoint: {stats['average_time_per_waypoint']:.1f}s")
            lines.append(f"   Temps max par waypoint: {stats['max_time_per_waypoint']:.1f}s")
            lines.append(f"   Temps min par waypoint: {stats['min_time_per_waypoint']:.1f}s")
            lines.append(f"   Erreur moyenne max: {stats['average_max_error']:.2f}m")
            lines.append(f"   Erreur max rencontrée: {stats['max_error_encountered']:.2f}m")
        
        # Détails par waypoint
        if detailed:
            lines.append(f"\n📍 DÉTAIL PAR WAYPOINT:")
            for i, result in enumerate(test_summary['results']):
                wp = result['waypoint']
                status = "✅" if result['success'] else "❌"
                lines.append(f"   {i+1:2d}. ({wp[0]:6.1f}, {wp[1]:6.1f}, {wp[2]:6.1f}) {status}")
                
                if result['success']:
                    lines.append(f"       Temps: {result['total_time']:.1f}s")
                    if 'max_error' in result['metrics']:
                        lines.append(f"       Erreur max: {result['metrics']['max_error']:.2f}m")
                else:
                    lines.append(f"       Erreur: {result.get('error', 'Inconnue')}")
        
        # Échecs
        failed_results = [r for r in test_summary['results'] if not r['success']]
        if failed_results:
            lines.append(f"\n❌ WAYPOINTS ÉCHOUÉS:")
            for result in failed_results:
                wp = result['waypoint']
                error = result.get('error', 'Erreur inconnue')
                lines.append(f"   ({wp[0]:.1f}, {wp[1]:.1f}, {wp[2]:.1f}): {error}")
        
        lines.append("\n" + "=" * 60)
        return "\n".join(lines)


def main():
    """Point d'entrée principal."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Test de navigation par waypoints",
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        epilog="""
Presets disponibles:
    square        - Carré 10x10m
    triangle      - Triangle
    circle        - Cercle de 10m de rayon
    figure8       - Figure en 8
    altitude_test - Test d'altitude verticale
    precision_test- Test de précision (petits mouvements)
    stress_test   - Test de stress (400 waypoints)
    long_distance - Test longue distance (50x50m)

Exemples:
    test_waypoints --preset square
    test_waypoints --custom "10,10,15" "20,10,15" "20,20,15"
    test_waypoints --preset stress_test --detailed
        """
    )
    
    # Modes de test
    test_group = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    test_group.add_argument('--preset', '-p', 
                           choices=['square', 'triangle', 'circle', 'figure8', 
                                   'altitude_test', 'precision_test', 'stress_test', 'long_distance'],
                           help='Preset de waypoints prédéfini')
    test_group.add_argument('--custom', '-c', nargs='+',
                           help='Waypoints personnalisés "x,y,z"')
    
    # Options
    parser.add_argument('--detailed', '-d', action='store_true',
                       help='Rapport détaillé avec tous les waypoints')
    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=float, default=60.0,
                       help='Timeout par waypoint en secondes (défaut: 60)')
    parser.add_argument('--export', '-e', type=str,
                       help='Exporter les résultats en JSON')
    parser.add_argument('--json', action='store_true',
                       help='Sortie en format JSON uniquement')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # Validation
    if args.timeout <= 0:
        parser.error("Le timeout doit être positif")
    
    # Préparation des waypoints
    if args.preset:
        test_name = f"Test preset: {args.preset}"
    else:
        test_name = "Test waypoints personnalisés"
    
    # Initialisation ROS2
    rclpy.init()
    
    try:
        client = TestWaypointsCLI()
        
        # Génération des waypoints
        if args.preset:
            waypoints = client.get_preset_waypoints(args.preset)
            if not waypoints:
                print(f"❌ Preset '{args.preset}' non trouvé ou vide")
                return 1
        else:
            waypoints = []
            for wp_str in args.custom:
                try:
                    coords = [float(x.strip()) for x in wp_str.split(',')]
                    if len(coords) == 2:
                        coords.append(15.0)  # Altitude par défaut
                    elif len(coords) != 3:
                        raise ValueError("Format invalide")
                    waypoints.append(tuple(coords))
                except ValueError:
                    print(f"❌ Waypoint invalide: '{wp_str}' (format: x,y[,z])")
                    return 1
        
        print(f"🚁 Test de navigation: {len(waypoints)} waypoints")
        print("Appuyez sur Ctrl+C pour arrêter")
        
        # Exécution du test
        test_summary = client.run_waypoint_test(waypoints, test_name)
        
        # Affichage des résultats
        if args.json:
            print(json.dumps(test_summary, indent=2))
        else:
            report = client.format_test_report(test_summary, args.detailed)
            print(report)
        
        # Export si demandé
        if args.export:
            try:
                with open(args.export, 'w') as f:
                    json.dump(test_summary, f, indent=2)
                print(f"\n📁 Résultats exportés vers: {args.export}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ Erreur export: {e}")
                return 1
        
        # Code de retour basé sur le taux de réussite
        success_rate = test_summary['success_rate']
        if success_rate >= 95:
            return 0  # Excellent
        elif success_rate >= 80:
            return 0  # Bon
        elif success_rate >= 60:
            return 1  # Moyen - avertissement
        else:
            return 2  # Insuffisant - erreur
    
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n🛑 Test interrompu par l'utilisateur")
        return 1
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ Erreur: {e}")
        return 1
    finally:
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Outil CLI pour diagnostics avancés du système de navigation.

Usage:
    nav_diagnostics
    nav_diagnostics --detailed
    nav_diagnostics --performance
    nav_diagnostics --export report.json
"""

import argparse
import sys
import json
import time
import math
import statistics
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime

import rclpy
from rclpy.node import Node
from std_msgs.msg import String
from geometry_msgs.msg import PoseStamped, TwistStamped
from sensor_msgs.msg import NavSatFix, Imu
from diagnostic_msgs.msg import DiagnosticArray, DiagnosticStatus


class NavDiagnosticsCLI(Node):
    """Client CLI pour diagnostics de navigation."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__('nav_diagnostics_cli')
        
        # Données collectées
        self._navigation_status = None
        self._position_history = []
        self._velocity_history = []
        self._imu_data = None
        self._diagnostics = []
        
        # Métriques calculées
        self._metrics = {}
        
        # Subscribers
        self._status_sub = self.create_subscription(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/status',
            self._status_callback,
            10
        )
        
        self._pose_sub = self.create_subscription(
            PoseStamped,
            '/mavros/local_position/pose',
            self._pose_callback,
            10
        )
        
        self._velocity_sub = self.create_subscription(
            TwistStamped,
            '/mavros/local_position/velocity_local',
            self._velocity_callback,
            10
        )
        
        self._imu_sub = self.create_subscription(
            Imu,
            '/mavros/imu/data',
            self._imu_callback,
            10
        )
        
        self._diag_sub = self.create_subscription(
            DiagnosticArray,
            '/diagnostics',
            self._diagnostics_callback,
            10
        )
        
        # Timers pour collecte de données
        self._collection_timer = self.create_timer(0.1, self._collect_data)
        
        # Historique limité
        self._max_history = 100
    
    def _status_callback(self, msg: String):
        """Callback pour statut de navigation."""
        try:
            self._navigation_status = json.loads(msg.data)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            self._navigation_status = {'raw_message': msg.data}
    
    def _pose_callback(self, msg: PoseStamped):
        """Callback pour position."""
        pose_data = {
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'x': msg.pose.position.x,
            'y': msg.pose.position.y,
            'z': msg.pose.position.z,
            'qx': msg.pose.orientation.x,
            'qy': msg.pose.orientation.y,
            'qz': msg.pose.orientation.z,
            'qw': msg.pose.orientation.w
        }
        
        self._position_history.append(pose_data)
        if len(self._position_history) > self._max_history:
            self._position_history.pop(0)
    
    def _velocity_callback(self, msg: TwistStamped):
        """Callback pour vélocité."""
        vel_data = {
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'vx': msg.twist.linear.x,
            'vy': msg.twist.linear.y,
            'vz': msg.twist.linear.z,
            'wx': msg.twist.angular.x,
            'wy': msg.twist.angular.y,
            'wz': msg.twist.angular.z
        }
        
        self._velocity_history.append(vel_data)
        if len(self._velocity_history) > self._max_history:
            self._velocity_history.pop(0)
    
    def _imu_callback(self, msg: Imu):
        """Callback pour IMU."""
        self._imu_data = {
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'acceleration': {
                'x': msg.linear_acceleration.x,
                'y': msg.linear_acceleration.y,
                'z': msg.linear_acceleration.z
            },
            'angular_velocity': {
                'x': msg.angular_velocity.x,
                'y': msg.angular_velocity.y,
                'z': msg.angular_velocity.z
            },
            'orientation': {
                'x': msg.orientation.x,
                'y': msg.orientation.y,
                'z': msg.orientation.z,
                'w': msg.orientation.w
            }
        }
    
    def _diagnostics_callback(self, msg: DiagnosticArray):
        """Callback pour diagnostics ROS."""
        for status in msg.status:
            diag_data = {
                'timestamp': time.time(),
                'name': status.name,
                'level': status.level,
                'message': status.message,
                'hardware_id': status.hardware_id,
                'values': {kv.key: kv.value for kv in status.values}
            }
            self._diagnostics.append(diag_data)
        
        # Limitation de l'historique
        if len(self._diagnostics) > self._max_history * 5:
            self._diagnostics = self._diagnostics[-self._max_history * 3:]
    
    def _collect_data(self):
        """Collecte périodique de données pour métriques."""
        # Cette méthode est appelée périodiquement pour maintenir les données à jour
        pass
    
    def calculate_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Calcule les métriques de diagnostic."""
        metrics = {
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'data_collection': {},
            'position_analysis': {},
            'velocity_analysis': {},
            'stability_analysis': {},
            'performance_analysis': {},
            'health_status': {}
        }
        
        # Analyse des données collectées
        metrics['data_collection'] = {
            'position_samples': len(self._position_history),
            'velocity_samples': len(self._velocity_history),
            'diagnostics_messages': len(self._diagnostics),
            'navigation_status_available': self._navigation_status is not None,
            'imu_data_available': self._imu_data is not None
        }
        
        # Analyse de position
        if len(self._position_history) > 1:
            positions = self._position_history[-20:]  # 20 dernières positions
            
            # Calcul de la dérive
            if len(positions) >= 2:
                start_pos = positions[0]
                end_pos = positions[-1]
                time_diff = end_pos['timestamp'] - start_pos['timestamp']
                
                if time_diff > 0:
                    dx = end_pos['x'] - start_pos['x']
                    dy = end_pos['y'] - start_pos['y']
                    dz = end_pos['z'] - start_pos['z']
                    
                    drift_rate = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) / time_diff
                    metrics['position_analysis']['drift_rate'] = drift_rate
            
            # Stabilité de position
            recent_x = [p['x'] for p in positions]
            recent_y = [p['y'] for p in positions]
            recent_z = [p['z'] for p in positions]
            
            if len(recent_x) > 2:
                metrics['position_analysis']['position_stability'] = {
                    'x_std': statistics.stdev(recent_x),
                    'y_std': statistics.stdev(recent_y),
                    'z_std': statistics.stdev(recent_z),
                    'x_range': max(recent_x) - min(recent_x),
                    'y_range': max(recent_y) - min(recent_y),
                    'z_range': max(recent_z) - min(recent_z)
                }
        
        # Analyse de vélocité
        if len(self._velocity_history) > 1:
            velocities = self._velocity_history[-20:]
            
            # Vitesses moyennes et maximales
            vx_values = [v['vx'] for v in velocities]
            vy_values = [v['vy'] for v in velocities]
            vz_values = [v['vz'] for v in velocities]
            
            ground_speeds = [math.sqrt(v['vx']**2 + v['vy']**2) for v in velocities]
            total_speeds = [math.sqrt(v['vx']**2 + v['vy']**2 + v['vz']**2) for v in velocities]
            
            if len(ground_speeds) > 0:
                metrics['velocity_analysis'] = {
                    'avg_ground_speed': statistics.mean(ground_speeds),
                    'max_ground_speed': max(ground_speeds),
                    'avg_total_speed': statistics.mean(total_speeds),
                    'max_total_speed': max(total_speeds),
                    'velocity_stability': {
                        'vx_std': statistics.stdev(vx_values) if len(vx_values) > 1 else 0,
                        'vy_std': statistics.stdev(vy_values) if len(vy_values) > 1 else 0,
                        'vz_std': statistics.stdev(vz_values) if len(vz_values) > 1 else 0
                    }
                }
        
        # Analyse IMU
        if self._imu_data:
            imu = self._imu_data
            accel = imu['acceleration']
            accel_magnitude = math.sqrt(accel['x']**2 + accel['y']**2 + accel['z']**2)
            
            angular_vel = imu['angular_velocity']
            angular_magnitude = math.sqrt(angular_vel['x']**2 + angular_vel['y']**2 + angular_vel['z']**2)
            
            metrics['stability_analysis'] = {
                'acceleration_magnitude': accel_magnitude,
                'angular_velocity_magnitude': angular_magnitude,
                'gravity_alignment': abs(accel['z'] - 9.81),  # Écart par rapport à g
                'imu_health': 'good' if accel_magnitude < 15.0 and angular_magnitude < 2.0 else 'warning'
            }
        
        # Analyse de performance du système de navigation
        if self._navigation_status:
            nav = self._navigation_status
            
            performance = {}
            
            # Fréquence de boucle
            if 'loop_frequency' in nav:
                freq = nav['loop_frequency']
                performance['loop_frequency'] = freq
                performance['loop_frequency_health'] = 'good' if freq > 20 else 'warning' if freq > 10 else 'error'
            
            # Utilisation des ressources
            if 'cpu_usage' in nav:
                cpu = nav['cpu_usage']
                performance['cpu_usage'] = cpu
                performance['cpu_health'] = 'good' if cpu < 70 else 'warning' if cpu < 90 else 'error'
            
            if 'memory_usage' in nav:
                mem = nav['memory_usage']
                performance['memory_usage'] = mem
                performance['memory_health'] = 'good' if mem < 80 else 'warning' if mem < 95 else 'error'
            
            # Temps de calcul des algorithmes
            for algo in ['path_planning_time', 'control_update_time', 'obstacle_detection_time']:
                if algo in nav:
                    performance[algo] = nav[algo]
            
            metrics['performance_analysis'] = performance
        
        # Analyse des diagnostics ROS
        recent_diagnostics = [d for d in self._diagnostics if time.time() - d['timestamp'] < 30]
        
        error_count = len([d for d in recent_diagnostics if d['level'] == DiagnosticStatus.ERROR])
        warn_count = len([d for d in recent_diagnostics if d['level'] == DiagnosticStatus.WARN])
        
        metrics['health_status'] = {
            'recent_errors': error_count,
            'recent_warnings': warn_count,
            'overall_health': 'good' if error_count == 0 and warn_count < 3 else 'warning' if error_count == 0 else 'error',
            'diagnostic_messages': len(recent_diagnostics)
        }
        
        # Score global de santé
        health_score = 100
        if metrics['health_status']['recent_errors'] > 0:
            health_score -= 30
        if metrics['health_status']['recent_warnings'] > 2:
            health_score -= 15
        if 'cpu_health' in metrics['performance_analysis'] and metrics['performance_analysis']['cpu_health'] != 'good':
            health_score -= 10
        if 'loop_frequency_health' in metrics['performance_analysis'] and metrics['performance_analysis']['loop_frequency_health'] != 'good':
            health_score -= 20
        
        metrics['health_status']['overall_score'] = max(0, health_score)
        
        return metrics
    
    def collect_data(self, duration: float = 10.0) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Collecte des données pendant une durée spécifiée."""
        self.get_logger().info(f"Collecte de données pendant {duration}s...")
        
        start_time = time.time()
        rate = self.create_rate(10.0)  # 10 Hz
        
        while rclpy.ok() and (time.time() - start_time) < duration:
            rclpy.spin_once(self, timeout_sec=0.01)
            rate.sleep()
        
        return self.calculate_metrics()
    
    def format_diagnostics_report(self, metrics: Dict[str, Any], detailed: bool = False) -> str:
        """Formate le rapport de diagnostics."""
        lines = []
        
        # En-tête
        lines.append("=" * 70)
        lines.append("RAPPORT DE DIAGNOSTICS - SYSTÈME DE NAVIGATION")
        lines.append("=" * 70)
        lines.append(f"Généré le: {datetime.fromtimestamp(metrics['timestamp']).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        
        # Score de santé global
        health = metrics.get('health_status', {})
        score = health.get('overall_score', 0)
        lines.append(f"\n🔍 SCORE DE SANTÉ GLOBAL: {score}/100")
        
        if score >= 80:
            lines.append("   ✅ État: EXCELLENT")
        elif score >= 60:
            lines.append("   ⚠️  État: ATTENTION REQUISE")
        else:
            lines.append("   ❌ État: INTERVENTION NÉCESSAIRE")
        
        # Collecte de données
        collection = metrics.get('data_collection', {})
        lines.append(f"\n📊 COLLECTE DE DONNÉES:")
        lines.append(f"   Position: {collection.get('position_samples', 0)} échantillons")
        lines.append(f"   Vélocité: {collection.get('velocity_samples', 0)} échantillons")
        lines.append(f"   Diagnostics: {collection.get('diagnostics_messages', 0)} messages")
        lines.append(f"   Statut navigation: {'✅' if collection.get('navigation_status_available') else '❌'}")
        lines.append(f"   Données IMU: {'✅' if collection.get('imu_data_available') else '❌'}")
        
        # Performances
        perf = metrics.get('performance_analysis', {})
        if perf:
            lines.append(f"\n⚡ PERFORMANCES:")
            
            if 'loop_frequency' in perf:
                freq = perf['loop_frequency']
                health_icon = "✅" if perf.get('loop_frequency_health') == 'good' else "⚠️" if perf.get('loop_frequency_health') == 'warning' else "❌"
                lines.append(f"   Fréquence boucle: {freq:.1f}Hz {health_icon}")
            
            if 'cpu_usage' in perf:
                cpu = perf['cpu_usage']
                health_icon = "✅" if perf.get('cpu_health') == 'good' else "⚠️" if perf.get('cpu_health') == 'warning' else "❌"
                lines.append(f"   Utilisation CPU: {cpu:.1f}% {health_icon}")
            
            if 'memory_usage' in perf:
                mem = perf['memory_usage']
                health_icon = "✅" if perf.get('memory_health') == 'good' else "⚠️" if perf.get('memory_health') == 'warning' else "❌"
                lines.append(f"   Utilisation mémoire: {mem:.1f}% {health_icon}")
        
        # Stabilité
        stability = metrics.get('stability_analysis', {})
        if stability:
            lines.append(f"\n🎯 STABILITÉ:")
            lines.append(f"   Magnitude accélération: {stability.get('acceleration_magnitude', 0):.2f}m/s²")
            lines.append(f"   Magnitude vitesse angulaire: {stability.get('angular_velocity_magnitude', 0):.3f}rad/s")
            lines.append(f"   Alignement gravité: {stability.get('gravity_alignment', 0):.2f}m/s²")
            
            imu_health = stability.get('imu_health', 'unknown')
            health_icon = "✅" if imu_health == 'good' else "⚠️"
            lines.append(f"   Santé IMU: {imu_health.upper()} {health_icon}")
        
        if detailed:
            # Analyse détaillée de position
            pos_analysis = metrics.get('position_analysis', {})
            if pos_analysis:
                lines.append(f"\n📍 ANALYSE POSITION DÉTAILLÉE:")
                
                if 'drift_rate' in pos_analysis:
                    drift = pos_analysis['drift_rate']
                    lines.append(f"   Taux de dérive: {drift:.4f}m/s")
                
                stability_pos = pos_analysis.get('position_stability', {})
                if stability_pos:
                    lines.append(f"   Stabilité X: ±{stability_pos.get('x_std', 0):.3f}m (étendue: {stability_pos.get('x_range', 0):.3f}m)")
                    lines.append(f"   Stabilité Y: ±{stability_pos.get('y_std', 0):.3f}m (étendue: {stability_pos.get('y_range', 0):.3f}m)")
                    lines.append(f"   Stabilité Z: ±{stability_pos.get('z_std', 0):.3f}m (étendue: {stability_pos.get('z_range', 0):.3f}m)")
            
            # Analyse détaillée de vélocité
            vel_analysis = metrics.get('velocity_analysis', {})
            if vel_analysis:
                lines.append(f"\n🚁 ANALYSE VÉLOCITÉ DÉTAILLÉE:")
                lines.append(f"   Vitesse sol moyenne: {vel_analysis.get('avg_ground_speed', 0):.2f}m/s")
                lines.append(f"   Vitesse sol maximale: {vel_analysis.get('max_ground_speed', 0):.2f}m/s")
                lines.append(f"   Vitesse totale moyenne: {vel_analysis.get('avg_total_speed', 0):.2f}m/s")
                
                vel_stability = vel_analysis.get('velocity_stability', {})
                if vel_stability:
                    lines.append(f"   Stabilité Vx: ±{vel_stability.get('vx_std', 0):.3f}m/s")
                    lines.append(f"   Stabilité Vy: ±{vel_stability.get('vy_std', 0):.3f}m/s")
                    lines.append(f"   Stabilité Vz: ±{vel_stability.get('vz_std', 0):.3f}m/s")
        
        # Erreurs et avertissements
        lines.append(f"\n🚨 ÉTAT SYSTÈME:")
        lines.append(f"   Erreurs récentes: {health.get('recent_errors', 0)}")
        lines.append(f"   Avertissements récents: {health.get('recent_warnings', 0)}")
        
        # Recommandations
        lines.append(f"\n💡 RECOMMANDATIONS:")
        
        if score < 60:
            lines.append("   ❗ Intervention urgente recommandée")
        elif score < 80:
            lines.append("   ⚠️  Surveillance accrue recommandée")
        else:
            lines.append("   ✅ Système en bon état")
        
        if perf.get('cpu_health') == 'error':
            lines.append("   - Réduire la charge CPU ou optimiser les algorithmes")
        if perf.get('loop_frequency_health') in ['warning', 'error']:
            lines.append("   - Vérifier la performance de la boucle de contrôle")
        if health.get('recent_errors', 0) > 0:
            lines.append("   - Investiguer les erreurs système récentes")
        
        lines.append("\n" + "=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(lines)


def main():
    """Point d'entrée principal."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Diagnostics avancés du système de navigation",
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        epilog="""
Exemples:
    nav_diagnostics                        # Diagnostic standard
    nav_diagnostics --detailed             # Rapport détaillé
    nav_diagnostics --performance          # Focus sur les performances
    nav_diagnostics --duration 30          # Collecte sur 30 secondes
    nav_diagnostics --export report.json   # Export en JSON
        """
    )
    
    parser.add_argument('--detailed', '-d', action='store_true',
                       help='Rapport détaillé avec toutes les métriques')
    parser.add_argument('--performance', '-p', action='store_true',
                       help='Focus sur l\'analyse de performance')
    parser.add_argument('--duration', type=float, default=10.0,
                       help='Durée de collecte en secondes (défaut: 10)')
    parser.add_argument('--export', '-e', type=str,
                       help='Exporter les résultats en JSON')
    parser.add_argument('--json', action='store_true',
                       help='Sortie en format JSON uniquement')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # Validation
    if args.duration <= 0:
        parser.error("La durée doit être positive")
    
    # Initialisation ROS2
    rclpy.init()
    
    try:
        client = NavDiagnosticsCLI()
        
        # Collecte de données
        metrics = client.collect_data(args.duration)
        
        # Affichage
        if args.json:
            print(json.dumps(metrics, indent=2))
        else:
            report = client.format_diagnostics_report(metrics, args.detailed or args.performance)
            print(report)
        
        # Export si demandé
        if args.export:
            try:
                with open(args.export, 'w') as f:
                    json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)
                print(f"\n📁 Rapport exporté vers: {args.export}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ Erreur export: {e}")
                return 1
        
        # Code de retour basé sur la santé du système
        health_score = metrics.get('health_status', {}).get('overall_score', 0)
        if health_score >= 80:
            return 0  # Tout va bien
        elif health_score >= 60:
            return 1  # Avertissement
        else:
            return 2  # Erreur critique
    
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterruption par l'utilisateur")
        return 1
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Erreur: {e}")
        return 1
    finally:
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Outil CLI pour afficher le statut de navigation en temps réel.

Usage:
    nav_status
    nav_status --watch
    nav_status --json
"""

import argparse
import sys
import json
import time
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

import rclpy
from rclpy.node import Node
from std_msgs.msg import String
from geometry_msgs.msg import PoseStamped, TwistStamped
from sensor_msgs.msg import NavSatFix


class NavStatusCLI(Node):
    """Client CLI pour affichage du statut de navigation."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__('nav_status_cli')
        
        # Données de statut
        self._navigation_status = None
        self._position = None
        self._velocity = None
        self._gps_position = None
        
        # Subscribers
        self._status_sub = self.create_subscription(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/status',
            self._status_callback,
            10
        )
        
        self._pose_sub = self.create_subscription(
            PoseStamped,
            '/mavros/local_position/pose',
            self._pose_callback,
            10
        )
        
        self._velocity_sub = self.create_subscription(
            TwistStamped,
            '/mavros/local_position/velocity_local',
            self._velocity_callback,
            10
        )
        
        self._gps_sub = self.create_subscription(
            NavSatFix,
            '/mavros/global_position/global',
            self._gps_callback,
            10
        )
    
    def _status_callback(self, msg: String):
        """Callback pour statut de navigation."""
        try:
            self._navigation_status = json.loads(msg.data)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            self._navigation_status = {'raw_message': msg.data}
    
    def _pose_callback(self, msg: PoseStamped):
        """Callback pour position locale."""
        self._position = {
            'x': msg.pose.position.x,
            'y': msg.pose.position.y,
            'z': msg.pose.position.z,
            'orientation': {
                'x': msg.pose.orientation.x,
                'y': msg.pose.orientation.y,
                'z': msg.pose.orientation.z,
                'w': msg.pose.orientation.w
            }
        }
    
    def _velocity_callback(self, msg: TwistStamped):
        """Callback pour vélocité."""
        self._velocity = {
            'linear': {
                'x': msg.twist.linear.x,
                'y': msg.twist.linear.y,
                'z': msg.twist.linear.z
            },
            'angular': {
                'x': msg.twist.angular.x,
                'y': msg.twist.angular.y,
                'z': msg.twist.angular.z
            }
        }
    
    def _gps_callback(self, msg: NavSatFix):
        """Callback pour position GPS."""
        self._gps_position = {
            'latitude': msg.latitude,
            'longitude': msg.longitude,
            'altitude': msg.altitude,
            'status': msg.status.status,
            'service': msg.status.service
        }
    
    def get_status_data(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Récupère toutes les données de statut."""
        # Attente des données
        timeout = 5.0
        start_time = time.time()
        
        while (time.time() - start_time) < timeout:
            rclpy.spin_once(self, timeout_sec=0.1)
            if self._navigation_status is not None:
                break
        
        # Compilation des données
        status_data = {
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'navigation': self._navigation_status or {},
            'position': self._position or {},
            'velocity': self._velocity or {},
            'gps': self._gps_position or {}
        }
        
        # Calculs dérivés
        if self._velocity:
            # Vitesse au sol
            vx, vy = self._velocity['linear']['x'], self._velocity['linear']['y']
            status_data['ground_speed'] = (vx**2 + vy**2)**0.5
            
            # Vitesse 3D
            vz = self._velocity['linear']['z']
            status_data['total_speed'] = (vx**2 + vy**2 + vz**2)**0.5
        
        if self._position:
            # Distance depuis origine
            x, y, z = self._position['x'], self._position['y'], self._position['z']
            status_data['distance_from_origin'] = (x**2 + y**2 + z**2)**0.5
        
        return status_data
    
    def format_status_human(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Formate le statut pour affichage humain."""
        lines = []
        
        # En-tête
        lines.append("=" * 60)
        lines.append("STATUT DE NAVIGATION DRONE")
        lines.append("=" * 60)
        
        # Navigation
        nav = data.get('navigation', {})
        if nav:
            lines.append("\n📍 NAVIGATION:")
            lines.append(f"  État: {nav.get('state', 'Inconnu')}")
            lines.append(f"  Mode: {nav.get('mode', 'Inconnu')}")
            
            if 'mission_progress' in nav:
                progress = nav['mission_progress']
                lines.append(f"  Progrès mission: {progress:.1f}%")
            
            if 'current_waypoint' in nav and 'total_waypoints' in nav:
                current = nav['current_waypoint']
                total = nav['total_waypoints']
                lines.append(f"  Waypoint: {current}/{total}")
            
            if 'distance_to_target' in nav:
                distance = nav['distance_to_target']
                lines.append(f"  Distance à la cible: {distance:.2f}m")
            
            if 'estimated_time_remaining' in nav:
                eta = nav['estimated_time_remaining']
                lines.append(f"  Temps restant estimé: {eta:.1f}s")
        
        # Position
        pos = data.get('position', {})
        if pos:
            lines.append(f"\n📍 POSITION LOCALE:")
            lines.append(f"  X: {pos.get('x', 0):.2f}m")
            lines.append(f"  Y: {pos.get('y', 0):.2f}m")
            lines.append(f"  Z: {pos.get('z', 0):.2f}m (altitude)")
            
            if 'distance_from_origin' in data:
                dist = data['distance_from_origin']
                lines.append(f"  Distance origine: {dist:.2f}m")
        
        # GPS
        gps = data.get('gps', {})
        if gps and gps.get('latitude') != 0:
            lines.append(f"\n🌍 POSITION GPS:")
            lines.append(f"  Latitude: {gps.get('latitude', 0):.7f}°")
            lines.append(f"  Longitude: {gps.get('longitude', 0):.7f}°")
            lines.append(f"  Altitude GPS: {gps.get('altitude', 0):.2f}m")
            
            status_names = {0: "NO_FIX", 1: "FIX", 2: "SBAS_FIX", 3: "GBAS_FIX"}
            gps_status = status_names.get(gps.get('status', -1), "UNKNOWN")
            lines.append(f"  Statut GPS: {gps_status}")
        
        # Vélocité
        vel = data.get('velocity', {})
        if vel:
            lines.append(f"\n🚁 VÉLOCITÉ:")
            linear = vel.get('linear', {})
            lines.append(f"  Vx: {linear.get('x', 0):.2f}m/s")
            lines.append(f"  Vy: {linear.get('y', 0):.2f}m/s")
            lines.append(f"  Vz: {linear.get('z', 0):.2f}m/s")
            
            if 'ground_speed' in data:
                lines.append(f"  Vitesse sol: {data['ground_speed']:.2f}m/s")
            
            if 'total_speed' in data:
                lines.append(f"  Vitesse totale: {data['total_speed']:.2f}m/s")
        
        # Obstacles et sécurité
        if nav and 'obstacles_detected' in nav:
            lines.append(f"\n⚠️  SÉCURITÉ:")
            obstacles = nav['obstacles_detected']
            lines.append(f"  Obstacles détectés: {len(obstacles) if isinstance(obstacles, list) else obstacles}")
            
            if 'geofence_status' in nav:
                geofence = nav['geofence_status']
                lines.append(f"  Statut geofence: {geofence}")
        
        # Performances
        if nav and any(key in nav for key in ['cpu_usage', 'memory_usage', 'loop_frequency']):
            lines.append(f"\n⚡ PERFORMANCES:")
            if 'cpu_usage' in nav:
                lines.append(f"  CPU: {nav['cpu_usage']:.1f}%")
            if 'memory_usage' in nav:
                lines.append(f"  Mémoire: {nav['memory_usage']:.1f}%")
            if 'loop_frequency' in nav:
                lines.append(f"  Fréquence: {nav['loop_frequency']:.1f}Hz")
        
        lines.append("\n" + "=" * 60)
        return "\n".join(lines)


def watch_status(client: NavStatusCLI, json_output: bool = False, refresh_rate: float = 1.0):
    """Mode watch pour affichage continu."""
    try:
        while rclpy.ok():
            # Effacement de l'écran
            if not json_output:
                print("\033[2J\033[H", end="")  # Clear screen and move cursor to top
            
            # Récupération et affichage des données
            data = client.get_status_data()
            
            if json_output:
                print(json.dumps(data, indent=2))
                print("-" * 40)
            else:
                print(client.format_status_human(data))
            
            # Attente
            time.sleep(refresh_rate)
            
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nArrêt du monitoring...")


def main():
    """Point d'entrée principal."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Affichage du statut de navigation",
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        epilog="""
Exemples:
    nav_status                    # Affichage unique
    nav_status --watch            # Monitoring continu
    nav_status --json             # Format JSON
    nav_status --watch --json     # Monitoring JSON
    nav_status --rate 0.5         # Refresh toutes les 0.5s
        """
    )
    
    parser.add_argument('--watch', '-w', action='store_true',
                       help='Mode monitoring continu')
    parser.add_argument('--json', '-j', action='store_true',
                       help='Format de sortie JSON')
    parser.add_argument('--rate', '-r', type=float, default=1.0,
                       help='Fréquence de rafraîchissement (Hz, défaut: 1.0)')
    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=float, default=5.0,
                       help='Timeout pour récupération des données (s)')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # Validation
    if args.rate <= 0:
        parser.error("La fréquence doit être positive")
    
    # Initialisation ROS2
    rclpy.init()
    
    try:
        client = NavStatusCLI()
        
        if args.watch:
            # Mode monitoring
            print("Démarrage du monitoring de navigation...")
            print("Appuyez sur Ctrl+C pour arrêter")
            watch_status(client, args.json, 1.0 / args.rate)
        else:
            # Affichage unique
            data = client.get_status_data()
            
            if args.json:
                print(json.dumps(data, indent=2))
            else:
                print(client.format_status_human(data))
    
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterruption par l'utilisateur")
        return 0
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Erreur: {e}")
        return 1
    finally:
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Outil CLI pour planifier et exécuter des missions de navigation.

Usage:
    plan_mission --file mission.yaml
    plan_mission --waypoints "10,20,15" "30,40,25"
    plan_mission --coverage --polygon "0,0 10,0 10,10 0,10" --pattern zigzag
"""

import argparse
import sys
import yaml
import json
import time
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple, Optional

import rclpy
from rclpy.node import Node
from geometry_msgs.msg import Point, Polygon, Point32
from std_msgs.msg import String
from nav_msgs.msg import Path
from std_srvs.srv import Trigger, SetBool
from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan


class PlanMissionCLI(Node):
    """Client CLI pour planification de missions."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__('plan_mission_cli')
        
        # Clients pour services de navigation
        self._plan_mission_client = self.create_client(
            GetPlan,
            '/drone_nav/plan_path'
        )
        
        self._execute_mission_client = self.create_client(
            Trigger,
            '/drone_nav/start_mission'
        )
        
        self._coverage_client = self.create_client(
            SetBool,
            '/drone_nav/set_waypoints'
        )
        
        # Subscriber pour le statut
        self._status_sub = self.create_subscription(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/status',
            self._status_callback,
            10
        )
        
        self._current_status = None
        self._mission_completed = False
        
    def _status_callback(self, msg: String):
        """Callback pour mise à jour du statut."""
        self._current_status = msg.data
        
        # Vérification si mission terminée
        if 'mission_completed' in msg.data or 'state": "idle"' in msg.data:
            self._mission_completed = True
    
    def plan_waypoint_mission(self, waypoints: List[Tuple[float, float, float]]) -> bool:
        """
        Planifie une mission avec waypoints.
        
        Args:
            waypoints: Liste de tuples (x, y, z)
            
        Returns:
            Succès de la planification
        """
        if not self._plan_mission_client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0):
            self.get_logger().error("Service de planification non disponible")
            return False
        
        # Création de la requête GetPlan standard
        request = GetPlan.Request()
        
        # Point de départ (premier waypoint)
        if waypoints:
            request.start.header.frame_id = "map"
            request.start.pose.position.x = waypoints[0][0]
            request.start.pose.position.y = waypoints[0][1] 
            request.start.pose.position.z = waypoints[0][2]
            
            # Point de destination (dernier waypoint)
            request.goal.header.frame_id = "map"
            request.goal.pose.position.x = waypoints[-1][0]
            request.goal.pose.position.y = waypoints[-1][1]
            request.goal.pose.position.z = waypoints[-1][2]
            
            request.tolerance = 1.0
        
        # Sérialisation des paramètres
        mission_params = {
            'waypoints': waypoints,
            'speed': 5.0,
            'precision_radius': 2.0
        }
        request.parameters = json.dumps(mission_params)
        
        # Envoi de la requête
        try:
            future = self._plan_service.call_async(request)
            rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(self, future, timeout_sec=10.0)
            
            if future.result() is not None:
                response = future.result()
                if response.success:
                    self.get_logger().info(f"Mission planifiée avec {len(waypoints)} waypoints")
                    
                    if execute:
                        return self.execute_mission(response.mission_id)
                    else:
                        self.get_logger().info(f"Mission ID: {response.mission_id}")
                        return True
                else:
                    self.get_logger().error(f"Échec de planification: {response.message}")
                    return False
            else:
                self.get_logger().error("Pas de réponse du service")
                return False
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur lors de l'appel du service: {e}")
            return False
    
    def plan_coverage_mission(self, polygon_points: List[Tuple[float, float]], 
                            pattern: str = "zigzag", altitude: float = 15.0,
                            spacing: float = 5.0, execute: bool = True) -> bool:
        """
        Planifie une mission de couverture de zone.
        
        Args:
            polygon_points: Points du polygone à couvrir
            pattern: Type de motif (zigzag, spiral, lawn_mower)
            altitude: Altitude de vol
            spacing: Espacement entre passes
            execute: Exécuter automatiquement
            
        Returns:
            True si succès
        """
        if not self._coverage_service.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0):
            self.get_logger().error("Service de couverture non disponible")
            return False
        
        # Création du polygone
        polygon = Polygon()
        for x, y in polygon_points:
            point = Point32()
            point.x = x
            point.y = y
            point.z = altitude
            polygon.points.append(point)
        
        # Requête de couverture
        coverage_request = GetCoveragePattern.Request()
        coverage_request.area = polygon
        coverage_request.pattern_type = pattern
        coverage_request.line_spacing = spacing
        coverage_request.altitude = altitude
        
        try:
            # Génération du motif de couverture
            future = self._coverage_service.call_async(coverage_request)
            rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(self, future, timeout_sec=10.0)
            
            if future.result() is None:
                self.get_logger().error("Pas de réponse du service de couverture")
                return False
            
            coverage_response = future.result()
            if not coverage_response.success:
                self.get_logger().error(f"Échec génération motif: {coverage_response.message}")
                return False
            
            # Conversion du path en waypoints
            waypoints = []
            for pose in coverage_response.path.poses:
                waypoints.append((
                    pose.pose.position.x,
                    pose.pose.position.y,
                    pose.pose.position.z
                ))
            
            self.get_logger().info(f"Motif {pattern} généré avec {len(waypoints)} points")
            
            # Planification de la mission
            if not self._plan_service.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0):
                self.get_logger().error("Service de planification non disponible")
                return False
            
            request = PlanMission.Request()
            request.mission_type = "coverage"
            
            mission_params = {
                'pattern': pattern,
                'polygon': polygon_points,
                'altitude': altitude,
                'spacing': spacing,
                'waypoints': waypoints
            }
            request.parameters = json.dumps(mission_params)
            
            # Planification
            future = self._plan_service.call_async(request)
            rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(self, future, timeout_sec=10.0)
            
            if future.result() is not None:
                response = future.result()
                if response.success:
                    self.get_logger().info(f"Mission de couverture planifiée")
                    
                    if execute:
                        return self.execute_mission(response.mission_id)
                    else:
                        self.get_logger().info(f"Mission ID: {response.mission_id}")
                        return True
                else:
                    self.get_logger().error(f"Échec de planification: {response.message}")
                    return False
            else:
                self.get_logger().error("Pas de réponse du service")
                return False
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur lors de la planification: {e}")
            return False
    
    def execute_mission(self, mission_id: str, wait: bool = True) -> bool:
        """
        Exécute une mission planifiée.
        
        Args:
            mission_id: ID de la mission à exécuter
            wait: Attendre la fin de l'exécution
            
        Returns:
            True si succès
        """
        if not self._execute_service.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0):
            self.get_logger().error("Service d'exécution non disponible")
            return False
        
        request = ExecuteMission.Request()
        request.mission_id = mission_id
        
        try:
            future = self._execute_service.call_async(request)
            rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(self, future, timeout_sec=10.0)
            
            if future.result() is not None:
                response = future.result()
                if response.success:
                    self.get_logger().info("Exécution de mission commencée")
                    
                    if wait:
                        return self._wait_for_completion()
                    else:
                        return True
                else:
                    self.get_logger().error(f"Échec d'exécution: {response.message}")
                    return False
            else:
                self.get_logger().error("Pas de réponse du service")
                return False
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur lors de l'exécution: {e}")
            return False
    
    def _wait_for_completion(self, timeout: float = 300.0) -> bool:
        """Attend la fin de la mission."""
        start_time = time.time()
        self._mission_completed = False
        
        rate = self.create_rate(1.0)  # 1 Hz
        
        while rclpy.ok() and not self._mission_completed:
            if time.time() - start_time > timeout:
                self.get_logger().warn("Timeout atteint")
                return False
            
            rclpy.spin_once(self, timeout_sec=0.1)
            
            # Affichage du statut
            if self._current_status:
                try:
                    status_data = json.loads(self._current_status)
                    progress = status_data.get('mission_progress', 0)
                    current_wp = status_data.get('current_waypoint', 0)
                    total_wp = status_data.get('total_waypoints', 0)
                    self.get_logger().info(f"Progrès: {progress:.1f}% ({current_wp}/{total_wp})")
                except:
                    pass
            
            rate.sleep()
        
        if self._mission_completed:
            self.get_logger().info("Mission terminée avec succès!")
            return True
        else:
            return False


def load_mission_file(filepath: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Charge un fichier de mission YAML."""
    try:
        with open(filepath, 'r') as file:
            return yaml.safe_load(file)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Erreur lecture fichier: {e}")
        return None


def parse_waypoints(waypoint_strings: List[str]) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
    """Parse les chaînes de waypoints."""
    waypoints = []
    for wp_str in waypoint_strings:
        try:
            coords = [float(x.strip()) for x in wp_str.split(',')]
            if len(coords) == 2:
                coords.append(15.0)  # Altitude par défaut
            elif len(coords) != 3:
                raise ValueError("Coordonnées invalides")
            waypoints.append(tuple(coords))
        except ValueError as e:
            print(f"Erreur parsing waypoint '{wp_str}': {e}")
            return None
    return waypoints


def parse_polygon(polygon_string: str) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
    """Parse une chaîne de polygone."""
    try:
        points = []
        coord_pairs = polygon_string.split()
        for pair in coord_pairs:
            x, y = [float(coord.strip()) for coord in pair.split(',')]
            points.append((x, y))
        return points
    except ValueError as e:
        print(f"Erreur parsing polygone: {e}")
        return None


def main():
    """Point d'entrée principal."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Planification et exécution de missions de navigation",
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        epilog="""
Exemples:
    plan_mission --file mission.yaml
    plan_mission --waypoints "10,20,15" "30,40,25" "50,10,20"
    plan_mission --coverage --polygon "0,0 10,0 10,10 0,10" --pattern zigzag
    plan_mission --waypoints "10,20" "30,40" --altitude 25 --no-execute
        """
    )
    
    # Mode fichier
    parser.add_argument('--file', '-f', type=str,
                       help='Fichier de mission YAML')
    
    # Mode waypoints
    parser.add_argument('--waypoints', '-w', nargs='+',
                       help='Liste de waypoints "x,y[,z]"')
    
    # Mode couverture
    coverage_group = parser.add_argument_group('Mission de couverture')
    coverage_group.add_argument('--coverage', action='store_true',
                               help='Mode couverture de zone')
    coverage_group.add_argument('--polygon', type=str,
                               help='Polygone "x1,y1 x2,y2 x3,y3 ..."')
    coverage_group.add_argument('--pattern', choices=['zigzag', 'spiral', 'lawn_mower'],
                               default='zigzag', help='Motif de couverture')
    coverage_group.add_argument('--spacing', type=float, default=5.0,
                               help='Espacement entre passes (mètres)')
    
    # Options générales
    parser.add_argument('--altitude', type=float, default=15.0,
                       help='Altitude par défaut (mètres)')
    parser.add_argument('--no-execute', action='store_true',
                       help='Planifier seulement, ne pas exécuter')
    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=float, default=300.0,
                       help='Timeout en secondes (défaut: 300)')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # Validation des arguments
    mode_count = sum([
        bool(args.file),
        bool(args.waypoints),
        bool(args.coverage)
    ])
    
    if mode_count == 0:
        parser.error("Spécifiez --file, --waypoints, ou --coverage")
    elif mode_count > 1:
        parser.error("Spécifiez un seul mode à la fois")
    
    if args.coverage and not args.polygon:
        parser.error("--coverage nécessite --polygon")
    
    # Initialisation ROS2
    rclpy.init()
    
    try:
        client = PlanMissionCLI()
        execute = not args.no_execute
        
        if args.file:
            # Mode fichier
            mission_data = load_mission_file(args.file)
            if mission_data is None:
                return 1
            
            print(f"Chargement mission depuis {args.file}")
            
            # Traitement selon le type de mission dans le fichier
            mission_type = mission_data.get('type', 'waypoint')
            
            if mission_type == 'waypoint':
                waypoints = [(wp['x'], wp['y'], wp.get('z', args.altitude)) 
                           for wp in mission_data.get('waypoints', [])]
                success = client.plan_waypoint_mission(waypoints, execute)
            elif mission_type == 'coverage':
                polygon_points = [(p['x'], p['y']) for p in mission_data.get('polygon', [])]
                pattern = mission_data.get('pattern', 'zigzag')
                spacing = mission_data.get('spacing', 5.0)
                success = client.plan_coverage_mission(
                    polygon_points, pattern, args.altitude, spacing, execute
                )
            else:
                print(f"Type de mission non supporté: {mission_type}")
                return 1
        
        elif args.waypoints:
            # Mode waypoints
            waypoints = parse_waypoints(args.waypoints)
            if waypoints is None:
                return 1
            
            # Application de l'altitude par défaut si non spécifiée
            waypoints = [(x, y, z if z != 15.0 else args.altitude) 
                        for x, y, z in waypoints]
            
            print(f"Mission waypoints: {len(waypoints)} points")
            success = client.plan_waypoint_mission(waypoints, execute)
        
        elif args.coverage:
            # Mode couverture
            polygon_points = parse_polygon(args.polygon)
            if polygon_points is None:
                return 1
            
            print(f"Mission couverture: polygone {len(polygon_points)} points, motif {args.pattern}")
            success = client.plan_coverage_mission(
                polygon_points, args.pattern, args.altitude, args.spacing, execute
            )
        
        if success:
            print("✅ Mission réussie!")
            return 0
        else:
            print("❌ Mission échouée!")
            return 1
    
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterruption par l'utilisateur")
        return 1
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Erreur: {e}")
        return 1
    finally:
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
