#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Outil CLI pour naviguer vers une position spécifique.

Usage:
    goto_position --x 10.0 --y 20.0 --z 15.0
    goto_position --lat 45.123456 --lon 2.654321 --alt 15.0
    goto_position --interactive
"""

import argparse
import sys
import time
from typing import Optional

import rclpy
from rclpy.node import Node
from geometry_msgs.msg import Point
from geometry_msgs.srv import GetPlan
from std_msgs.msg import String


class GotoPositionCLI(Node):
    """Client CLI pour navigation vers position."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__('goto_position_cli')
        
        # Service client pour navigation
        self._goto_service = self.create_client(
            GetPlan,
            '/drone_nav/goto_position'
        )
        
        # Subscriber pour le statut
        self._status_sub = self.create_subscription(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/status',
            self._status_callback,
            10
        )
        
        self._current_status = None
        self._mission_completed = False
        
    def _status_callback(self, msg: String):
        """Callback pour mise à jour du statut."""
        self._current_status = msg.data
        
        # Vérification si mission terminée
        if 'state": "idle"' in msg.data or 'is_converged": true' in msg.data:
            self._mission_completed = True
    
    def goto_position(self, x: float, y: float, z: float, wait: bool = True) -> bool:
        """
        Navigue vers une position.
        
        Args:
            x, y, z: Coordonnées de destination
            wait: Attendre la fin de la navigation
            
        Returns:
            True si succès
        """
        # Attente du service
        if not self._goto_service.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5.0):
            self.get_logger().error("Service de navigation non disponible")
            return False
        
        # Création de la requête
        request = GetPlan.Request()
        request.start.header.frame_id = "map"
        request.start.pose.position.x = 0.0  # Position actuelle (sera déterminée par le service)
        request.start.pose.position.y = 0.0
        request.start.pose.position.z = 0.0
        
        request.goal.header.frame_id = "map"
        request.goal.pose.position.x = x
        request.goal.pose.position.y = y
        request.goal.pose.position.z = z
        
        # Envoi de la requête
        try:
            future = self._goto_service.call_async(request)
            rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(self, future, timeout_sec=10.0)
            
            if future.result() is not None:
                response = future.result()
                if len(response.plan.poses) > 0:
                    self.get_logger().info(f"Navigation vers ({x}, {y}, {z}) initiée")
                    
                    if wait:
                        return self._wait_for_completion()
                    else:
                        return True
                else:
                    self.get_logger().error("Échec de la planification")
                    return False
            else:
                self.get_logger().error("Pas de réponse du service")
                return False
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur lors de l'appel du service: {e}")
            return False
    
    def _wait_for_completion(self, timeout: float = 120.0) -> bool:
        """Attend la fin de la navigation."""
        start_time = time.time()
        self._mission_completed = False
        
        rate = self.create_rate(2.0)  # 2 Hz
        
        while rclpy.ok() and not self._mission_completed:
            if time.time() - start_time > timeout:
                self.get_logger().warn("Timeout atteint")
                return False
            
            rclpy.spin_once(self, timeout_sec=0.1)
            
            # Affichage du statut si disponible
            if self._current_status:
                try:
                    import json
                    status_data = json.loads(self._current_status)
                    progress = status_data.get('progress_percentage', 0)
                    distance = status_data.get('distance_to_target', 0)
                    self.get_logger().info(f"Progrès: {progress:.1f}%, Distance: {distance:.1f}m")
                except:
                    pass  # Ignore les erreurs de parsing
            
            rate.sleep()
        
        if self._mission_completed:
            self.get_logger().info("Navigation terminée avec succès!")
            return True
        else:
            return False


def interactive_mode():
    """Mode interactif pour saisie des coordonnées."""
    print("\n=== Mode interactif GoTo Position ===")
    print("Entrez les coordonnées de destination:")
    
    try:
        x = float(input("X (mètres): "))
        y = float(input("Y (mètres): "))
        z = float(input("Z/Altitude (mètres): "))
        
        wait_input = input("Attendre la fin de navigation? (O/n): ").lower()
        wait = wait_input != 'n'
        
        return x, y, z, wait
        
    except (ValueError, KeyboardInterrupt):
        print("Opération annulée")
        return None


def gps_to_local(lat: float, lon: float, alt: float) -> tuple:
    """
    Convertit des coordonnées GPS en coordonnées locales.
    (Implémentation simplifiée - à remplacer par une vraie conversion)
    """
    # Conversion basique - à améliorer avec une vraie transformation
    # Suppose un point de référence arbitraire
    ref_lat = 45.0
    ref_lon = 2.0
    
    # Approximation pour petites distances
    x = (lon - ref_lon) * 111320.0 * math.cos(math.radians(lat))
    y = (lat - ref_lat) * 110540.0
    z = alt
    
    return x, y, z


def main():
    """Point d'entrée principal."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Navigation vers une position spécifique",
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        epilog="""
Exemples:
    goto_position --x 10.0 --y 20.0 --z 15.0
    goto_position --lat 45.123456 --lon 2.654321 --alt 15.0
    goto_position --interactive
    goto_position --x 10 --y 20 --z 15 --no-wait
        """
    )
    
    # Groupe pour coordonnées locales
    local_group = parser.add_argument_group('Coordonnées locales')
    local_group.add_argument('--x', type=float, help='Coordonnée X (mètres)')
    local_group.add_argument('--y', type=float, help='Coordonnée Y (mètres)')
    local_group.add_argument('--z', type=float, help='Coordonnée Z/Altitude (mètres)')
    
    # Groupe pour coordonnées GPS
    gps_group = parser.add_argument_group('Coordonnées GPS')
    gps_group.add_argument('--lat', type=float, help='Latitude (degrés)')
    gps_group.add_argument('--lon', type=float, help='Longitude (degrés)')
    gps_group.add_argument('--alt', type=float, help='Altitude (mètres)')
    
    # Options
    parser.add_argument('--interactive', '-i', action='store_true',
                       help='Mode interactif')
    parser.add_argument('--no-wait', action='store_true',
                       help='Ne pas attendre la fin de navigation')
    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=float, default=120.0,
                       help='Timeout en secondes (défaut: 120)')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # Validation des arguments
    has_local = all(v is not None for v in [args.x, args.y, args.z])
    has_gps = all(v is not None for v in [args.lat, args.lon, args.alt])
    
    if not args.interactive and not has_local and not has_gps:
        parser.error("Spécifiez soit --x/--y/--z, soit --lat/--lon/--alt, soit --interactive")
    
    if has_local and has_gps:
        parser.error("Spécifiez soit les coordonnées locales, soit GPS, pas les deux")
    
    # Initialisation ROS2
    rclpy.init()
    
    try:
        client = GotoPositionCLI()
        
        # Détermination des coordonnées
        if args.interactive:
            coords = interactive_mode()
            if coords is None:
                return 1
            x, y, z, wait = coords
        elif has_local:
            x, y, z = args.x, args.y, args.z
            wait = not args.no_wait
        elif has_gps:
            import math
            x, y, z = gps_to_local(args.lat, args.lon, args.alt)
            wait = not args.no_wait
            print(f"Coordonnées GPS converties: ({x:.2f}, {y:.2f}, {z:.2f})")
        
        print(f"Navigation vers: X={x:.2f}, Y={y:.2f}, Z={z:.2f}")
        
        # Lancement de la navigation
        success = client.goto_position(x, y, z, wait)
        
        if success:
            print("✅ Navigation réussie!")
            return 0
        else:
            print("❌ Navigation échouée!")
            return 1
    
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterruption par l'utilisateur")
        return 1
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Erreur: {e}")
        return 1
    finally:
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Gestionnaire de géobarrières pour drone de pollinisation.

Implémente la gestion de zones de sécurité avec:
- Zones multiples inclusion/exclusion
- Polygones complexes avec validation géométrique
- Actions automatiques (warning, RTL, emergency stop)
- Contraintes d'altitude min/max
- Mises à jour dynamiques runtime
"""

import time
import math
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import numpy as np
from shapely.geometry import Polygon, Point as ShapelyPoint, MultiPolygon
from shapely.ops import unary_union

from geometry_msgs.msg import Point, PolygonStamped


class GeofenceAction(Enum):
    """Actions automatiques en cas de violation."""
    WARNING = "warning"          # Avertissement seulement
    RTL = "rtl"                 # Retour automatique
    LAND = "land"               # Atterrissage automatique
    HOVER = "hover"             # Maintien position
    EMERGENCY_STOP = "emergency_stop"  # Arrêt d'urgence


class GeofenceType(Enum):
    """Types de géobarrières."""
    INCLUSION = "inclusion"     # Zone autorisée
    EXCLUSION = "exclusion"     # Zone interdite
    ALTITUDE_MIN = "altitude_min"  # Altitude minimale
    ALTITUDE_MAX = "altitude_max"  # Altitude maximale


@dataclass
class GeofenceZone:
    """Définition d'une zone de géobarrière."""
    zone_id: int
    name: str
    zone_type: GeofenceType
    polygon: List[Point]  # Points définissant le polygone
    min_altitude: float = 0.0
    max_altitude: float = 1000.0
    action: GeofenceAction = GeofenceAction.WARNING
    buffer_distance: float = 0.0  # Distance de sécurité
    enabled: bool = True
    priority: int = 1  # 1 = faible, 10 = critique
    timestamp: float = 0.0


@dataclass
class GeofenceViolation:
    """Événement de violation de géobarrière."""
    violation_id: int
    zone_id: int
    zone_name: str
    violation_type: str
    position: Point
    distance_to_boundary: float
    severity: float  # 0.0 à 1.0
    timestamp: float
    action_taken: GeofenceAction
    resolved: bool = False


class GeofenceManager:
    """
    Gestionnaire de géobarrières pour drone de pollinisation.
    
    Surveille en temps réel la position du drone et déclenche
    des actions automatiques en cas de violation des zones définies.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, node, logger):
        """
        Initialise le gestionnaire de géobarrières.
        
        Args:
            node: Nœud ROS2 parent
            logger: Logger pour les messages
        """
        self._node = node
        self._logger = logger
        
        # État du système
        self._active = False
        self._enabled = True
        
        # Base de données des zones
        self._zones: Dict[int, GeofenceZone] = {}
        self._next_zone_id = 1
        
        # Historique des violations
        self._violations: Dict[int, GeofenceViolation] = {}
        self._next_violation_id = 1
        self._max_violation_history = 1000
        
        # Zones compilées pour performance
        self._inclusion_zones: List[Polygon] = []
        self._exclusion_zones: List[Polygon] = []
        self._zones_dirty = True  # Flag pour recompilation
        
        # Configuration
        self._check_frequency = 10.0  # Hz
        self._violation_timeout = 30.0  # secondes
        
        # Statistiques
        self._stats = {
            'total_zones': 0,
            'active_zones': 0,
            'total_violations': 0,
            'current_violations': 0,
            'rtl_triggered': 0,
            'emergency_stops': 0
        }
        
        self._logger.info("Gestionnaire de géobarrières initialisé")
    
    def activate(self):
        """Active le gestionnaire de géobarrières."""
        self._active = True
        self._logger.info("Gestionnaire de géobarrières activé")
    
    def deactivate(self):
        """Désactive le gestionnaire de géobarrières."""
        self._active = False
        self._logger.info("Gestionnaire de géobarrières désactivé")
    
    def add_zone(self, zone: GeofenceZone) -> int:
        """
        Ajoute une zone de géobarrière.
        
        Args:
            zone: Zone à ajouter
            
        Returns:
            ID de la zone ajoutée
        """
        zone.zone_id = self._next_zone_id
        zone.timestamp = time.time()
        self._next_zone_id += 1
        
        # Validation de la zone
        if not self._validate_zone(zone):
            self._logger.error(f"Zone invalide: {zone.name}")
            return -1
        
        self._zones[zone.zone_id] = zone
        self._zones_dirty = True
        
        self._logger.info(f"Zone ajoutée: {zone.name} (ID: {zone.zone_id})")
        self._update_stats()
        
        return zone.zone_id
    
    def remove_zone(self, zone_id: int) -> bool:
        """
        Supprime une zone de géobarrière.
        
        Args:
            zone_id: ID de la zone à supprimer
            
        Returns:
            True si suppression réussie
        """
        if zone_id in self._zones:
            zone_name = self._zones[zone_id].name
            del self._zones[zone_id]
            self._zones_dirty = True
            self._logger.info(f"Zone supprimée: {zone_name} (ID: {zone_id})")
            self._update_stats()
            return True
        
        return False
    
    def update_zone(self, zone_id: int, updated_zone: GeofenceZone) -> bool:
        """
        Met à jour une zone existante.
        
        Args:
            zone_id: ID de la zone à mettre à jour
            updated_zone: Nouvelles données de la zone
            
        Returns:
            True si mise à jour réussie
        """
        if zone_id not in self._zones:
            return False
        
        if not self._validate_zone(updated_zone):
            return False
        
        updated_zone.zone_id = zone_id
        updated_zone.timestamp = time.time()
        self._zones[zone_id] = updated_zone
        self._zones_dirty = True
        
        self._logger.info(f"Zone mise à jour: {updated_zone.name}")
        return True
    
    def enable_zone(self, zone_id: int, enabled: bool = True) -> bool:
        """
        Active/désactive une zone spécifique.
        
        Args:
            zone_id: ID de la zone
            enabled: True pour activer, False pour désactiver
            
        Returns:
            True si opération réussie
        """
        if zone_id not in self._zones:
            return False
        
        self._zones[zone_id].enabled = enabled
        self._zones_dirty = True
        
        status = "activée" if enabled else "désactivée"
        self._logger.info(f"Zone {self._zones[zone_id].name} {status}")
        self._update_stats()
        
        return True
    
    def check_violation(self, position: Point) -> bool:
        """
        Vérifie s'il y a violation de géobarrière à une position.
        
        Args:
            position: Position à vérifier
            
        Returns:
            True s'il y a violation
        """
        if not self._active or not self._enabled:
            return False
        
        try:
            # Recompilation des zones si nécessaire
            if self._zones_dirty:
                self._compile_zones()
            
            violations = self._detect_violations(position)
            
            # Traitement des nouvelles violations
            for violation in violations:
                self._handle_violation(violation)
            
            return len(violations) > 0
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur vérification géobarrière: {e}")
            return False
    
    def get_violations(self, active_only: bool = True) -> List[GeofenceViolation]:
        """
        Retourne la liste des violations.
        
        Args:
            active_only: Si True, retourne seulement les violations non résolues
            
        Returns:
            Liste des violations
        """
        violations = list(self._violations.values())
        
        if active_only:
            violations = [v for v in violations if not v.resolved]
        
        return violations
    
    def resolve_violation(self, violation_id: int) -> bool:
        """
        Marque une violation comme résolue.
        
        Args:
            violation_id: ID de la violation
            
        Returns:
            True si résolution réussie
        """
        if violation_id in self._violations:
            self._violations[violation_id].resolved = True
            self._logger.info(f"Violation {violation_id} résolue")
            return True
        
        return False
    
    def clear_all_zones(self):
        """Supprime toutes les zones de géobarrière."""
        self._zones.clear()
        self._zones_dirty = True
        self._logger.info("Toutes les zones de géobarrière supprimées")
        self._update_stats()
    
    def load_zones_from_config(self, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """
        Charge les zones depuis une configuration.
        
        Args:
            config: Configuration contenant les zones
            
        Returns:
            True si chargement réussi
        """
        try:
            zones_config = config.get('geofence_zones', [])
            
            for zone_config in zones_config:
                zone = self._config_to_zone(zone_config)
                if zone:
                    self.add_zone(zone)
            
            self._logger.info(f"{len(zones_config)} zones chargées depuis la configuration")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur chargement configuration: {e}")
            return False
    
    def _validate_zone(self, zone: GeofenceZone) -> bool:
        """Valide une zone de géobarrière."""
        # Vérification du polygone
        if not zone.polygon or len(zone.polygon) < 3:
            return False
        
        # Vérification des altitudes
        if zone.min_altitude < 0 or zone.max_altitude < zone.min_altitude:
            return False
        
        # Vérification que le polygone est valide
        try:
            coords = [(p.x, p.y) for p in zone.polygon]
            polygon = Polygon(coords)
            if not polygon.is_valid:
                return False
        except Exception:
            return False
        
        return True
    
    def _compile_zones(self):
        """Compile les zones pour optimiser les vérifications."""
        self._inclusion_zones.clear()
        self._exclusion_zones.clear()
        
        for zone in self._zones.values():
            if not zone.enabled:
                continue
            
            try:
                # Conversion en polygone Shapely
                coords = [(p.x, p.y) for p in zone.polygon]
                polygon = Polygon(coords)
                
                # Application du buffer si spécifié
                if zone.buffer_distance > 0:
                    polygon = polygon.buffer(zone.buffer_distance)
                
                # Ajout à la liste appropriée
                if zone.zone_type == GeofenceType.INCLUSION:
                    self._inclusion_zones.append(polygon)
                elif zone.zone_type == GeofenceType.EXCLUSION:
                    self._exclusion_zones.append(polygon)
                    
            except Exception as e:
                self._logger.error(f"Erreur compilation zone {zone.name}: {e}")
        
        self._zones_dirty = False
        self._logger.debug(f"Zones compilées: {len(self._inclusion_zones)} inclusion, {len(self._exclusion_zones)} exclusion")
    
    def _detect_violations(self, position: Point) -> List[GeofenceViolation]:
        """Détecte les violations à une position donnée."""
        violations = []
        point = ShapelyPoint(position.x, position.y)
        
        # Vérification des zones d'inclusion
        if self._inclusion_zones:
            in_allowed_zone = False
            for polygon in self._inclusion_zones:
                if polygon.contains(point):
                    in_allowed_zone = True
                    break
            
            if not in_allowed_zone:
                # Calcul de la distance à la zone la plus proche
                min_distance = float('inf')
                closest_zone_id = -1
                
                for zone_id, zone in self._zones.items():
                    if zone.zone_type == GeofenceType.INCLUSION and zone.enabled:
                        coords = [(p.x, p.y) for p in zone.polygon]
                        polygon = Polygon(coords)
                        distance = point.distance(polygon)
                        
                        if distance < min_distance:
                            min_distance = distance
                            closest_zone_id = zone_id
                
                if closest_zone_id != -1:
                    violation = self._create_violation(
                        zone_id=closest_zone_id,
                        position=position,
                        violation_type="inclusion",
                        distance=min_distance
                    )
                    violations.append(violation)
        
        # Vérification des zones d'exclusion
        for zone_id, zone in self._zones.items():
            if (zone.zone_type == GeofenceType.EXCLUSION and 
                zone.enabled):
                
                coords = [(p.x, p.y) for p in zone.polygon]
                polygon = Polygon(coords)
                
                # Application du buffer
                if zone.buffer_distance > 0:
                    polygon = polygon.buffer(zone.buffer_distance)
                
                if polygon.contains(point):
                    violation = self._create_violation(
                        zone_id=zone_id,
                        position=position,
                        violation_type="exclusion",
                        distance=0.0
                    )
                    violations.append(violation)
        
        # Vérification des contraintes d'altitude
        for zone_id, zone in self._zones.items():
            if not zone.enabled:
                continue
            
            coords = [(p.x, p.y) for p in zone.polygon]
            polygon = Polygon(coords)
            
            if polygon.contains(point):
                # Vérification altitude minimale
                if position.z < zone.min_altitude:
                    violation = self._create_violation(
                        zone_id=zone_id,
                        position=position,
                        violation_type="altitude_min",
                        distance=zone.min_altitude - position.z
                    )
                    violations.append(violation)
                
                # Vérification altitude maximale
                if position.z > zone.max_altitude:
                    violation = self._create_violation(
                        zone_id=zone_id,
                        position=position,
                        violation_type="altitude_max",
                        distance=position.z - zone.max_altitude
                    )
                    violations.append(violation)
        
        return violations
    
    def _create_violation(self, zone_id: int, position: Point, 
                         violation_type: str, distance: float) -> GeofenceViolation:
        """Crée un objet violation."""
        zone = self._zones[zone_id]
        
        # Calcul de la sévérité
        severity = self._calculate_severity(zone, distance, violation_type)
        
        violation = GeofenceViolation(
            violation_id=self._next_violation_id,
            zone_id=zone_id,
            zone_name=zone.name,
            violation_type=violation_type,
            position=position,
            distance_to_boundary=distance,
            severity=severity,
            timestamp=time.time(),
            action_taken=zone.action,
            resolved=False
        )
        
        self._next_violation_id += 1
        return violation
    
    def _calculate_severity(self, zone: GeofenceZone, distance: float, violation_type: str) -> float:
        """Calcule la sévérité d'une violation."""
        # Facteur de base selon le type
        base_severity = {
            'inclusion': 0.8,
            'exclusion': 1.0,
            'altitude_min': 0.6,
            'altitude_max': 0.7
        }.get(violation_type, 0.5)
        
        # Ajustement selon la distance
        if distance > 0:
            # Plus on est loin de la limite, moins c'est sévère
            distance_factor = max(0.1, 1.0 - (distance / 50.0))  # Normalisation par 50m
        else:
            distance_factor = 1.0  # À l'intérieur = sévérité maximale
        
        # Ajustement selon la priorité de la zone
        priority_factor = zone.priority / 10.0
        
        severity = base_severity * distance_factor * priority_factor
        return min(1.0, severity)
    
    def _handle_violation(self, violation: GeofenceViolation):
        """Traite une violation détectée."""
        # Enregistrement de la violation
        self._violations[violation.violation_id] = violation
        
        # Nettoyage de l'historique si nécessaire
        if len(self._violations) > self._max_violation_history:
            oldest_id = min(self._violations.keys())
            del self._violations[oldest_id]
        
        # Log de la violation
        zone = self._zones[violation.zone_id]
        self._logger.warn(
            f"VIOLATION GÉOBARRIÈRE: {zone.name} "
            f"({violation.violation_type}, sévérité: {violation.severity:.2f})"
        )
        
        # Exécution de l'action automatique
        self._execute_action(violation, zone.action)
        
        # Mise à jour des statistiques
        self._stats['total_violations'] += 1
        self._stats['current_violations'] = len([v for v in self._violations.values() if not v.resolved])
    
    def _execute_action(self, violation: GeofenceViolation, action: GeofenceAction):
        """Exécute l'action automatique pour une violation."""
        try:
            if action == GeofenceAction.WARNING:
                self._logger.warn(f"Avertissement géobarrière: {violation.zone_name}")
            
            elif action == GeofenceAction.RTL:
                self._logger.warn(f"RTL déclenché par géobarrière: {violation.zone_name}")
                self._trigger_rtl()
                self._stats['rtl_triggered'] += 1
            
            elif action == GeofenceAction.LAND:
                self._logger.warn(f"Atterrissage déclenché par géobarrière: {violation.zone_name}")
                self._trigger_landing()
            
            elif action == GeofenceAction.HOVER:
                self._logger.warn(f"Mode hover déclenché par géobarrière: {violation.zone_name}")
                self._trigger_hover()
            
            elif action == GeofenceAction.EMERGENCY_STOP:
                self._logger.error(f"ARRÊT D'URGENCE déclenché par géobarrière: {violation.zone_name}")
                self._trigger_emergency_stop()
                self._stats['emergency_stops'] += 1
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur exécution action géobarrière: {e}")
    
    def _trigger_rtl(self):
        """Déclenche un retour automatique (RTL)."""
        # Interface avec le système de navigation
        # (sera connecté au navigation_node)
        pass
    
    def _trigger_landing(self):
        """Déclenche un atterrissage automatique."""
        # Interface avec le système de navigation
        pass
    
    def _trigger_hover(self):
        """Déclenche le mode hover."""
        # Interface avec le système de navigation
        pass
    
    def _trigger_emergency_stop(self):
        """Déclenche un arrêt d'urgence."""
        # Interface avec le système de navigation
        pass
    
    def _config_to_zone(self, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[GeofenceZone]:
        """Convertit une configuration en zone de géobarrière."""
        try:
            # Points du polygone
            polygon_points = []
            for point_config in config.get('polygon', []):
                point = Point()
                point.x = point_config['x']
                point.y = point_config['y']
                point.z = point_config.get('z', 0.0)
                polygon_points.append(point)
            
            # Type de zone
            zone_type_str = config.get('type', 'inclusion')
            zone_type = GeofenceType(zone_type_str)
            
            # Action
            action_str = config.get('action', 'warning')
            action = GeofenceAction(action_str)
            
            zone = GeofenceZone(
                zone_id=0,  # Sera assigné lors de l'ajout
                name=config.get('name', 'Zone sans nom'),
                zone_type=zone_type,
                polygon=polygon_points,
                min_altitude=config.get('min_altitude', 0.0),
                max_altitude=config.get('max_altitude', 1000.0),
                action=action,
                buffer_distance=config.get('buffer_distance', 0.0),
                enabled=config.get('enabled', True),
                priority=config.get('priority', 1)
            )
            
            return zone
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur conversion configuration zone: {e}")
            return None
    
    def _update_stats(self):
        """Met à jour les statistiques."""
        self._stats['total_zones'] = len(self._zones)
        self._stats['active_zones'] = len([z for z in self._zones.values() if z.enabled])
    
    def get_zones(self) -> List[GeofenceZone]:
        """Retourne la liste de toutes les zones."""
        return list(self._zones.values())
    
    def get_zone(self, zone_id: int) -> Optional[GeofenceZone]:
        """Retourne une zone spécifique."""
        return self._zones.get(zone_id)
    
    def get_statistics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Retourne les statistiques du gestionnaire."""
        return self._stats.copy()
    
    def is_position_safe(self, position: Point) -> bool:
        """
        Vérifie si une position est sûre (pas de violation).
        
        Args:
            position: Position à vérifier
            
        Returns:
            True si position sûre
        """
        return not self.check_violation(position)
    
    def get_safe_zone_distance(self, position: Point) -> float:
        """
        Calcule la distance à la zone sûre la plus proche.
        
        Args:
            position: Position de référence
            
        Returns:
            Distance en mètres (0.0 si déjà en zone sûre)
        """
        if self.is_position_safe(position):
            return 0.0
        
        # Calcul de la distance aux zones d'inclusion
        min_distance = float('inf')
        point = ShapelyPoint(position.x, position.y)
        
        for zone in self._zones.values():
            if (zone.zone_type == GeofenceType.INCLUSION and 
                zone.enabled):
                
                coords = [(p.x, p.y) for p in zone.polygon]
                polygon = Polygon(coords)
                distance = point.distance(polygon)
                min_distance = min(min_distance, distance)
        
        return min_distance if min_distance != float('inf') else 0.0

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Optimiseur de chemins pour drone de pollinisation.

Implémente des algorithmes d'optimisation pour:
- Minimisation temps/distance/énergie
- Optimisation multi-objectifs avec algorithmes génétiques
- Contraintes dynamiques (vitesse, accélération, batterie)
- Replanification temps réel
"""

import time
import math
import random
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution

from geometry_msgs.msg import Point


class OptimizationObjective(Enum):
    """Objectifs d'optimisation disponibles."""
    MINIMIZE_TIME = "minimize_time"
    MINIMIZE_DISTANCE = "minimize_distance"
    MINIMIZE_ENERGY = "minimize_energy"
    MULTI_OBJECTIVE = "multi_objective"


@dataclass
class OptimizationConfig:
    """Configuration pour l'optimisation."""
    objective: OptimizationObjective = OptimizationObjective.MINIMIZE_TIME
    max_velocity: float = 10.0
    max_acceleration: float = 5.0
    battery_capacity: float = 100.0  # %
    wind_conditions: Dict[str, float] = None
    weights: Dict[str, float] = None  # Poids pour multi-objectifs
    constraints: Dict[str, Any] = None


@dataclass
class PathOptimizationResult:
    """Résultat d'optimisation de chemin."""
    success: bool
    optimized_path: List[Point]
    original_cost: float
    optimized_cost: float
    improvement_percentage: float
    computation_time: float
    algorithm_used: str
    message: str


class Individual:
    """Individu pour algorithme génétique."""
    def __init__(self, path: List[Point]):
        self.path = path.copy()
        self.fitness = float('inf')
        self.age = 0
    
    def mutate(self, mutation_rate: float, bounds: Dict):
        """Applique une mutation à l'individu."""
        for i in range(len(self.path)):
            if random.random() < mutation_rate:
                # Mutation de position
                point = self.path[i]
                point.x += random.gauss(0, 1.0)  # Écart-type de 1m
                point.y += random.gauss(0, 1.0)
                
                # Respect des contraintes
                if bounds:
                    point.x = max(bounds['min_x'], min(bounds['max_x'], point.x))
                    point.y = max(bounds['min_y'], min(bounds['max_y'], point.y))
    
    def crossover(self, other: 'Individual') -> 'Individual':
        """Croisement avec un autre individu."""
        # Croisement par ordre (OX)
        size = min(len(self.path), len(other.path))
        if size < 2:
            return Individual(self.path)
        
        start = random.randint(0, size - 2)
        end = random.randint(start + 1, size - 1)
        
        child_path = [None] * size
        # Copie du segment du parent 1
        child_path[start:end] = self.path[start:end]
        
        # Complète avec les points du parent 2
        other_points = [p for p in other.path if p not in child_path]
        j = 0
        for i in range(size):
            if child_path[i] is None and j < len(other_points):
                child_path[i] = other_points[j]
                j += 1
        
        # Remplit les trous avec les points originaux
        for i in range(size):
            if child_path[i] is None:
                child_path[i] = self.path[i]
        
        return Individual(child_path)


class PathOptimizer:
    """
    Optimiseur de chemins avancé pour drone de pollinisation.
    
    Utilise différents algorithmes d'optimisation pour minimiser
    le temps de vol, la distance ou la consommation d'énergie.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, node, logger):
        """
        Initialise l'optimiseur de chemins.
        
        Args:
            node: Nœud ROS2 parent
            logger: Logger pour les messages
        """
        self._node = node
        self._logger = logger
        
        # Configuration par défaut
        self._default_config = OptimizationConfig()
        
        # Paramètres algorithme génétique
        self._ga_params = {
            'population_size': 50,
            'generations': 100,
            'mutation_rate': 0.1,
            'crossover_rate': 0.8,
            'elite_size': 5
        }
        
        # Statistiques
        self._optimization_stats = {
            'total_optimizations': 0,
            'successful_optimizations': 0,
            'average_improvement': 0.0,
            'total_computation_time': 0.0
        }
        
        self._logger.info("Optimiseur de chemins initialisé")
    
    def optimize_path(self, original_path: List[Point], 
                     config: OptimizationConfig) -> PathOptimizationResult:
        """
        Optimise un chemin selon la configuration spécifiée.
        
        Args:
            original_path: Chemin original à optimiser
            config: Configuration d'optimisation
            
        Returns:
            Résultat d'optimisation avec chemin optimisé
        """
        start_time = time.time()
        self._optimization_stats['total_optimizations'] += 1
        
        try:
            self._logger.info(f"Optimisation chemin ({config.objective.value})")
            
            # Validation
            if not self._validate_path(original_path):
                return PathOptimizationResult(
                    success=False,
                    optimized_path=original_path,
                    original_cost=0.0,
                    optimized_cost=0.0,
                    improvement_percentage=0.0,
                    computation_time=time.time() - start_time,
                    algorithm_used="none",
                    message="Chemin invalide"
                )
            
            # Calcul du coût original
            original_cost = self._calculate_path_cost(original_path, config)
            
            # Sélection de l'algorithme d'optimisation
            if config.objective == OptimizationObjective.MULTI_OBJECTIVE:
                result = self._genetic_algorithm_optimization(original_path, config)
            elif len(original_path) > 20:  # Gros problèmes
                result = self._genetic_algorithm_optimization(original_path, config)
            else:  # Petits problèmes
                result = self._scipy_optimization(original_path, config)
            
            # Calcul des métriques
            optimized_cost = self._calculate_path_cost(result.optimized_path, config)
            improvement = ((original_cost - optimized_cost) / original_cost) * 100.0
            
            result.original_cost = original_cost
            result.optimized_cost = optimized_cost
            result.improvement_percentage = improvement
            result.computation_time = time.time() - start_time
            
            # Mise à jour des statistiques
            if result.success:
                self._optimization_stats['successful_optimizations'] += 1
                self._update_average_improvement(improvement)
            
            self._optimization_stats['total_computation_time'] += result.computation_time
            
            self._logger.info(f"Optimisation terminée: {improvement:.1f}% d'amélioration")
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur lors de l'optimisation: {e}")
            return PathOptimizationResult(
                success=False,
                optimized_path=original_path,
                original_cost=0.0,
                optimized_cost=0.0,
                improvement_percentage=0.0,
                computation_time=time.time() - start_time,
                algorithm_used="error",
                message=f"Erreur: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    def _validate_path(self, path: List[Point]) -> bool:
        """Valide un chemin."""
        if not path or len(path) < 2:
            return False
        
        # Vérification des points valides
        for point in path:
            if not hasattr(point, 'x') or not hasattr(point, 'y') or not hasattr(point, 'z'):
                return False
        
        return True
    
    def _calculate_path_cost(self, path: List[Point], config: OptimizationConfig) -> float:
        """
        Calcule le coût d'un chemin selon l'objectif d'optimisation.
        
        Args:
            path: Chemin à évaluer
            config: Configuration d'optimisation
            
        Returns:
            Coût total du chemin
        """
        if len(path) < 2:
            return float('inf')
        
        if config.objective == OptimizationObjective.MINIMIZE_DISTANCE:
            return self._calculate_total_distance(path)
        elif config.objective == OptimizationObjective.MINIMIZE_TIME:
            return self._calculate_travel_time(path, config)
        elif config.objective == OptimizationObjective.MINIMIZE_ENERGY:
            return self._calculate_energy_consumption(path, config)
        elif config.objective == OptimizationObjective.MULTI_OBJECTIVE:
            return self._calculate_multi_objective_cost(path, config)
        else:
            return self._calculate_total_distance(path)
    
    def _calculate_total_distance(self, path: List[Point]) -> float:
        """Calcule la distance totale d'un chemin."""
        total_distance = 0.0
        for i in range(1, len(path)):
            dx = path[i].x - path[i-1].x
            dy = path[i].y - path[i-1].y
            dz = path[i].z - path[i-1].z
            distance = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz)
            total_distance += distance
        return total_distance
    
    def _calculate_travel_time(self, path: List[Point], config: OptimizationConfig) -> float:
        """Calcule le temps de parcours estimé."""
        total_time = 0.0
        current_velocity = 0.0
        max_vel = config.max_velocity
        max_accel = config.max_acceleration
        
        for i in range(1, len(path)):
            # Distance du segment
            dx = path[i].x - path[i-1].x
            dy = path[i].y - path[i-1].y
            dz = path[i].z - path[i-1].z
            segment_distance = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz)
            
            if segment_distance == 0:
                continue
            
            # Calcul du temps avec contraintes d'accélération
            # Modèle simplifié: accélération puis vitesse constante
            if current_velocity < max_vel:
                accel_time = min(segment_distance / max_vel, (max_vel - current_velocity) / max_accel)
                accel_distance = current_velocity * accel_time + 0.5 * max_accel * accel_time**2
                current_velocity += max_accel * accel_time
                
                remaining_distance = segment_distance - accel_distance
                const_vel_time = remaining_distance / current_velocity if current_velocity > 0 else 0
                
                total_time += accel_time + const_vel_time
            else:
                total_time += segment_distance / max_vel
        
        return total_time
    
    def _calculate_energy_consumption(self, path: List[Point], config: OptimizationConfig) -> float:
        """Calcule la consommation d'énergie estimée."""
        total_energy = 0.0
        
        # Modèle énergétique simplifié
        base_power = 100.0  # Watts (hovering)
        motion_power_factor = 10.0  # Watts par m/s
        
        for i in range(1, len(path)):
            dx = path[i].x - path[i-1].x
            dy = path[i].y - path[i-1].y
            dz = path[i].z - path[i-1].z
            distance = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz)
            
            # Vitesse moyenne estimée
            avg_velocity = min(config.max_velocity * 0.7, distance)
            
            # Temps du segment
            segment_time = distance / avg_velocity if avg_velocity > 0 else 0
            
            # Consommation = puissance_base + puissance_mouvement
            power = base_power + motion_power_factor * avg_velocity
            
            # Majoration pour montée
            if dz > 0:
                power *= (1 + dz / 10.0)  # +10% par 10m de montée
            
            # Effet du vent
            if config.wind_conditions:
                wind_speed = config.wind_conditions.get('speed', 0)
                power *= (1 + wind_speed / 20.0)  # +5% par 20 km/h de vent
            
            energy = power * segment_time / 3600.0  # Conversion en Wh
            total_energy += energy
        
        return total_energy
    
    def _calculate_multi_objective_cost(self, path: List[Point], config: OptimizationConfig) -> float:
        """Calcule un coût multi-objectifs pondéré."""
        weights = config.weights or {'time': 0.4, 'distance': 0.3, 'energy': 0.3}
        
        # Normalisation des coûts
        time_cost = self._calculate_travel_time(path, config)
        distance_cost = self._calculate_total_distance(path)
        energy_cost = self._calculate_energy_consumption(path, config)
        
        # Normalisation basique (à améliorer avec des valeurs de référence)
        norm_time = time_cost / 100.0  # Normalisation par 100 secondes
        norm_distance = distance_cost / 1000.0  # Normalisation par 1 km
        norm_energy = energy_cost / 500.0  # Normalisation par 500 Wh
        
        total_cost = (weights['time'] * norm_time + 
                     weights['distance'] * norm_distance + 
                     weights['energy'] * norm_energy)
        
        return total_cost
    
    def _scipy_optimization(self, original_path: List[Point], 
                           config: OptimizationConfig) -> PathOptimizationResult:
        """Optimisation avec scipy pour petits problèmes."""
        try:
            # Conversion en array numpy pour scipy
            x0 = []
            for point in original_path[1:-1]:  # Exclut start et end
                x0.extend([point.x, point.y, point.z])
            
            x0 = np.array(x0)
            
            # Fonction objectif
            def objective(x):
                path = self._array_to_path(x, original_path[0], original_path[-1])
                return self._calculate_path_cost(path, config)
            
            # Optimisation
            result = minimize(objective, x0, method='BFGS', 
                            options={'maxiter': 100, 'disp': False})
            
            if result.success:
                optimized_path = self._array_to_path(result.x, original_path[0], original_path[-1])
                return PathOptimizationResult(
                    success=True,
                    optimized_path=optimized_path,
                    original_cost=0.0,  # Sera calculé plus tard
                    optimized_cost=0.0,
                    improvement_percentage=0.0,
                    computation_time=0.0,
                    algorithm_used="scipy_BFGS",
                    message="Optimisation scipy réussie"
                )
            else:
                return PathOptimizationResult(
                    success=False,
                    optimized_path=original_path,
                    original_cost=0.0,
                    optimized_cost=0.0,
                    improvement_percentage=0.0,
                    computation_time=0.0,
                    algorithm_used="scipy_BFGS",
                    message="Optimisation scipy échouée"
                )
                
        except Exception as e:
            return PathOptimizationResult(
                success=False,
                optimized_path=original_path,
                original_cost=0.0,
                optimized_cost=0.0,
                improvement_percentage=0.0,
                computation_time=0.0,
                algorithm_used="scipy_BFGS",
                message=f"Erreur scipy: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    def _genetic_algorithm_optimization(self, original_path: List[Point], 
                                      config: OptimizationConfig) -> PathOptimizationResult:
        """Optimisation par algorithme génétique."""
        try:
            population_size = self._ga_params['population_size']
            generations = self._ga_params['generations']
            mutation_rate = self._ga_params['mutation_rate']
            crossover_rate = self._ga_params['crossover_rate']
            elite_size = self._ga_params['elite_size']
            
            # Initialisation de la population
            population = []
            bounds = self._get_bounds(original_path)
            
            # Individu original
            population.append(Individual(original_path))
            
            # Génération de population aléatoire
            for _ in range(population_size - 1):
                individual = self._create_random_individual(original_path, bounds)
                population.append(individual)
            
            best_individual = None
            best_fitness = float('inf')
            
            # Évolution
            for generation in range(generations):
                # Évaluation
                for individual in population:
                    individual.fitness = self._calculate_path_cost(individual.path, config)
                    individual.age += 1
                
                # Tri par fitness
                population.sort(key=lambda x: x.fitness)
                
                # Mise à jour du meilleur
                if population[0].fitness < best_fitness:
                    best_fitness = population[0].fitness
                    best_individual = Individual(population[0].path)
                
                # Sélection des élites
                new_population = population[:elite_size]
                
                # Génération nouvelle population
                while len(new_population) < population_size:
                    # Sélection des parents
                    parent1 = self._tournament_selection(population, 3)
                    parent2 = self._tournament_selection(population, 3)
                    
                    # Croisement
                    if random.random() < crossover_rate:
                        child = parent1.crossover(parent2)
                    else:
                        child = Individual(parent1.path)
                    
                    # Mutation
                    child.mutate(mutation_rate, bounds)
                    
                    new_population.append(child)
                
                population = new_population
                
                # Log du progrès
                if generation % 20 == 0:
                    self._logger.debug(f"Génération {generation}: meilleur fitness = {best_fitness:.2f}")
            
            return PathOptimizationResult(
                success=True,
                optimized_path=best_individual.path if best_individual else original_path,
                original_cost=0.0,
                optimized_cost=0.0,
                improvement_percentage=0.0,
                computation_time=0.0,
                algorithm_used="genetic_algorithm",
                message=f"AG terminé après {generations} générations"
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            return PathOptimizationResult(
                success=False,
                optimized_path=original_path,
                original_cost=0.0,
                optimized_cost=0.0,
                improvement_percentage=0.0,
                computation_time=0.0,
                algorithm_used="genetic_algorithm",
                message=f"Erreur AG: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    def _array_to_path(self, x: np.ndarray, start_point: Point, end_point: Point) -> List[Point]:
        """Convertit un array numpy en chemin de Points."""
        path = [start_point]
        
        for i in range(0, len(x), 3):
            if i + 2 < len(x):
                point = Point()
                point.x = x[i]
                point.y = x[i + 1]
                point.z = x[i + 2]
                path.append(point)
        
        path.append(end_point)
        return path
    
    def _get_bounds(self, path: List[Point]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Calcule les limites de l'espace de recherche."""
        if not path:
            return {'min_x': -100, 'max_x': 100, 'min_y': -100, 'max_y': 100}
        
        min_x = min(p.x for p in path) - 50
        max_x = max(p.x for p in path) + 50
        min_y = min(p.y for p in path) - 50
        max_y = max(p.y for p in path) + 50
        
        return {'min_x': min_x, 'max_x': max_x, 'min_y': min_y, 'max_y': max_y}
    
    def _create_random_individual(self, reference_path: List[Point], bounds: Dict) -> Individual:
        """Crée un individu aléatoire basé sur un chemin de référence."""
        new_path = []
        
        for point in reference_path:
            new_point = Point()
            # Perturbation aléatoire
            new_point.x = point.x + random.gauss(0, 10.0)  # ±10m
            new_point.y = point.y + random.gauss(0, 10.0)
            new_point.z = point.z + random.gauss(0, 2.0)   # ±2m
            
            # Respect des limites
            new_point.x = max(bounds['min_x'], min(bounds['max_x'], new_point.x))
            new_point.y = max(bounds['min_y'], min(bounds['max_y'], new_point.y))
            new_point.z = max(1.0, min(100.0, new_point.z))  # Altitude raisonnable
            
            new_path.append(new_point)
        
        return Individual(new_path)
    
    def _tournament_selection(self, population: List[Individual], tournament_size: int) -> Individual:
        """Sélection par tournoi."""
        tournament = random.sample(population, min(tournament_size, len(population)))
        return min(tournament, key=lambda x: x.fitness)
    
    def _update_average_improvement(self, improvement: float):
        """Met à jour la moyenne des améliorations."""
        n_success = self._optimization_stats['successful_optimizations']
        old_avg = self._optimization_stats['average_improvement']
        new_avg = ((old_avg * (n_success - 1)) + improvement) / n_success
        self._optimization_stats['average_improvement'] = new_avg
    
    def reoptimize_dynamic(self, current_path: List[Point], 
                          current_position: Point,
                          new_constraints: Dict[str, Any]) -> PathOptimizationResult:
        """
        Reoptimise un chemin en temps réel avec nouvelles contraintes.
        
        Args:
            current_path: Chemin actuel
            current_position: Position actuelle du drone
            new_constraints: Nouvelles contraintes (obstacles, vent, etc.)
            
        Returns:
            Nouveau chemin optimisé
        """
        # Trouve le point le plus proche sur le chemin actuel
        closest_index = self._find_closest_point_index(current_position, current_path)
        
        # Extrait la portion restante du chemin
        remaining_path = current_path[closest_index:]
        
        # Crée une nouvelle configuration avec les contraintes mises à jour
        config = OptimizationConfig()
        config.constraints = new_constraints
        
        # Reoptimise la portion restante
        return self.optimize_path(remaining_path, config)
    
    def _find_closest_point_index(self, position: Point, path: List[Point]) -> int:
        """Trouve l'index du point le plus proche dans un chemin."""
        min_distance = float('inf')
        closest_index = 0
        
        for i, point in enumerate(path):
            dx = point.x - position.x
            dy = point.y - position.y
            dz = point.z - position.z
            distance = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz)
            
            if distance < min_distance:
                min_distance = distance
                closest_index = i
        
        return closest_index
    
    def get_statistics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Retourne les statistiques d'optimisation."""
        return self._optimization_stats.copy()

"""
Drone Navigation Package

évitement d'obstacles et patterns de couverture optimisés.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Contrôleur de position PID avancé pour drone de pollinisation.

Implémente des contrôleurs PID avec:
- Anti-windup protection
- Filtrage dérivé
- Gestion des saturations
- Différents modes de contrôle
- Optimisation pour approche de précision
"""

import time
import math
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import numpy as np

from geometry_msgs.msg import Point, Vector3, Twist
import mavros_msgs.msg as mavros_msgs


class ControlMode(Enum):
    """Modes de contrôle disponibles."""
    POSITION_HOLD = "position_hold"
    GUIDED = "guided"
    FOLLOW_TRAJECTORY = "follow_trajectory"
    PRECISION_APPROACH = "precision_approach"
    EMERGENCY_LAND = "emergency_land"


@dataclass
class PIDGains:
    """Gains PID pour un axe."""
    kp: float = 1.0
    ki: float = 0.1
    kd: float = 0.05
    
    # Limites anti-windup
    max_integral: float = 10.0
    max_output: float = 10.0
    
    # Filtrage dérivé
    derivative_filter_alpha: float = 0.1


@dataclass
class ControlLimits:
    """Limites de contrôle."""
    max_velocity_xy: float = 10.0  # m/s
    max_velocity_z: float = 5.0    # m/s
    max_acceleration: float = 5.0   # m/s²
    max_jerk: float = 10.0         # m/s³
    max_tilt_angle: float = 45.0   # degrés


@dataclass
class ControlState:
    """État interne du contrôleur."""
    position_error: Point
    velocity_error: Vector3
    last_error: Point
    integral_error: Point
    derivative_error: Point
    filtered_derivative: Point
    last_update_time: float
    output_velocity: Vector3
    is_converged: bool


class PIDController:
    """Contrôleur PID pour un axe unique."""
    
    def __init__(self, gains: PIDGains):
        self.gains = gains
        self.reset()
    
    def reset(self):
        """Remet à zéro l'état du contrôleur."""
        self.integral = 0.0
        self.last_error = 0.0
        self.last_derivative = 0.0
        self.last_time = time.time()
    
    def update(self, error: float, dt: float) -> float:
        """
        Met à jour le contrôleur PID.
        
        Args:
            error: Erreur actuelle
            dt: Intervalle de temps
            
        Returns:
            Sortie du contrôleur
        """
        if dt <= 0:
            return 0.0
        
        # Terme proportionnel
        proportional = self.gains.kp * error
        
        # Terme intégral avec anti-windup
        self.integral += error * dt
        self.integral = np.clip(self.integral, -self.gains.max_integral, self.gains.max_integral)
        integral = self.gains.ki * self.integral
        
        # Terme dérivé avec filtrage
        derivative = (error - self.last_error) / dt
        self.last_derivative = (self.gains.derivative_filter_alpha * derivative + 
                               (1.0 - self.gains.derivative_filter_alpha) * self.last_derivative)
        derivative_term = self.gains.kd * self.last_derivative
        
        # Sortie totale
        output = proportional + integral + derivative_term
        output = np.clip(output, -self.gains.max_output, self.gains.max_output)
        
        # Sauvegarde pour prochaine itération
        self.last_error = error
        
        return output
    
    def set_gains(self, gains: PIDGains):
        """Met à jour les gains."""
        self.gains = gains


class PositionController:
    """
    Contrôleur de position PID avancé pour drone de pollinisation.
    
    Fournit différents modes de contrôle optimisés pour diverses phases
    de vol et approche de précision pour pollinisation.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, node, logger):
        """
        Initialise le contrôleur de position.
        
        Args:
            node: Nœud ROS2 parent
            logger: Logger pour les messages
        """
        self._node = node
        self._logger = logger
        
        # État du contrôleur
        self._active = False
        self._current_mode = ControlMode.POSITION_HOLD
        self._last_update_time = time.time()
        
        # Gains PID par défaut (chargés depuis paramètres)
        self._gains_xy = PIDGains()
        self._gains_z = PIDGains()
        self._gains_velocity = PIDGains()
        
        # Contrôleurs PID
        self._pid_x = PIDController(self._gains_xy)
        self._pid_y = PIDController(self._gains_xy)
        self._pid_z = PIDController(self._gains_z)
        
        # Contrôleurs de vitesse
        self._pid_vx = PIDController(self._gains_velocity)
        self._pid_vy = PIDController(self._gains_velocity)
        self._pid_vz = PIDController(self._gains_velocity)
        
        # Limites de contrôle
        self._limits = ControlLimits()
        
        # État interne
        self._control_state = ControlState(
            position_error=Point(),
            velocity_error=Vector3(),
            last_error=Point(),
            integral_error=Point(),
            derivative_error=Point(),
            filtered_derivative=Point(),
            last_update_time=time.time(),
            output_velocity=Vector3(),
            is_converged=False
        )
        
        # Position et vitesse actuelles
        self._current_position = Point()
        self._current_velocity = Vector3()
        self._target_position = Point()
        self._target_velocity = Vector3()
        
        # Configuration des gains depuis paramètres
        self._load_parameters()
        
        # Publisher pour commandes MAVROS
        self._velocity_pub = self._node.create_publisher(
            geometry_msgs.msg.TwistStamped,
            '/mavros/setpoint_velocity/cmd_vel',
            10
        )
        
        # Publisher pour setpoints de position
        self._position_pub = self._node.create_publisher(
            mavros_msgs.PositionTarget,
            '/mavros/setpoint_raw/local',
            10
        )
        
        self._logger.info("Contrôleur de position initialisé")
    
    def _load_parameters(self):
        """Charge les paramètres de configuration."""
        try:
            # Gains position XY
            self._gains_xy.kp = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.p_xy').value
            self._gains_xy.ki = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.i_xy').value
            self._gains_xy.kd = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.d_xy').value
            
            # Gains position Z
            self._gains_z.kp = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.p_z').value
            self._gains_z.ki = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.i_z').value
            self._gains_z.kd = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.d_z').value
            
            # Limites
            self._limits.max_velocity_xy = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.limits.max_velocity_xy').value
            self._limits.max_velocity_z = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.limits.max_velocity_z').value
            self._limits.max_acceleration = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.limits.max_acceleration').value
            self._limits.max_jerk = self._node.get_parameter('position_control.limits.max_jerk').value
            
            # Mise à jour des contrôleurs
            self._pid_x.set_gains(self._gains_xy)
            self._pid_y.set_gains(self._gains_xy)
            self._pid_z.set_gains(self._gains_z)
            
            self._logger.info("Paramètres de contrôle chargés")
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur lors du chargement des paramètres: {e}")
    
    def activate(self):
        """Active le contrôleur."""
        self._active = True
        self._reset_controllers()
        self._logger.info("Contrôleur de position activé")
    
    def deactivate(self):
        """Désactive le contrôleur."""
        self._active = False
        
        # Arrêt des commandes
        self._send_zero_velocity()
        
        self._logger.info("Contrôleur de position désactivé")
    
    def set_mode(self, mode: ControlMode):
        """
        Change le mode de contrôle.
        
        Args:
            mode: Nouveau mode de contrôle
        """
        if mode != self._current_mode:
            self._logger.info(f"Changement mode contrôle: {self._current_mode.value} -> {mode.value}")
            self._current_mode = mode
            self._reset_controllers()
    
    def update(self, current_position: Point, target_position: Point, 
               current_velocity: Optional[Vector3] = None, target_velocity: Optional[Vector3] = None):
        """
        Met à jour le contrôleur de position.
        
        Args:
            current_position: Position actuelle du drone
            target_position: Position cible
            current_velocity: Vitesse actuelle (optionnel)
            target_velocity: Vitesse cible (optionnel)
        """
        if not self._active:
            return
        
        current_time = time.time()
        dt = current_time - self._last_update_time
        self._last_update_time = current_time
        
        if dt <= 0 or dt > 0.1:  # Protection contre les dt aberrants
            return
        
        # Mise à jour de l'état
        self._current_position = current_position
        self._target_position = target_position
        
        if current_velocity:
            self._current_velocity = current_velocity
        if target_velocity:
            self._target_velocity = target_velocity
        
        # Calcul des erreurs de position
        error_x = target_position.x - current_position.x
        error_y = target_position.y - current_position.y
        error_z = target_position.z - current_position.z
        
        # Mise à jour de l'état de contrôle
        self._control_state.position_error.x = error_x
        self._control_state.position_error.y = error_y
        self._control_state.position_error.z = error_z
        
        # Contrôle selon le mode
        if self._current_mode == ControlMode.POSITION_HOLD:
            self._position_hold_control(error_x, error_y, error_z, dt)
        elif self._current_mode == ControlMode.GUIDED:
            self._guided_control(error_x, error_y, error_z, dt)
        elif self._current_mode == ControlMode.FOLLOW_TRAJECTORY:
            self._trajectory_control(error_x, error_y, error_z, dt)
        elif self._current_mode == ControlMode.PRECISION_APPROACH:
            self._precision_approach_control(error_x, error_y, error_z, dt)
        elif self._current_mode == ControlMode.EMERGENCY_LAND:
            self._emergency_land_control(dt)
        
        # Vérification de convergence
        self._check_convergence()
        
        # Envoi des commandes
        self._send_control_commands()
    
    def _position_hold_control(self, error_x: float, error_y: float, error_z: float, dt: float):
        """Contrôle de maintien de position."""
        # PID classique pour maintien de position
        vel_x = self._pid_x.update(error_x, dt)
        vel_y = self._pid_y.update(error_y, dt)
        vel_z = self._pid_z.update(error_z, dt)
        
        # Application des limites
        vel_x = np.clip(vel_x, -self._limits.max_velocity_xy, self._limits.max_velocity_xy)
        vel_y = np.clip(vel_y, -self._limits.max_velocity_xy, self._limits.max_velocity_xy)
        vel_z = np.clip(vel_z, -self._limits.max_velocity_z, self._limits.max_velocity_z)
        
        self._control_state.output_velocity.x = vel_x
        self._control_state.output_velocity.y = vel_y
        self._control_state.output_velocity.z = vel_z
    
    def _guided_control(self, error_x: float, error_y: float, error_z: float, dt: float):
        """Contrôle en mode guidé."""
        # Contrôle plus agressif pour navigation
        gains_boost = 1.2  # Boost des gains pour réactivité
        
        vel_x = self._pid_x.update(error_x, dt) * gains_boost
        vel_y = self._pid_y.update(error_y, dt) * gains_boost
        vel_z = self._pid_z.update(error_z, dt) * gains_boost
        
        # Limitation avec profil de vitesse
        max_vel_xy = self._limits.max_velocity_xy * 0.8  # 80% de la vitesse max
        max_vel_z = self._limits.max_velocity_z * 0.8
        
        vel_x = np.clip(vel_x, -max_vel_xy, max_vel_xy)
        vel_y = np.clip(vel_y, -max_vel_xy, max_vel_xy)
        vel_z = np.clip(vel_z, -max_vel_z, max_vel_z)
        
        self._control_state.output_velocity.x = vel_x
        self._control_state.output_velocity.y = vel_y
        self._control_state.output_velocity.z = vel_z
    
    def _trajectory_control(self, error_x: float, error_y: float, error_z: float, dt: float):
        """Contrôle de suivi de trajectoire."""
        # Contrôle prédictif avec feedforward
        
        # Terme feedforward basé sur la vitesse cible
        ff_x = self._target_velocity.x
        ff_y = self._target_velocity.y
        ff_z = self._target_velocity.z
        
        # Terme feedback PID
        fb_x = self._pid_x.update(error_x, dt)
        fb_y = self._pid_y.update(error_y, dt)
        fb_z = self._pid_z.update(error_z, dt)
        
        # Combinaison feedforward + feedback
        vel_x = ff_x + fb_x
        vel_y = ff_y + fb_y
        vel_z = ff_z + fb_z
        
        # Application des limites
        vel_x = np.clip(vel_x, -self._limits.max_velocity_xy, self._limits.max_velocity_xy)
        vel_y = np.clip(vel_y, -self._limits.max_velocity_xy, self._limits.max_velocity_xy)
        vel_z = np.clip(vel_z, -self._limits.max_velocity_z, self._limits.max_velocity_z)
        
        self._control_state.output_velocity.x = vel_x
        self._control_state.output_velocity.y = vel_y
        self._control_state.output_velocity.z = vel_z
    
    def _precision_approach_control(self, error_x: float, error_y: float, error_z: float, dt: float):
        """Contrôle de précision pour approche pollinisation."""
        # Gains réduits pour approche lente et précise
        precision_factor = 0.5
        
        # Distance à la cible
        distance = math.sqrt(error_x**2 + error_y**2 + error_z**2)
        
        # Réduction dynamique de la vitesse près de la cible
        if distance < 5.0:  # Moins de 5m
            speed_factor = max(0.1, distance / 5.0)
        else:
            speed_factor = 1.0
        
        vel_x = self._pid_x.update(error_x, dt) * precision_factor * speed_factor
        vel_y = self._pid_y.update(error_y, dt) * precision_factor * speed_factor
        vel_z = self._pid_z.update(error_z, dt) * precision_factor * speed_factor
        
        # Limites strictes pour précision
        max_vel_precision = 2.0  # m/s maximum en approche précision
        vel_x = np.clip(vel_x, -max_vel_precision, max_vel_precision)
        vel_y = np.clip(vel_y, -max_vel_precision, max_vel_precision)
        vel_z = np.clip(vel_z, -max_vel_precision, max_vel_precision)
        
        self._control_state.output_velocity.x = vel_x
        self._control_state.output_velocity.y = vel_y
        self._control_state.output_velocity.z = vel_z
    
    def _emergency_land_control(self, dt: float):
        """Contrôle d'atterrissage d'urgence."""
        # Maintien position XY, descente contrôlée
        error_x = self._target_position.x - self._current_position.x
        error_y = self._target_position.y - self._current_position.y
        
        vel_x = self._pid_x.update(error_x, dt) * 0.3  # Gains réduits
        vel_y = self._pid_y.update(error_y, dt) * 0.3
        vel_z = -1.0  # Descente constante à 1 m/s
        
        self._control_state.output_velocity.x = vel_x
        self._control_state.output_velocity.y = vel_y
        self._control_state.output_velocity.z = vel_z
    
    def _check_convergence(self):
        """Vérifie si le contrôleur a convergé."""
        position_tolerance = 0.5  # mètres
        velocity_tolerance = 0.2  # m/s
        
        position_error = math.sqrt(
            self._control_state.position_error.x**2 +
            self._control_state.position_error.y**2 +
            self._control_state.position_error.z**2
        )
        
        velocity_magnitude = math.sqrt(
            self._control_state.output_velocity.x**2 +
            self._control_state.output_velocity.y**2 +
            self._control_state.output_velocity.z**2
        )
        
        self._control_state.is_converged = (
            position_error < position_tolerance and
            velocity_magnitude < velocity_tolerance
        )
    
    def _send_control_commands(self):
        """Envoie les commandes de contrôle via MAVROS."""
        try:
            # Création du message de vitesse
            vel_msg = geometry_msgs.msg.TwistStamped()
            vel_msg.header.stamp = self._node.get_clock().now().to_msg()
            vel_msg.header.frame_id = "base_link"
            
            vel_msg.twist.linear.x = self._control_state.output_velocity.x
            vel_msg.twist.linear.y = self._control_state.output_velocity.y
            vel_msg.twist.linear.z = self._control_state.output_velocity.z
            
            # Publication
            self._velocity_pub.publish(vel_msg)
            
            # Alternative: commande de position raw pour modes spéciaux
            if self._current_mode in [ControlMode.PRECISION_APPROACH, ControlMode.POSITION_HOLD]:
                self._send_position_setpoint()
                
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur envoi commandes: {e}")
    
    def _send_position_setpoint(self):
        """Envoie un setpoint de position pour modes précis."""
        try:
            pos_msg = mavros_msgs.PositionTarget()
            pos_msg.header.stamp = self._node.get_clock().now().to_msg()
            pos_msg.header.frame_id = "map"
            pos_msg.coordinate_frame = mavros_msgs.PositionTarget.FRAME_LOCAL_NED
            
            # Type de contrôle: position + vitesse
            pos_msg.type_mask = (
                mavros_msgs.PositionTarget.IGNORE_AFX |
                mavros_msgs.PositionTarget.IGNORE_AFY |
                mavros_msgs.PositionTarget.IGNORE_AFZ |
                mavros_msgs.PositionTarget.IGNORE_YAW |
                mavros_msgs.PositionTarget.IGNORE_YAW_RATE
            )
            
            # Position cible
            pos_msg.position.x = self._target_position.x
            pos_msg.position.y = self._target_position.y
            pos_msg.position.z = self._target_position.z
            
            # Vitesse cible
            pos_msg.velocity.x = self._control_state.output_velocity.x
            pos_msg.velocity.y = self._control_state.output_velocity.y
            pos_msg.velocity.z = self._control_state.output_velocity.z
            
            self._position_pub.publish(pos_msg)
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur envoi setpoint position: {e}")
    
    def _send_zero_velocity(self):
        """Envoie une commande de vitesse nulle."""
        try:
            vel_msg = geometry_msgs.msg.TwistStamped()
            vel_msg.header.stamp = self._node.get_clock().now().to_msg()
            vel_msg.header.frame_id = "base_link"
            # Toutes les vitesses à zéro par défaut
            self._velocity_pub.publish(vel_msg)
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur envoi vitesse nulle: {e}")
    
    def _reset_controllers(self):
        """Remet à zéro tous les contrôleurs PID."""
        self._pid_x.reset()
        self._pid_y.reset()
        self._pid_z.reset()
        self._pid_vx.reset()
        self._pid_vy.reset()
        self._pid_vz.reset()
        
        # Reset de l'état de contrôle
        self._control_state.position_error = Point()
        self._control_state.velocity_error = Vector3()
        self._control_state.last_error = Point()
        self._control_state.integral_error = Point()
        self._control_state.derivative_error = Point()
        self._control_state.filtered_derivative = Point()
        self._control_state.output_velocity = Vector3()
        self._control_state.is_converged = False
        self._control_state.last_update_time = time.time()
    
    # Getters pour monitoring
    def get_control_state(self) -> ControlState:
        """Retourne l'état actuel du contrôleur."""
        return self._control_state
    
    def get_current_mode(self) -> ControlMode:
        """Retourne le mode de contrôle actuel."""
        return self._current_mode
    
    def is_active(self) -> bool:
        """Retourne si le contrôleur est actif."""
        return self._active
    
    def is_converged(self) -> bool:
        """Retourne si le contrôleur a convergé."""
        return self._control_state.is_converged
    
    def get_gains(self) -> Tuple[PIDGains, PIDGains]:
        """Retourne les gains actuels (XY, Z)."""
        return self._gains_xy, self._gains_z
    
    def set_gains(self, gains_xy: PIDGains, gains_z: PIDGains):
        """
        Met à jour les gains PID.
        
        Args:
            gains_xy: Nouveaux gains pour axes X et Y
            gains_z: Nouveaux gains pour axe Z
        """
        self._gains_xy = gains_xy
        self._gains_z = gains_z
        
        self._pid_x.set_gains(gains_xy)
        self._pid_y.set_gains(gains_xy)
        self._pid_z.set_gains(gains_z)
        
        self._logger.info("Gains PID mis à jour")
    
    def set_limits(self, limits: ControlLimits):
        """
        Met à jour les limites de contrôle.
        
        Args:
            limits: Nouvelles limites
        """
        self._limits = limits
        self._logger.info("Limites de contrôle mises à jour")
    
    def emergency_stop(self):
        """Arrêt d'urgence - passe en mode hover."""
        self._logger.warn("ARRÊT D'URGENCE CONTRÔLEUR!")
        self._target_position = self._current_position  # Hover sur place
        self.set_mode(ControlMode.POSITION_HOLD)
        self._send_zero_velocity()
