#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Patterns de couverture optimisés pour drone de pollinisation.

Implémente différents patterns de navigation:
- Zigzag pour couverture uniforme
- Spiral pour zones circulaires
- Lawn mower pour zones rectangulaires
- Boustrophedon pour efficacité maximale
- Adaptive selon densité de fleurs
"""

import math
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple, Dict, Any, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import numpy as np
from shapely.geometry import Polygon, Point as ShapelyPoint, LineString
from shapely.ops import unary_union

from geometry_msgs.msg import Point, Polygon as GeometryPolygon


class PatternType(Enum):
    """Types de patterns de couverture disponibles."""
    ZIGZAG = "zigzag"
    SPIRAL = "spiral"
    LAWN_MOWER = "lawn_mower"
    BOUSTROPHEDON = "boustrophedon"
    ADAPTIVE = "adaptive"
    CUSTOM = "custom"


@dataclass
class CoverageParameters:
    """Paramètres pour génération de patterns."""
    area_polygon: List[Point]  # Polygone de la zone à couvrir
    altitude: float = 10.0     # Altitude de vol
    overlap_percentage: float = 20.0  # Chevauchement entre passes
    stripe_width: float = 10.0  # Largeur des bandes
    turn_radius: float = 5.0   # Rayon de virage minimum
    approach_angle: float = 0.0  # Angle d'approche (degrés)
    speed: float = 5.0         # Vitesse de vol (m/s)
    flower_density_map: Optional[Dict] = None  # Carte de densité de fleurs


@dataclass
class CoverageResult:
    """Résultat de génération de pattern."""
    success: bool
    waypoints: List[Point]
    pattern_type: PatternType
    total_distance: float
    estimated_time: float
    coverage_percentage: float
    number_of_passes: int
    message: str


class CoveragePatterns:
    """
    Générateur de patterns de couverture pour drone de pollinisation.
    
    Optimisé pour maximiser l'efficacité de pollinisation tout en
    minimisant le temps de vol et la consommation d'énergie.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, node, logger):
        """
        Initialise le générateur de patterns.
        
        Args:
            node: Nœud ROS2 parent
            logger: Logger pour les messages
        """
        self._node = node
        self._logger = logger
        
        # Paramètres par défaut
        self._default_params = CoverageParameters(
            area_polygon=[],
            altitude=10.0,
            overlap_percentage=20.0,
            stripe_width=10.0,
            turn_radius=5.0,
            approach_angle=0.0,
            speed=5.0
        )
        
        # Statistiques
        self._generation_stats = {
            "total_patterns_generated": 0,
            "successful_generations": 0,
            "average_coverage_percentage": 0.0,
            "total_distance_planned": 0.0
        }
        
        self._logger.info("Générateur de patterns de couverture initialisé")
    
    def generate_pattern(self, pattern_type: PatternType, 
                        parameters: CoverageParameters) -> CoverageResult:
        """
        Génère un pattern de couverture selon le type spécifié.
        
        Args:
            pattern_type: Type de pattern à générer
            parameters: Paramètres de génération
            
        Returns:
            Résultat avec waypoints générés ou erreur
        """
        self._generation_stats["total_patterns_generated"] += 1
        
        try:
            self._logger.info(f"Génération pattern {pattern_type.value}")
            
            # Validation des paramètres
            if not self._validate_parameters(parameters):
                return CoverageResult(
                    success=False,
                    waypoints=[],
                    pattern_type=pattern_type,
                    total_distance=0.0,
                    estimated_time=0.0,
                    coverage_percentage=0.0,
                    number_of_passes=0,
                    message="Paramètres invalides"
                )
            
            # Génération selon le type
            if pattern_type == PatternType.ZIGZAG:
                result = self._generate_zigzag_pattern(parameters)
            elif pattern_type == PatternType.SPIRAL:
                result = self._generate_spiral_pattern(parameters)
            elif pattern_type == PatternType.LAWN_MOWER:
                result = self._generate_lawn_mower_pattern(parameters)
            elif pattern_type == PatternType.BOUSTROPHEDON:
                result = self._generate_boustrophedon_pattern(parameters)
            elif pattern_type == PatternType.ADAPTIVE:
                result = self._generate_adaptive_pattern(parameters)
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Type de pattern non supporté: {pattern_type}")
            
            # Mise à jour des statistiques
            if result.success:
                self._generation_stats["successful_generations"] += 1
                self._generation_stats["total_distance_planned"] += result.total_distance
                
                # Mise à jour pourcentage moyen de couverture
                old_avg = self._generation_stats["average_coverage_percentage"]
                n_success = self._generation_stats["successful_generations"]
                new_avg = ((old_avg * (n_success - 1)) + result.coverage_percentage) / n_success
                self._generation_stats["average_coverage_percentage"] = new_avg
            
            self._logger.info(f"Pattern généré: {result.success}, {len(result.waypoints)} waypoints")
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._logger.error(f"Erreur génération pattern: {e}")
            return CoverageResult(
                success=False,
                waypoints=[],
                pattern_type=pattern_type,
                total_distance=0.0,
                estimated_time=0.0,
                coverage_percentage=0.0,
                number_of_passes=0,
                message=f"Erreur: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    def _validate_parameters(self, params: CoverageParameters) -> bool:
        """Valide les paramètres de génération."""
        if not params.area_polygon or len(params.area_polygon) < 3:
            return False
        
        if params.altitude <= 0 or params.stripe_width <= 0:
            return False
        
        if params.overlap_percentage < 0 or params.overlap_percentage > 90:
            return False
        
        return True
    
    def _generate_zigzag_pattern(self, params: CoverageParameters) -> CoverageResult:
        """
        Génère un pattern zigzag pour couverture uniforme.
        
        Pattern optimal pour zones rectangulaires ou polygonales régulières.
        """
        try:
            # Conversion en polygone Shapely
            polygon = self._points_to_shapely_polygon(params.area_polygon)
            bounds = polygon.bounds  # (minx, miny, maxx, maxy)
            
            # Calcul des paramètres
            effective_width = params.stripe_width * (1 - params.overlap_percentage / 100)
            angle_rad = math.radians(params.approach_angle)
            
            waypoints = []
            current_y = bounds[1]  # miny
            pass_number = 0
            direction = 1  # 1 pour droite, -1 pour gauche
            
            while current_y <= bounds[3]:  # maxy
                pass_number += 1
                
                # Points de la passe actuelle
                if direction > 0:
                    start_x, end_x = bounds[0], bounds[2]  # Gauche vers droite
                else:
                    start_x, end_x = bounds[2], bounds[0]  # Droite vers gauche
                
                # Application de la rotation
                start_point = self._rotate_point(start_x, current_y, angle_rad, polygon.centroid)
                end_point = self._rotate_point(end_x, current_y, angle_rad, polygon.centroid)
                
                # Intersection avec le polygone
                line = LineString([start_point, end_point])
                intersections = polygon.intersection(line)
                
                if not intersections.is_empty:
                    if intersections.geom_type == 'LineString':
                        coords = list(intersections.coords)
                        for coord in coords:
                            waypoint = Point()
                            waypoint.x = coord[0]
                            waypoint.y = coord[1]
                            waypoint.z = params.altitude
                            waypoints.append(waypoint)
                    elif intersections.geom_type == 'MultiLineString':
                        for line_seg in intersections.geoms:
                            coords = list(line_seg.coords)
                            for coord in coords:
                                waypoint = Point()
                                waypoint.x = coord[0]
                                waypoint.y = coord[1]
                                waypoint.z = params.altitude
                                waypoints.append(waypoint)
                
                # Passage à la ligne suivante
                current_y += effective_width
                direction *= -1  # Alternance de direction
            
            # Optimisation des virages
            waypoints = self._optimize_turns(waypoints, params.turn_radius)
            
            # Calcul des métriques
            total_distance = self._calculate_path_distance(waypoints)
            estimated_time = total_distance / params.speed
            coverage_percentage = self._estimate_coverage_percentage(waypoints, polygon, params.stripe_width)
            
            return CoverageResult(
                success=True,
                waypoints=waypoints,
                pattern_type=PatternType.ZIGZAG,
                total_distance=total_distance,
                estimated_time=estimated_time,
                coverage_percentage=coverage_percentage,
                number_of_passes=pass_number,
                message=f"Pattern zigzag généré avec {pass_number} passes"
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            return CoverageResult(
                success=False,
                waypoints=[],
                pattern_type=PatternType.ZIGZAG,
                total_distance=0.0,
                estimated_time=0.0,
                coverage_percentage=0.0,
                number_of_passes=0,
                message=f"Erreur zigzag: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    def _generate_spiral_pattern(self, params: CoverageParameters) -> CoverageResult:
        """
        Génère un pattern spiral pour zones circulaires.
        
        Optimal pour jardins circulaires ou zones avec centre d'intérêt.
        """
        try:
            polygon = self._points_to_shapely_polygon(params.area_polygon)
            centroid = polygon.centroid
            
            # Estimation du rayon maximal
            max_radius = 0
            for point in params.area_polygon:
                distance = math.sqrt((point.x - centroid.x)**2 + (point.y - centroid.y)**2)
                max_radius = max(max_radius, distance)
            
            waypoints = []
            current_radius = params.stripe_width / 2
            angle = 0.0
            angular_step = math.radians(10)  # 10 degrés par step
            
            while current_radius < max_radius:
                # Position actuelle
                x = centroid.x + current_radius * math.cos(angle)
                y = centroid.y + current_radius * math.sin(angle)
                point = ShapelyPoint(x, y)
                
                # Vérification si dans le polygone
                if polygon.contains(point):
                    waypoint = Point()
                    waypoint.x = x
                    waypoint.y = y
                    waypoint.z = params.altitude
                    waypoints.append(waypoint)
                
                # Mise à jour angle et rayon
                angle += angular_step
                
                # Expansion du rayon (spiral d'Archimède)
                radius_increment = params.stripe_width * angular_step / (2 * math.pi)
                current_radius += radius_increment
                
                # Protection contre boucle infinie
                if angle > 4 * math.pi * max_radius / params.stripe_width:
                    break
            
            total_distance = self._calculate_path_distance(waypoints)
            estimated_time = total_distance / params.speed
            coverage_percentage = self._estimate_coverage_percentage(waypoints, polygon, params.stripe_width)
            
            return CoverageResult(
                success=True,
                waypoints=waypoints,
                pattern_type=PatternType.SPIRAL,
                total_distance=total_distance,
                estimated_time=estimated_time,
                coverage_percentage=coverage_percentage,
                number_of_passes=1,
                message=f"Pattern spiral généré avec {len(waypoints)} waypoints"
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            return CoverageResult(
                success=False,
                waypoints=[],
                pattern_type=PatternType.SPIRAL,
                total_distance=0.0,
                estimated_time=0.0,
                coverage_percentage=0.0,
                number_of_passes=0,
                message=f"Erreur spiral: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    def _generate_lawn_mower_pattern(self, params: CoverageParameters) -> CoverageResult:
        """
        Génère un pattern lawn mower avec virages optimisés.
        
        Similaire au zigzag mais avec virages arrondis pour efficacité.
        """
        try:
            # Génération base zigzag
            zigzag_result = self._generate_zigzag_pattern(params)
            
            if not zigzag_result.success:
                return zigzag_result
            
            # Optimisation avec virages arrondis
            optimized_waypoints = self._add_curved_turns(
                zigzag_result.waypoints, 
                params.turn_radius
            )
            
            total_distance = self._calculate_path_distance(optimized_waypoints)
            estimated_time = total_distance / params.speed
            
            return CoverageResult(
                success=True,
                waypoints=optimized_waypoints,
                pattern_type=PatternType.LAWN_MOWER,
                total_distance=total_distance,
                estimated_time=estimated_time,
                coverage_percentage=zigzag_result.coverage_percentage,
                number_of_passes=zigzag_result.number_of_passes,
                message=f"Pattern lawn mower avec virages optimisés"
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            return CoverageResult(
                success=False,
                waypoints=[],
                pattern_type=PatternType.LAWN_MOWER,
                total_distance=0.0,
                estimated_time=0.0,
                coverage_percentage=0.0,
                number_of_passes=0,
                message=f"Erreur lawn mower: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    def _generate_boustrophedon_pattern(self, params: CoverageParameters) -> CoverageResult:
        """
        Génère un pattern boustrophedon pour efficacité maximale.
        
        Optimise la décomposition cellulaire pour minimiser les virages.
        """
        try:
            polygon = self._points_to_shapely_polygon(params.area_polygon)
            
            # Décomposition en cellules trapézoïdales
            cells = self._trapezoidal_decomposition(polygon)
            
            waypoints = []
            total_passes = 0
            
            for cell in cells:
                # Pattern zigzag pour chaque cellule
                cell_params = CoverageParameters(
                    area_polygon=self._shapely_polygon_to_points(cell),
                    altitude=params.altitude,
                    overlap_percentage=params.overlap_percentage,
                    stripe_width=params.stripe_width,
                    turn_radius=params.turn_radius,
                    approach_angle=params.approach_angle,
                    speed=params.speed
                )
                
                cell_result = self._generate_zigzag_pattern(cell_params)
                if cell_result.success:
                    waypoints.extend(cell_result.waypoints)
                    total_passes += cell_result.number_of_passes
            
            # Optimisation globale des transitions entre cellules
            waypoints = self._optimize_cell_transitions(waypoints, params.turn_radius)
            
            total_distance = self._calculate_path_distance(waypoints)
            estimated_time = total_distance / params.speed
            coverage_percentage = self._estimate_coverage_percentage(waypoints, polygon, params.stripe_width)
            
            return CoverageResult(
                success=True,
                waypoints=waypoints,
                pattern_type=PatternType.BOUSTROPHEDON,
                total_distance=total_distance,
                estimated_time=estimated_time,
                coverage_percentage=coverage_percentage,
                number_of_passes=total_passes,
                message=f"Pattern boustrophedon avec {len(cells)} cellules"
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            return CoverageResult(
                success=False,
                waypoints=[],
                pattern_type=PatternType.BOUSTROPHEDON,
                total_distance=0.0,
                estimated_time=0.0,
                coverage_percentage=0.0,
                number_of_passes=0,
                message=f"Erreur boustrophedon: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    def _generate_adaptive_pattern(self, params: CoverageParameters) -> CoverageResult:
        """
        Génère un pattern adaptatif selon la densité de fleurs.
        
        Optimise la couverture selon les zones à forte densité florale.
        """
        try:
            if not params.flower_density_map:
                # Fallback vers zigzag si pas de carte de densité
                return self._generate_zigzag_pattern(params)
            
            polygon = self._points_to_shapely_polygon(params.area_polygon)
            density_map = params.flower_density_map
            
            waypoints = []
            
            # Échantillonnage adaptatif basé sur la densité
            bounds = polygon.bounds
            x_step = params.stripe_width / 4  # Résolution plus fine
            y_step = params.stripe_width / 4
            
            current_y = bounds[1]
            while current_y <= bounds[3]:
                current_x = bounds[0]
                direction = 1
                
                while current_x <= bounds[2]:
                    point = ShapelyPoint(current_x, current_y)
                    
                    if polygon.contains(point):
                        # Récupération de la densité locale
                        density = self._get_density_at_point(
                            current_x, current_y, density_map
                        )
                        
                        # Adaptation de l'échantillonnage selon la densité
                        if density > 0.7:  # Zone dense
                            # Ajout de waypoints supplémentaires
                            waypoint = Point()
                            waypoint.x = current_x
                            waypoint.y = current_y
                            waypoint.z = params.altitude
                            waypoints.append(waypoint)
                            
                            # Points supplémentaires autour
                            for offset in [-x_step/2, x_step/2]:
                                extra_point = Point()
                                extra_point.x = current_x + offset
                                extra_point.y = current_y
                                extra_point.z = params.altitude
                                if polygon.contains(ShapelyPoint(extra_point.x, extra_point.y)):
                                    waypoints.append(extra_point)
                        
                        elif density > 0.3:  # Zone moyenne
                            waypoint = Point()
                            waypoint.x = current_x
                            waypoint.y = current_y
                            waypoint.z = params.altitude
                            waypoints.append(waypoint)
                    
                    # Pas adaptatif selon la densité
                    local_density = self._get_density_at_point(current_x, current_y, density_map)
                    adaptive_step = x_step * (2 - local_density)  # Plus dense = pas plus petit
                    current_x += adaptive_step * direction
                
                current_y += y_step
            
            # Optimisation finale
            waypoints = self._optimize_adaptive_path(waypoints, params.turn_radius)
            
            total_distance = self._calculate_path_distance(waypoints)
            estimated_time = total_distance / params.speed
            coverage_percentage = self._estimate_coverage_percentage(waypoints, polygon, params.stripe_width)
            
            return CoverageResult(
                success=True,
                waypoints=waypoints,
                pattern_type=PatternType.ADAPTIVE,
                total_distance=total_distance,
                estimated_time=estimated_time,
                coverage_percentage=coverage_percentage,
                number_of_passes=1,
                message=f"Pattern adaptatif avec {len(waypoints)} waypoints"
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            return CoverageResult(
                success=False,
                waypoints=[],
                pattern_type=PatternType.ADAPTIVE,
                total_distance=0.0,
                estimated_time=0.0,
                coverage_percentage=0.0,
                number_of_passes=0,
                message=f"Erreur adaptatif: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    # Méthodes utilitaires
    def _points_to_shapely_polygon(self, points: List[Point]) -> Polygon:
        """Convertit des Points ROS en Polygon Shapely."""
        coords = [(p.x, p.y) for p in points]
        return Polygon(coords)
    
    def _shapely_polygon_to_points(self, polygon: Polygon) -> List[Point]:
        """Convertit un Polygon Shapely en Points ROS."""
        points = []
        for coord in polygon.exterior.coords[:-1]:  # Exclut le dernier (identique au premier)
            point = Point()
            point.x = coord[0]
            point.y = coord[1]
            point.z = 0.0  # Sera défini par altitude
            points.append(point)
        return points
    
    def _calculate_path_distance(self, waypoints: List[Point]) -> float:
        """Calcule la distance totale d'un chemin."""
        if len(waypoints) < 2:
            return 0.0
        
        total_distance = 0.0
        for i in range(1, len(waypoints)):
            dx = waypoints[i].x - waypoints[i-1].x
            dy = waypoints[i].y - waypoints[i-1].y
            dz = waypoints[i].z - waypoints[i-1].z
            distance = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz)
            total_distance += distance
        
        return total_distance
    
    def _estimate_coverage_percentage(self, waypoints: List[Point], 
                                    polygon: Polygon, stripe_width: float) -> float:
        """Estime le pourcentage de couverture."""
        if not waypoints:
            return 0.0
        
        # Approximation basée sur la distance totale et la largeur
        total_distance = self._calculate_path_distance(waypoints)
        covered_area = total_distance * stripe_width
        polygon_area = polygon.area
        
        coverage = min(100.0, (covered_area / polygon_area) * 100.0)
        return coverage
    
    def _optimize_turns(self, waypoints: List[Point], turn_radius: float) -> List[Point]:
        """Optimise les virages en bout de ligne."""
        if len(waypoints) < 3:
            return waypoints
        
        optimized = [waypoints[0]]
        
        for i in range(1, len(waypoints) - 1):
            current = waypoints[i]
            
            # Détection des virages (changement de direction significatif)
            if self._is_turn_point(waypoints[i-1], current, waypoints[i+1]):
                # Ajout de points intermédiaires pour virage arrondi
                turn_points = self._generate_turn_waypoints(
                    waypoints[i-1], current, waypoints[i+1], turn_radius
                )
                optimized.extend(turn_points)
            else:
                optimized.append(current)
        
        optimized.append(waypoints[-1])
        return optimized
    
    def _is_turn_point(self, prev_point: Point, current: Point, next_point: Point) -> bool:
        """Détermine si un point est un virage."""
        # Calcul de l'angle entre les segments
        v1 = (current.x - prev_point.x, current.y - prev_point.y)
        v2 = (next_point.x - current.x, next_point.y - current.y)
        
        # Produit scalaire normalisé
        mag1 = math.sqrt(v1[0]**2 + v1[1]**2)
        mag2 = math.sqrt(v2[0]**2 + v2[1]**2)
        
        if mag1 == 0 or mag2 == 0:
            return False
        
        cos_angle = (v1[0]*v2[0] + v1[1]*v2[1]) / (mag1 * mag2)
        cos_angle = max(-1, min(1, cos_angle))  # Clamping
        angle = math.acos(cos_angle)
        
        return angle > math.radians(30)  # Virage si > 30°
    
    def _generate_turn_waypoints(self, prev_point: Point, turn_point: Point, 
                               next_point: Point, radius: float) -> List[Point]:
        """Génère des waypoints pour un virage arrondi."""
        # Implémentation simplifiée - arc de cercle
        waypoints = []
        
        # Centre du cercle de virage
        # (Calcul géométrique complexe simplifié ici)
        
        # Pour cette implémentation, on utilise une approximation linéaire
        n_points = 5
        for i in range(n_points):
            t = i / (n_points - 1)
            
            # Interpolation quadratique
            point = Point()
            point.x = (1-t)**2 * prev_point.x + 2*(1-t)*t * turn_point.x + t**2 * next_point.x
            point.y = (1-t)**2 * prev_point.y + 2*(1-t)*t * turn_point.y + t**2 * next_point.y
            point.z = turn_point.z
            
            waypoints.append(point)
        
        return waypoints
    
    def _add_curved_turns(self, waypoints: List[Point], radius: float) -> List[Point]:
        """Ajoute des virages courbes au pattern."""
        return self._optimize_turns(waypoints, radius)
    
    def _trapezoidal_decomposition(self, polygon: Polygon) -> List[Polygon]:
        """Décomposition trapézoïdale simplifiée."""
        # Implémentation basique - division verticale
        bounds = polygon.bounds
        width = bounds[2] - bounds[0]
        n_cells = max(2, int(width / 20))  # Cellules de ~20m
        
        cells = []
        cell_width = width / n_cells
        
        for i in range(n_cells):
            left = bounds[0] + i * cell_width
            right = bounds[0] + (i + 1) * cell_width
            
            # Rectangle de découpe
            cell_rect = Polygon([
                (left, bounds[1]),
                (right, bounds[1]),
                (right, bounds[3]),
                (left, bounds[3])
            ])
            
            # Intersection avec le polygone original
            intersection = polygon.intersection(cell_rect)
            
            if not intersection.is_empty and intersection.geom_type == 'Polygon':
                cells.append(intersection)
        
        return cells
    
    def _optimize_cell_transitions(self, waypoints: List[Point], radius: float) -> List[Point]:
        """Optimise les transitions entre cellules."""
        # Implémentation basique - lissage des transitions
        return self._optimize_turns(waypoints, radius)
    
    def _get_density_at_point(self, x: float, y: float, density_map: Dict) -> float:
        """Récupère la densité de fleurs à un point."""
        # Implémentation basique - interpolation depuis une grille
        # Suppose que density_map contient une grille de densités
        
        # Valeur par défaut si pas de données
        if 'grid' not in density_map:
            return 0.5
        
        grid = density_map['grid']
        bounds = density_map.get('bounds', [-100, -100, 100, 100])
        resolution = density_map.get('resolution', 1.0)
        
        # Conversion en indices de grille
        grid_x = int((x - bounds[0]) / resolution)
        grid_y = int((y - bounds[1]) / resolution)
        
        # Vérification des limites
        if (0 <= grid_x < len(grid[0]) and 0 <= grid_y < len(grid)):
            return grid[grid_y][grid_x]
        
        return 0.5  # Densité moyenne par défaut
    
    def _optimize_adaptive_path(self, waypoints: List[Point], radius: float) -> List[Point]:
        """Optimise un chemin adaptatif."""
        # Suppression des points trop proches
        if not waypoints:
            return waypoints
        
        optimized = [waypoints[0]]
        min_distance = radius / 2
        
        for i in range(1, len(waypoints)):
            prev = optimized[-1]
            current = waypoints[i]
            
            distance = math.sqrt(
                (current.x - prev.x)**2 + 
                (current.y - prev.y)**2
            )
            
            if distance >= min_distance:
                optimized.append(current)
        
        return optimized
    
    def _rotate_point(self, x: float, y: float, angle: float, center) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """Applique une rotation à un point."""
        cos_a = math.cos(angle)
        sin_a = math.sin(angle)
        
        # Translation vers origine
        x_centered = x - center.x
        y_centered = y - center.y
        
        # Rotation
        x_rot = x_centered * cos_a - y_centered * sin_a
        y_rot = x_centered * sin_a + y_centered * cos_a
        
        # Translation retour
        return (x_rot + center.x, y_rot + center.y)
    
    def get_statistics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Retourne les statistiques de génération."""
        return self._generation_stats.copy()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Nœud principal de navigation pour drone autonome de pollinisation.

Ce nœud fournit des capacités complètes de navigation incluant:
- Planification de trajectoires avancée
- Contrôle de position PID temps réel
- Évitement d'obstacles dynamique
- Patterns de couverture optimisés
- Gestion de géobarrières
- Intégration MAVROS complète
"""

import time
import threading
import json
from typing import Optional, List, Dict, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from enum import Enum

import rclpy
from rclpy.lifecycle import LifecycleNode, LifecycleState, TransitionCallbackReturn
from rclpy.lifecycle import LifecycleNodeMixin
from rclpy.callback_groups import ReentrantCallbackGroup
from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, QoSReliabilityPolicy, QoSHistoryPolicy
from rclpy.parameter import Parameter

# Messages ROS2
from std_msgs.msg import String, Bool, Header
from geometry_msgs.msg import Point, PointStamped, PoseStamped, Twist, Vector3
from nav_msgs.msg import Path
from sensor_msgs.msg import NavSatFix, Imu
from diagnostic_msgs.msg import DiagnosticArray, DiagnosticStatus, KeyValue

# Services ROS2
from std_srvs.srv import Empty, SetBool, Trigger
from nav_msgs.srv import GetPlan

# MAVROS
import mavros_msgs.msg as mavros_msgs
import mavros_msgs.srv as mavros_srvs

# Modules locaux
from .trajectory_planner import TrajectoryPlanner, TrajectoryRequest, TrajectoryResult
from .position_controller import PositionController, ControlMode
from .path_optimizer import PathOptimizer, OptimizationConfig
from .obstacle_avoidance import ObstacleAvoidance, AvoidanceStrategy
from .geofence_manager import GeofenceManager, GeofenceZone, GeofenceAction
from .coverage_patterns import CoveragePatterns, PatternType

# 
from rclpy.executors import MultiThreadedExecutor



class NavigationState(Enum):
    """États de navigation."""
    IDLE = "idle"
    PLANNING = "planning"
    NAVIGATING = "navigating"
    HOLDING = "holding"
    LANDING = "landing"
    RTL = "rtl"
    EMERGENCY = "emergency"


@dataclass
class NavigationStatus:
    """État détaillé de navigation."""
    state: NavigationState
    current_position: Point
    target_position: Optional[Point]
    distance_to_target: float
    progress_percentage: float
    active_waypoint: int
    total_waypoints: int
    is_armed: bool
    flight_mode: str
    battery_percentage: float
    obstacles_detected: bool
    geofence_violation: bool
    last_update: float
    
    def to_json(self) -> str:
        """Convertit en JSON pour publication."""
        data = asdict(self)
        data['state'] = self.state.value
        return json.dumps(data, indent=2)


class NavigationNode(LifecycleNode):
    """
    Nœud principal de navigation avec lifecycle management complet.
    
    Fournit tous les services et capacités nécessaires pour la navigation
    autonome d'un drone de pollinisation avec intégration MAVROS.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__('navigation_node')
        
        # Threading sécurisé
        self._callback_group = ReentrantCallbackGroup()
        
        # État interne
        self._navigation_state = NavigationState.IDLE
        self._current_position = Point()
        self._target_position = None
        self._waypoints = []
        self._current_waypoint_index = 0
        self._home_position = None
        
        # Composants principaux (initialisés dans configure)
        self._trajectory_planner: Optional[TrajectoryPlanner] = None
        self._position_controller: Optional[PositionController] = None
        self._path_optimizer: Optional[PathOptimizer] = None
        self._obstacle_avoidance: Optional[ObstacleAvoidance] = None
        self._geofence_manager: Optional[GeofenceManager] = None
        self._coverage_patterns: Optional[CoveragePatterns] = None
        
        # Lock pour thread safety
        self._state_lock = threading.RLock()
        
        # QoS profiles
        self._reliable_qos = QoSProfile(
            reliability=QoSReliabilityPolicy.RELIABLE,
            history=QoSHistoryPolicy.KEEP_LAST,
            depth=10
        )
        
        self._best_effort_qos = QoSProfile(
            reliability=QoSReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT,
            history=QoSHistoryPolicy.KEEP_LAST,
            depth=1
        )
        
        # Timers (initialisés dans activate)
        self._status_timer = None
        self._control_timer = None
        self._safety_timer = None
        
        self.get_logger().info("Navigation node créé avec succès")

    def on_configure(self, state: LifecycleState) -> TransitionCallbackReturn:
        """Configuration du nœud - initialisation des composants."""
        self.get_logger().info("Configuration du nœud de navigation...")
        
        try:
            # Déclaration des paramètres
            self._declare_parameters()
            
            # Initialisation des composants principaux
            self._initialize_components()
            
            # Création des services
            self._create_services()
            
            # Création des publishers
            self._create_publishers()
            
            # Création des subscribers
            self._create_subscribers()
            
            self.get_logger().info("Nœud de navigation configuré avec succès")
            return TransitionCallbackReturn.SUCCESS
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur lors de la configuration: {e}")
            return TransitionCallbackReturn.FAILURE

    def on_activate(self, state: LifecycleState) -> TransitionCallbackReturn:
        """Activation du nœud - démarrage des timers et services."""
        self.get_logger().info("Activation du nœud de navigation...")
        
        try:
            # Activation des publishers
            self._status_pub.on_activate(state)
            self._trajectory_pub.on_activate(state)
            self._waypoint_reached_pub.on_activate(state)
            self._path_progress_pub.on_activate(state)
            self._obstacles_pub.on_activate(state)
            self._diagnostics_pub.on_activate(state)
            
            # Démarrage des timers
            self._start_timers()
            
            # Activation des composants
            self._position_controller.activate()
            self._obstacle_avoidance.activate()
            self._geofence_manager.activate()
            
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.IDLE
            
            self.get_logger().info("Nœud de navigation activé avec succès")
            return TransitionCallbackReturn.SUCCESS
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur lors de l'activation: {e}")
            return TransitionCallbackReturn.FAILURE

    def on_deactivate(self, state: LifecycleState) -> TransitionCallbackReturn:
        """Désactivation du nœud."""
        self.get_logger().info("Désactivation du nœud de navigation...")
        
        try:
            # Arrêt des timers
            self._stop_timers()
            
            # Arrêt d'urgence de la navigation
            self._emergency_stop()
            
            # Désactivation des composants
            if self._position_controller:
                self._position_controller.deactivate()
            if self._obstacle_avoidance:
                self._obstacle_avoidance.deactivate()
            if self._geofence_manager:
                self._geofence_manager.deactivate()
            
            # Désactivation des publishers
            self._status_pub.on_deactivate(state)
            self._trajectory_pub.on_deactivate(state)
            self._waypoint_reached_pub.on_deactivate(state)
            self._path_progress_pub.on_deactivate(state)
            self._obstacles_pub.on_deactivate(state)
            self._diagnostics_pub.on_deactivate(state)
            
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.IDLE
            
            self.get_logger().info("Nœud de navigation désactivé")
            return TransitionCallbackReturn.SUCCESS
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur lors de la désactivation: {e}")
            return TransitionCallbackReturn.FAILURE

    def on_cleanup(self, state: LifecycleState) -> TransitionCallbackReturn:
        """Nettoyage des ressources."""
        self.get_logger().info("Nettoyage du nœud de navigation...")
        
        try:
            # Destruction des timers
            if self._status_timer:
                self._status_timer.destroy()
            if self._control_timer:
                self._control_timer.destroy()
            if self._safety_timer:
                self._safety_timer.destroy()
                
            # Nettoyage des composants
            self._trajectory_planner = None
            self._position_controller = None
            self._path_optimizer = None
            self._obstacle_avoidance = None
            self._geofence_manager = None
            self._coverage_patterns = None
            
            self.get_logger().info("Nœud de navigation nettoyé")
            return TransitionCallbackReturn.SUCCESS
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur lors du nettoyage: {e}")
            return TransitionCallbackReturn.FAILURE

    def on_shutdown(self, state: LifecycleState) -> TransitionCallbackReturn:
        """Arrêt complet du nœud."""
        self.get_logger().info("Arrêt du nœud de navigation...")
        
        # Arrêt d'urgence
        self._emergency_stop()
        
        return TransitionCallbackReturn.SUCCESS

    def _declare_parameters(self):
        """Déclaration de tous les paramètres du nœud."""
        # Paramètres de contrôle de position
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.p_xy', 1.0)
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.i_xy', 0.1)
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.d_xy', 0.05)
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.p_z', 1.5)
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.i_z', 0.2)
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.pid_gains.position.d_z', 0.1)
        
        # Limites de vitesse et accélération
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.limits.max_velocity_xy', 10.0)
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.limits.max_velocity_z', 5.0)
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.limits.max_acceleration', 5.0)
        self.declare_parameter('position_control.limits.max_jerk', 10.0)
        
        # Paramètres de planification de trajectoire
        self.declare_parameter('trajectory.planning.lookahead_distance', 10.0)
        self.declare_parameter('trajectory.planning.path_resolution', 1.0)
        self.declare_parameter('trajectory.planning.smoothing_factor', 0.8)
        self.declare_parameter('trajectory.planning.turn_radius_min', 2.0)
        
        # Paramètres d'évitement d'obstacles
        self.declare_parameter('obstacles.detection.enabled', True)
        self.declare_parameter('obstacles.detection.safety_margin', 2.0)
        self.declare_parameter('obstacles.detection.max_detection_range', 50.0)
        
        # Paramètres de géobarrière
        self.declare_parameter('geofence.enabled', True)
        self.declare_parameter('geofence.action', 'RTL')
        self.declare_parameter('geofence.buffer_distance', 5.0)
        self.declare_parameter('geofence.altitude_limits.min', 5.0)
        self.declare_parameter('geofence.altitude_limits.max', 120.0)
        
        # Paramètres de sécurité
        self.declare_parameter('safety.emergency.rtl_altitude', 50.0)
        self.declare_parameter('safety.emergency.emergency_descent_rate', 2.0)
        self.declare_parameter('safety.emergency.communication_timeout', 10.0)
        
        # Fréquences de mise à jour
        self.declare_parameter('performance.update_rates.position_control', 50.0)
        self.declare_parameter('performance.update_rates.trajectory_planning', 10.0)
        self.declare_parameter('performance.update_rates.status_publishing', 5.0)

    def _initialize_components(self):
        """Initialisation des composants principaux."""
        # Planificateur de trajectoires
        self._trajectory_planner = TrajectoryPlanner(
            node=self,
            logger=self.get_logger()
        )
        
        # Contrôleur de position
        self._position_controller = PositionController(
            node=self,
            logger=self.get_logger()
        )
        
        # Optimiseur de chemins
        self._path_optimizer = PathOptimizer(
            node=self,
            logger=self.get_logger()
        )
        
        # Évitement d'obstacles
        self._obstacle_avoidance = ObstacleAvoidance(
            node=self,
            logger=self.get_logger()
        )
        
        # Gestionnaire de géobarrières
        self._geofence_manager = GeofenceManager(
            node=self,
            logger=self.get_logger()
        )
        
        # Patterns de couverture
        self._coverage_patterns = CoveragePatterns(
            node=self,
            logger=self.get_logger()
        )

    def _create_services(self):
        """Création de tous les services ROS2."""
        # Services de navigation
        self._goto_position_srv = self.create_service(
            geometry_msgs.srv.GetPlan,
            '/drone_nav/goto_position',
            self._goto_position_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._goto_local_srv = self.create_service(
            geometry_msgs.srv.GetPlan,
            '/drone_nav/goto_local',
            self._goto_local_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._plan_path_srv = self.create_service(
            geometry_msgs.srv.GetPlan,
            '/drone_nav/plan_path',
            self._plan_path_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._set_waypoints_srv = self.create_service(
            std_srvs.srv.SetBool,
            '/drone_nav/set_waypoints',
            self._set_waypoints_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._start_mission_srv = self.create_service(
            std_srvs.srv.Trigger,
            '/drone_nav/start_mission',
            self._start_mission_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._pause_mission_srv = self.create_service(
            std_srvs.srv.Trigger,
            '/drone_nav/pause_mission',
            self._pause_mission_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._resume_mission_srv = self.create_service(
            std_srvs.srv.Trigger,
            '/drone_nav/resume_mission',
            self._resume_mission_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._emergency_rtl_srv = self.create_service(
            std_srvs.srv.Trigger,
            '/drone_nav/emergency_rtl',
            self._emergency_rtl_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._set_geofence_srv = self.create_service(
            std_srvs.srv.SetBool,
            '/drone_nav/set_geofence',
            self._set_geofence_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._clear_geofence_srv = self.create_service(
            std_srvs.srv.Trigger,
            '/drone_nav/clear_geofence',
            self._clear_geofence_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        self._set_home_srv = self.create_service(
            geometry_msgs.srv.GetPlan,
            '/drone_nav/set_home',
            self._set_home_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )

    def _create_publishers(self):
        """Création des publishers ROS2."""
        # Publisher pour l'état de navigation (JSON détaillé)
        self._status_pub = self.create_lifecycle_publisher(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/status',
            qos_profile=self._reliable_qos
        )
        
        # Publisher pour la trajectoire planifiée
        self._trajectory_pub = self.create_lifecycle_publisher(
            Path,
            '/drone_nav/trajectory',
            qos_profile=self._reliable_qos
        )
        
        # Publisher pour les événements waypoints
        self._waypoint_reached_pub = self.create_lifecycle_publisher(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/waypoint_reached',
            qos_profile=self._reliable_qos
        )
        
        # Publisher pour le progrès du chemin
        self._path_progress_pub = self.create_lifecycle_publisher(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/path_progress',
            qos_profile=self._best_effort_qos
        )
        
        # Publisher pour les obstacles détectés
        self._obstacles_pub = self.create_lifecycle_publisher(
            String,
            '/drone_nav/obstacles_detected',
            qos_profile=self._reliable_qos
        )
        
        # Publisher pour les diagnostics
        self._diagnostics_pub = self.create_lifecycle_publisher(
            DiagnosticArray,
            '/diagnostics',
            qos_profile=self._reliable_qos
        )

    def _create_subscribers(self):
        """Création des subscribers ROS2."""
        # Abonnement à la position MAVROS
        self._position_sub = self.create_subscription(
            PoseStamped,
            '/mavros/local_position/pose',
            self._position_callback,
            qos_profile=self._best_effort_qos,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        # Abonnement à l'état MAVROS
        self._state_sub = self.create_subscription(
            mavros_msgs.State,
            '/mavros/state',
            self._state_callback,
            qos_profile=self._reliable_qos,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        # Abonnement à la batterie
        self._battery_sub = self.create_subscription(
            sensor_msgs.msg.BatteryState,
            '/mavros/battery',
            self._battery_callback,
            qos_profile=self._best_effort_qos,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )

    def _start_timers(self):
        """Démarrage des timers principaux."""
        # Timer pour publication du statut (5 Hz)
        status_rate = self.get_parameter('performance.update_rates.status_publishing').value
        self._status_timer = self.create_timer(
            1.0 / status_rate,
            self._status_timer_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        # Timer pour contrôle de position (50 Hz)
        control_rate = self.get_parameter('performance.update_rates.position_control').value
        self._control_timer = self.create_timer(
            1.0 / control_rate,
            self._control_timer_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )
        
        # Timer pour vérifications de sécurité (1 Hz)
        self._safety_timer = self.create_timer(
            1.0,
            self._safety_timer_callback,
            callback_group=self._callback_group
        )

    def _stop_timers(self):
        """Arrêt de tous les timers."""
        if self._status_timer:
            self._status_timer.cancel()
        if self._control_timer:
            self._control_timer.cancel()
        if self._safety_timer:
            self._safety_timer.cancel()

    def _status_timer_callback(self):
        """Publication périodique du statut de navigation."""
        with self._state_lock:
            status = NavigationStatus(
                state=self._navigation_state,
                current_position=self._current_position,
                target_position=self._target_position,
                distance_to_target=self._calculate_distance_to_target(),
                progress_percentage=self._calculate_progress_percentage(),
                active_waypoint=self._current_waypoint_index,
                total_waypoints=len(self._waypoints),
                is_armed=getattr(self, '_is_armed', False),
                flight_mode=getattr(self, '_flight_mode', 'UNKNOWN'),
                battery_percentage=getattr(self, '_battery_percentage', 0.0),
                obstacles_detected=self._obstacle_avoidance.has_obstacles() if self._obstacle_avoidance else False,
                geofence_violation=self._geofence_manager.check_violation(self._current_position) if self._geofence_manager else False,
                last_update=time.time()
            )
            
            # Publication du statut JSON
            status_msg = String()
            status_msg.data = status.to_json()
            self._status_pub.publish(status_msg)

    def _control_timer_callback(self):
        """Boucle principale de contrôle de navigation."""
        if self._navigation_state == NavigationState.NAVIGATING and self._target_position:
            try:
                # Mise à jour du contrôleur de position
                if self._position_controller:
                    self._position_controller.update(
                        current_position=self._current_position,
                        target_position=self._target_position
                    )
                
                # Vérification si waypoint atteint
                if self._is_waypoint_reached():
                    self._handle_waypoint_reached()
                    
            except Exception as e:
                self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur dans la boucle de contrôle: {e}")

    def _safety_timer_callback(self):
        """Vérifications de sécurité périodiques."""
        try:
            # Vérification géobarrière
            if self._geofence_manager and self._geofence_manager.check_violation(self._current_position):
                self.get_logger().warn("Violation de géobarrière détectée!")
                self._handle_geofence_violation()
            
            # Vérification obstacles
            if self._obstacle_avoidance and self._obstacle_avoidance.has_obstacles():
                self.get_logger().warn("Obstacles détectés!")
                self._handle_obstacle_detection()
            
            # Publication des diagnostics
            self._publish_diagnostics()
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Erreur dans les vérifications de sécurité: {e}")

    def _position_callback(self, msg: PoseStamped):
        """Callback pour mise à jour de position."""
        with self._state_lock:
            self._current_position.x = msg.pose.position.x
            self._current_position.y = msg.pose.position.y
            self._current_position.z = msg.pose.position.z

    def _state_callback(self, msg: mavros_msgs.State):
        """Callback pour état MAVROS."""
        with self._state_lock:
            self._is_armed = msg.armed
            self._flight_mode = msg.mode

    def _battery_callback(self, msg):
        """Callback pour niveau de batterie."""
        with self._state_lock:
            self._battery_percentage = msg.percentage * 100.0

    # Service callbacks (implémentations basiques)
    def _goto_position_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour aller à une position."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service goto_position appelé")
        response.plan.header.stamp = self.get_clock().now().to_msg()
        response.plan.header.frame_id = "map"
        return response

    def _goto_local_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour navigation locale."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service goto_local appelé")
        return response

    def _plan_path_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour planification de chemin."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service plan_path appelé")
        return response

    def _set_waypoints_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour définir des waypoints."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service set_waypoints appelé")
        response.success = True
        response.message = "Waypoints définis avec succès"
        return response

    def _start_mission_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour démarrer une mission."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service start_mission appelé")
        with self._state_lock:
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.NAVIGATING
        response.success = True
        response.message = "Mission démarrée"
        return response

    def _pause_mission_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour pauser une mission."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service pause_mission appelé")
        with self._state_lock:
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.HOLDING
        response.success = True
        response.message = "Mission en pause"
        return response

    def _resume_mission_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour reprendre une mission."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service resume_mission appelé")
        with self._state_lock:
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.NAVIGATING
        response.success = True
        response.message = "Mission reprise"
        return response

    def _emergency_rtl_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour retour d'urgence."""
        self.get_logger().warn("RTL d'urgence activé!")
        with self._state_lock:
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.RTL
        response.success = True
        response.message = "RTL d'urgence activé"
        return response

    def _set_geofence_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour configurer géobarrière."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service set_geofence appelé")
        response.success = True
        response.message = "Géobarrière configurée"
        return response

    def _clear_geofence_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour effacer géobarrière."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service clear_geofence appelé")
        response.success = True
        response.message = "Géobarrière effacée"
        return response

    def _set_home_callback(self, request, response):
        """Service pour définir position home."""
        self.get_logger().info("Service set_home appelé")
        with self._state_lock:
            self._home_position = self._current_position
        return response

    # Méthodes utilitaires
    def _calculate_distance_to_target(self) -> float:
        """Calcule la distance au target actuel."""
        if not self._target_position:
            return 0.0
        
        dx = self._target_position.x - self._current_position.x
        dy = self._target_position.y - self._current_position.y
        dz = self._target_position.z - self._current_position.z
        return (dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) ** 0.5

    def _calculate_progress_percentage(self) -> float:
        """Calcule le pourcentage de progression."""
        if not self._waypoints or self._current_waypoint_index >= len(self._waypoints):
            return 100.0
        
        return (self._current_waypoint_index / len(self._waypoints)) * 100.0

    def _is_waypoint_reached(self) -> bool:
        """Vérifie si le waypoint actuel est atteint."""
        if not self._target_position:
            return False
        
        distance = self._calculate_distance_to_target()
        tolerance = 1.0  # mètres
        return distance < tolerance

    def _handle_waypoint_reached(self):
        """Gère l'événement waypoint atteint."""
        self.get_logger().info(f"Waypoint {self._current_waypoint_index} atteint")
        
        # Publication événement
        event_msg = String()
        event_data = {
            'waypoint_index': self._current_waypoint_index,
            'position': {
                'x': self._current_position.x,
                'y': self._current_position.y,
                'z': self._current_position.z
            },
            'timestamp': time.time()
        }
        event_msg.data = json.dumps(event_data)
        self._waypoint_reached_pub.publish(event_msg)
        
        # Passage au waypoint suivant
        self._current_waypoint_index += 1
        if self._current_waypoint_index < len(self._waypoints):
            self._target_position = self._waypoints[self._current_waypoint_index]
        else:
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.IDLE
            self.get_logger().info("Mission terminée!")

    def _handle_geofence_violation(self):
        """Gère une violation de géobarrière."""
        geofence_action = self.get_parameter('geofence.action').value
        
        if geofence_action == 'RTL':
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.RTL
        elif geofence_action == 'HOVER':
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.HOLDING
        elif geofence_action == 'STOP':
            self._emergency_stop()

    def _handle_obstacle_detection(self):
        """Gère la détection d'obstacles."""
        if self._obstacle_avoidance:
            avoidance_path = self._obstacle_avoidance.compute_avoidance_path(
                current_pos=self._current_position,
                target_pos=self._target_position
            )
            
            if avoidance_path:
                self.get_logger().info("Chemin d'évitement calculé")
                # Mise à jour de la trajectoire

    def _emergency_stop(self):
        """Arrêt d'urgence complet."""
        self.get_logger().warn("ARRÊT D'URGENCE ACTIVÉ!")
        with self._state_lock:
            self._navigation_state = NavigationState.EMERGENCY
            self._target_position = self._current_position  # Hover sur place

    def _publish_diagnostics(self):
        """Publication des diagnostics système."""
        diag_array = DiagnosticArray()
        diag_array.header.stamp = self.get_clock().now().to_msg()
        diag_array.header.frame_id = "base_link"
        
        # Diagnostic général
        nav_diag = DiagnosticStatus()
        nav_diag.name = "drone_navigation"
        nav_diag.level = DiagnosticStatus.OK
        nav_diag.message = f"Navigation state: {self._navigation_state.value}"
        
        nav_diag.values.append(KeyValue(key="state", value=self._navigation_state.value))
        nav_diag.values.append(KeyValue(key="waypoint_index", value=str(self._current_waypoint_index)))
        nav_diag.values.append(KeyValue(key="total_waypoints", value=str(len(self._waypoints))))
        
        diag_array.status.append(nav_diag)
        self._diagnostics_pub.publish(diag_array)


def main(args=None):
    """Point d'entrée principal."""
    rclpy.init(args=args)
    
    try:
        navigation_node = NavigationNode()
        
        # Utilisation d'un executor multi-threadé pour performance
        executor = MultiThreadedExecutor(num_threads=4)
        
        executor.add_node(navigation_node)
        
        navigation_node.get_logger().info("Nœud de navigation démarré. En attente de configuration...")
        
        try:
            executor.spin()
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            navigation_node.get_logger().info("Arrêt demandé par l'utilisateur")
        finally:
            executor.shutdown()
            navigation_node.destroy_node()
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Erreur fatale: {e}")
    finally:
        rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Launch file pour les outils de debug et développement.

Usage:
    ros2 launch drone_navigation debug.launch.py
    ros2 launch drone_navigation debug.launch.py enable_all:=true
    ros2 launch drone_navigation debug.launch.py tool:=visualizer
"""

from launch import LaunchDescription
from launch.actions import DeclareLaunchArgument, OpaqueFunction
from launch.conditions import IfCondition
from launch.substitutions import LaunchConfiguration, PathJoinSubstitution
from launch_ros.actions import Node
from launch_ros.substitutions import FindPackageShare
from ament_index_python.packages import get_package_share_directory
import os


def generate_launch_description():
    """Génère la description de lancement pour le debug."""
    
    # Déclaration des arguments
    declared_arguments = [
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'tool',
            default_value='all',
            description='Outil de debug à lancer (all, visualizer, monitor, analyzer, logger)',
            choices=['all', 'visualizer', 'monitor', 'analyzer', 'logger', 'recorder']
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'enable_all',
            default_value='false',
            description='Activer tous les outils de debug'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'namespace',
            default_value='debug',
            description='Namespace pour les outils de debug'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'log_level',
            default_value='debug',
            description='Niveau de log pour le debug'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'record_data',
            default_value='false',
            description='Enregistrer les données pour analyse'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'data_dir',
            default_value='/tmp/drone_navigation_debug',
            description='Répertoire pour les données de debug'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'rviz_config',
            default_value='debug.rviz',
            description='Configuration RViz pour le debug'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'use_sim_time',
            default_value='false',
            description='Utiliser le temps de simulation'
        )
    ]
    
    def launch_setup(context, *args, **kwargs):
        # Récupération des paramètres
        tool = LaunchConfiguration('tool').perform(context)
        enable_all = LaunchConfiguration('enable_all').perform(context)
        namespace = LaunchConfiguration('namespace').perform(context)
        log_level = LaunchConfiguration('log_level').perform(context)
        record_data = LaunchConfiguration('record_data').perform(context)
        data_dir = LaunchConfiguration('data_dir').perform(context)
        rviz_config = LaunchConfiguration('rviz_config').perform(context)
        use_sim_time = LaunchConfiguration('use_sim_time').perform(context)
        
        # Déterminer quels outils lancer
        launch_all = (tool == 'all' or enable_all == 'true')
        
        # Chemin des packages
        pkg_share = get_package_share_directory('drone_navigation')
        
        nodes = []
        
        # Paramètres communs
        common_params = [
            {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'},
            {'log_level': log_level},
            {'data_directory': data_dir}
        ]
        
        # === VISUALISEUR DE TRAJECTOIRES ===
        if launch_all or tool == 'visualizer':
            trajectory_visualizer = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='trajectory_visualizer',
                name='trajectory_visualizer',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=common_params + [
                    {'update_frequency': 10.0},
                    {'max_trajectory_points': 1000},
                    {'show_planned_path': True},
                    {'show_executed_path': True},
                    {'show_waypoints': True},
                    {'show_obstacles': True},
                    {'color_by_speed': True}
                ],
                arguments=['--ros-args', '--log-level', log_level]
            )
            nodes.append(trajectory_visualizer)
        
        # === MONITEUR DE PERFORMANCE ===
        if launch_all or tool == 'monitor':
            performance_monitor = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='debug_performance_monitor',
                name='performance_monitor',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=common_params + [
                    {'monitor_frequency': 5.0},
                    {'track_cpu_usage': True},
                    {'track_memory_usage': True},
                    {'track_computation_time': True},
                    {'track_loop_frequency': True},
                    {'alert_thresholds': {
                        'cpu_usage': 80.0,
                        'memory_usage': 85.0,
                        'loop_frequency_min': 15.0
                    }}
                ],
                arguments=['--ros-args', '--log-level', log_level]
            )
            nodes.append(performance_monitor)
        
        # === ANALYSEUR DE DONNÉES ===
        if launch_all or tool == 'analyzer':
            data_analyzer = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='debug_data_analyzer',
                name='data_analyzer',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=common_params + [
                    {'analysis_frequency': 1.0},
                    {'window_size': 100},  # Nombre d'échantillons
                    {'analyze_trajectory_quality': True},
                    {'analyze_control_performance': True},
                    {'analyze_algorithm_efficiency': True},
                    {'generate_statistics': True},
                    {'export_analysis': record_data == 'true'}
                ],
                arguments=['--ros-args', '--log-level', log_level]
            )
            nodes.append(data_analyzer)
        
        # === LOGGER AVANCÉ ===
        if launch_all or tool == 'logger':
            advanced_logger = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='debug_logger',
                name='advanced_logger',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=common_params + [
                    {'log_frequency': 20.0},
                    {'log_trajectory_data': True},
                    {'log_control_signals': True},
                    {'log_sensor_data': True},
                    {'log_algorithm_internals': True},
                    {'log_format': 'json'},  # json, csv, binary
                    {'compress_logs': True},
                    {'max_log_size': 100},  # MB
                    {'log_rotation': True}
                ],
                arguments=['--ros-args', '--log-level', log_level]
            )
            nodes.append(advanced_logger)
        
        # === ENREGISTREUR DE DONNÉES ===
        if launch_all or tool == 'recorder' or record_data == 'true':
            data_recorder = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='debug_data_recorder',
                name='data_recorder',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=common_params + [
                    {'record_all_topics': False},
                    {'topics_to_record': [
                        '/drone_nav/status',
                        '/drone_nav/trajectory',
                        '/drone_nav/current_waypoint',
                        '/mavros/local_position/pose',
                        '/mavros/local_position/velocity_local',
                        '/mavros/setpoint_position/local'
                    ]},
                    {'record_services': True},
                    {'record_parameters': True},
                    {'bag_format': 'sqlite3'},  # sqlite3, mcap
                    {'compression': 'zstd'},
                    {'max_bag_size': 1000},  # MB
                    {'split_bags': True}
                ],
                arguments=['--ros-args', '--log-level', log_level]
            )
            nodes.append(data_recorder)
        
        # === RVIZ POUR DEBUG ===
        rviz_config_path = os.path.join(pkg_share, 'rviz', rviz_config)
        if os.path.exists(rviz_config_path):
            rviz_debug = Node(
                package='rviz2',
                executable='rviz2',
                name='rviz2_debug',
                namespace=namespace,
                arguments=['-d', rviz_config_path],
                output='screen',
                parameters=[{'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'}]
            )
            nodes.append(rviz_debug)
        
        # === NŒUD DE CONTRÔLE DEBUG ===
        debug_controller = Node(
            package='drone_navigation',
            executable='debug_controller',
            name='debug_controller',
            namespace=namespace,
            output='screen',
            parameters=common_params + [
                {'enable_interactive_debugging': True},
                {'enable_step_by_step': False},
                {'enable_breakpoints': False},
                {'debug_topics': [
                    '/drone_nav/debug/trajectory_planning',
                    '/drone_nav/debug/position_control',
                    '/drone_nav/debug/obstacle_avoidance'
                ]}
            ],
            arguments=['--ros-args', '--log-level', log_level]
        )
        nodes.append(debug_controller)
        
        # === NŒUD D'INJECTION DE FAUTES ===
        fault_injector = Node(
            package='drone_navigation',
            executable='debug_fault_injector',
            name='fault_injector',
            namespace=namespace,
            output='screen',
            parameters=common_params + [
                {'enable_fault_injection': False},  # Désactivé par défaut
                {'fault_types': ['sensor_noise', 'communication_delay', 'computation_error']},
                {'fault_probability': 0.01},  # 1% de chance
                {'fault_duration': 5.0},  # secondes
                {'safety_limits': True}  # Respecter les limites de sécurité
            ],
            arguments=['--ros-args', '--log-level', log_level]
        )
        nodes.append(fault_injector)
        
        # === NŒUD DE MÉTRIQUES EN TEMPS RÉEL ===
        realtime_metrics = Node(
            package='drone_navigation',
            executable='debug_realtime_metrics',
            name='realtime_metrics',
            namespace=namespace,
            output='screen',
            parameters=common_params + [
                {'publish_frequency': 10.0},
                {'metrics_topics': {
                    'position_error': '/debug/metrics/position_error',
                    'velocity_error': '/debug/metrics/velocity_error',
                    'computation_time': '/debug/metrics/computation_time',
                    'memory_usage': '/debug/metrics/memory_usage',
                    'algorithm_performance': '/debug/metrics/algorithm_performance'
                }},
                {'enable_alerts': True},
                {'alert_thresholds': {
                    'position_error_max': 5.0,  # mètres
                    'velocity_error_max': 2.0,  # m/s
                    'computation_time_max': 0.1,  # secondes
                    'memory_usage_max': 80.0  # pourcentage
                }}
            ],
            arguments=['--ros-args', '--log-level', log_level]
        )
        nodes.append(realtime_metrics)
        
        # === NŒUD DE VALIDATION EN TEMPS RÉEL ===
        realtime_validator = Node(
            package='drone_navigation',
            executable='debug_realtime_validator',
            name='realtime_validator',
            namespace=namespace,
            output='screen',
            parameters=common_params + [
                {'validation_frequency': 5.0},
                {'validate_trajectory_feasibility': True},
                {'validate_control_signals': True},
                {'validate_safety_constraints': True},
                {'validate_algorithm_outputs': True},
                {'stop_on_validation_error': False},
                {'log_validation_results': True}
            ],
            arguments=['--ros-args', '--log-level', log_level]
        )
        nodes.append(realtime_validator)
        
        return nodes
    
    # Description de lancement
    ld = LaunchDescription()
    
    # Ajout des arguments
    for arg in declared_arguments:
        ld.add_action(arg)
    
    # Ajout de la fonction de setup
    ld.add_action(OpaqueFunction(function=launch_setup))
    
    return ld
