#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Launch file principal pour le système de navigation drone.

Usage:
    ros2 launch drone_navigation navigation.launch.py
    ros2 launch drone_navigation navigation.launch.py config_file:=custom_config.yaml
    ros2 launch drone_navigation navigation.launch.py debug:=true
"""

from launch import LaunchDescription
from launch.actions import DeclareLaunchArgument, OpaqueFunction, GroupAction
from launch.conditions import IfCondition, UnlessCondition
from launch.substitutions import LaunchConfiguration, PathJoinSubstitution
from launch_ros.actions import Node, LoadComposableNodes
from launch_ros.descriptions import ComposableNode
from launch_ros.substitutions import FindPackageShare
from ament_index_python.packages import get_package_share_directory
import os


def generate_launch_description():
    """Génère la description de lancement."""
    
    # Déclaration des arguments de lancement
    declared_arguments = [
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'config_file',
            default_value='navigation_params.yaml',
            description='Fichier de configuration à utiliser'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'namespace',
            default_value='',
            description='Namespace pour les nœuds'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'debug',
            default_value='false',
            description='Activer le mode debug'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'use_sim_time',
            default_value='false',
            description='Utiliser le temps de simulation'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'log_level',
            default_value='info',
            description='Niveau de log (debug, info, warn, error)'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'enable_diagnostics',
            default_value='true',
            description='Activer les diagnostics'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'launch_rviz',
            default_value='false',
            description='Lancer RViz automatiquement'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'rviz_config',
            default_value='navigation.rviz',
            description='Fichier de configuration RViz'
        )
    ]
    
    # Fonction pour créer les nœuds
    def launch_setup(context, *args, **kwargs):
        # Récupération des paramètres
        namespace = LaunchConfiguration('namespace').perform(context)
        config_file = LaunchConfiguration('config_file').perform(context)
        debug = LaunchConfiguration('debug').perform(context)
        use_sim_time = LaunchConfiguration('use_sim_time').perform(context)
        log_level = LaunchConfiguration('log_level').perform(context)
        enable_diagnostics = LaunchConfiguration('enable_diagnostics').perform(context)
        launch_rviz = LaunchConfiguration('launch_rviz').perform(context)
        rviz_config = LaunchConfiguration('rviz_config').perform(context)
        
        # Chemin vers les fichiers de configuration
        pkg_share = get_package_share_directory('drone_navigation')
        config_path = os.path.join(pkg_share, 'config', config_file)
        pid_config_path = os.path.join(pkg_share, 'config', 'pid_tuning.yaml')
        geofence_config_path = os.path.join(pkg_share, 'config', 'geofence_zones.yaml')
        coverage_config_path = os.path.join(pkg_share, 'config', 'coverage_patterns.yaml')
        
        # Paramètres communs
        common_parameters = [
            config_path,
            pid_config_path,
            geofence_config_path,
            coverage_config_path,
            {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'},
            {'log_level': log_level}
        ]
        
        # Arguments communs
        common_arguments = [
            '--ros-args',
            '--log-level', log_level
        ]
        
        if debug == 'true':
            common_arguments.extend(['--enable-stdout-logs'])
        
        # Liste des nœuds à lancer
        nodes = []
        
        # === NŒUD PRINCIPAL DE NAVIGATION ===
        navigation_node = Node(
            package='drone_navigation',
            executable='navigation_node',
            name='navigation_node',
            namespace=namespace,
            output='screen',
            parameters=common_parameters,
            arguments=common_arguments,
            respawn=True,
            respawn_delay=2.0
        )
        nodes.append(navigation_node)
        
        # === NŒUDS DE DIAGNOSTIC (optionnel) ===
        if enable_diagnostics == 'true':
            # Nœud de monitoring des performances
            performance_monitor = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='performance_monitor',
                name='performance_monitor',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=common_parameters,
                arguments=common_arguments,
                condition=IfCondition(LaunchConfiguration('enable_diagnostics'))
            )
            nodes.append(performance_monitor)
            
            # Nœud de diagnostics système
            system_diagnostics = Node(
                package='diagnostic_updater',
                executable='diagnostic_aggregator',
                name='diagnostic_aggregator',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=[{
                    'analyzers': {
                        'navigation': {
                            'type': 'diagnostic_aggregator/AnalyzerGroup',
                            'path': 'Navigation',
                            'analyzers': {
                                'navigation_node': {
                                    'type': 'diagnostic_aggregator/GenericAnalyzer',
                                    'path': 'Navigation Node',
                                    'find_and_remove_prefix': ['navigation_node']
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }],
                condition=IfCondition(LaunchConfiguration('enable_diagnostics'))
            )
            nodes.append(system_diagnostics)
        
        # === RVIZ (optionnel) ===
        if launch_rviz == 'true':
            rviz_config_path = os.path.join(pkg_share, 'rviz', rviz_config)
            if os.path.exists(rviz_config_path):
                rviz_node = Node(
                    package='rviz2',
                    executable='rviz2',
                    name='rviz2',
                    namespace=namespace,
                    arguments=['-d', rviz_config_path],
                    output='screen',
                    condition=IfCondition(LaunchConfiguration('launch_rviz'))
                )
                nodes.append(rviz_node)
        
        # === STATIC TRANSFORMS ===
        # Transform pour le frame de référence
        static_transform_publisher = Node(
            package='tf2_ros',
            executable='static_transform_publisher',
            name='map_to_base_link_publisher',
            namespace=namespace,
            arguments=['0', '0', '0', '0', '0', '0', 'map', 'base_link'],
            output='screen'
        )
        nodes.append(static_transform_publisher)
        
        # === ROBOT STATE PUBLISHER (si nécessaire) ===
        # Note: Normalement fourni par le package drone_interface
        
        return nodes
    
    # Description de lancement
    ld = LaunchDescription()
    
    # Ajout des arguments déclarés
    for arg in declared_arguments:
        ld.add_action(arg)
    
    # Ajout de la fonction de setup
    ld.add_action(OpaqueFunction(function=launch_setup))
    
    return ld

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Launch file pour les tests de navigation.

Usage:
    ros2 launch drone_navigation test_navigation.launch.py
    ros2 launch drone_navigation test_navigation.launch.py test_type:=waypoints
    ros2 launch drone_navigation test_navigation.launch.py test_type:=coverage auto_start:=true
"""

from launch import LaunchDescription
from launch.actions import DeclareLaunchArgument, OpaqueFunction, TimerAction, ExecuteProcess
from launch.conditions import IfCondition
from launch.substitutions import LaunchConfiguration, PathJoinSubstitution
from launch_ros.actions import Node
from launch_ros.substitutions import FindPackageShare
from ament_index_python.packages import get_package_share_directory
import os


def generate_launch_description():
    """Génère la description de lancement pour les tests."""
    
    # Déclaration des arguments
    declared_arguments = [
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'test_type',
            default_value='basic',
            description='Type de test (basic, waypoints, coverage, stress, performance)',
            choices=['basic', 'waypoints', 'coverage', 'stress', 'performance']
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'auto_start',
            default_value='false',
            description='Démarrer automatiquement les tests'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'test_duration',
            default_value='300',
            description='Durée du test en secondes'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'namespace',
            default_value='test',
            description='Namespace pour les nœuds de test'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'log_level',
            default_value='info',
            description='Niveau de log'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'save_results',
            default_value='true',
            description='Sauvegarder les résultats de test'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'results_dir',
            default_value='/tmp/drone_navigation_tests',
            description='Répertoire pour sauvegarder les résultats'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'use_sim_time',
            default_value='true',
            description='Utiliser le temps de simulation'
        ),
        DeclareLaunchArgument(
            'launch_navigation',
            default_value='true',
            description='Lancer aussi le système de navigation'
        )
    ]
    
    def launch_setup(context, *args, **kwargs):
        # Récupération des paramètres
        test_type = LaunchConfiguration('test_type').perform(context)
        auto_start = LaunchConfiguration('auto_start').perform(context)
        test_duration = LaunchConfiguration('test_duration').perform(context)
        namespace = LaunchConfiguration('namespace').perform(context)
        log_level = LaunchConfiguration('log_level').perform(context)
        save_results = LaunchConfiguration('save_results').perform(context)
        results_dir = LaunchConfiguration('results_dir').perform(context)
        use_sim_time = LaunchConfiguration('use_sim_time').perform(context)
        launch_navigation = LaunchConfiguration('launch_navigation').perform(context)
        
        # Chemin des packages
        pkg_share = get_package_share_directory('drone_navigation')
        
        nodes = []
        
        # === LANCEMENT DU SYSTÈME DE NAVIGATION ===
        if launch_navigation == 'true':
            navigation_launch = ExecuteProcess(
                cmd=[
                    'ros2', 'launch', 'drone_navigation', 'navigation.launch.py',
                    f'use_sim_time:={use_sim_time}',
                    f'log_level:={log_level}',
                    'enable_diagnostics:=true'
                ],
                output='screen',
                condition=IfCondition(LaunchConfiguration('launch_navigation'))
            )
            nodes.append(navigation_launch)
        
        # === NŒUD DE CONTRÔLE DES TESTS ===
        test_controller = Node(
            package='drone_navigation',
            executable='test_controller',
            name='test_controller',
            namespace=namespace,
            output='screen',
            parameters=[
                {'test_type': test_type},
                {'test_duration': int(test_duration)},
                {'auto_start': auto_start == 'true'},
                {'save_results': save_results == 'true'},
                {'results_directory': results_dir},
                {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'},
                {'log_level': log_level}
            ],
            arguments=[
                '--ros-args',
                '--log-level', log_level
            ]
        )
        nodes.append(test_controller)
        
        # === NŒUDS SPÉCIFIQUES PAR TYPE DE TEST ===
        
        if test_type == 'waypoints':
            # Test de navigation par waypoints
            waypoint_tester = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='waypoint_tester',
                name='waypoint_tester',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=[
                    {'test_patterns': ['square', 'triangle', 'circle']},
                    {'timeout_per_waypoint': 60.0},
                    {'precision_threshold': 1.0},
                    {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'}
                ]
            )
            nodes.append(waypoint_tester)
            
        elif test_type == 'coverage':
            # Test de motifs de couverture
            coverage_tester = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='coverage_tester',
                name='coverage_tester',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=[
                    {'test_patterns': ['zigzag', 'spiral', 'lawn_mower']},
                    {'test_areas': ['square_10x10', 'rectangle_20x10', 'irregular']},
                    {'coverage_threshold': 0.95},
                    {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'}
                ]
            )
            nodes.append(coverage_tester)
            
        elif test_type == 'stress':
            # Test de stress
            stress_tester = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='stress_tester',
                name='stress_tester',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=[
                    {'max_waypoints': 1000},
                    {'rapid_changes': True},
                    {'resource_monitoring': True},
                    {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'}
                ]
            )
            nodes.append(stress_tester)
            
        elif test_type == 'performance':
            # Test de performance
            performance_tester = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='performance_tester',
                name='performance_tester',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=[
                    {'benchmark_algorithms': ['a_star', 'rrt_star', 'dijkstra']},
                    {'measure_computation_time': True},
                    {'measure_memory_usage': True},
                    {'measure_accuracy': True},
                    {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'}
                ]
            )
            nodes.append(performance_tester)
        
        # === NŒUD DE MONITORING DES TESTS ===
        test_monitor = Node(
            package='drone_navigation',
            executable='test_monitor',
            name='test_monitor',
            namespace=namespace,
            output='screen',
            parameters=[
                {'monitor_frequency': 1.0},
                {'collect_metrics': True},
                {'publish_status': True},
                {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'}
            ]
        )
        nodes.append(test_monitor)
        
        # === NŒUD DE VALIDATION ===
        test_validator = Node(
            package='drone_navigation',
            executable='test_validator',
            name='test_validator',
            namespace=namespace,
            output='screen',
            parameters=[
                {'validation_criteria_file': os.path.join(pkg_share, 'config', 'test_validation.yaml')},
                {'strict_validation': True},
                {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'}
            ]
        )
        nodes.append(test_validator)
        
        # === NŒUD DE GÉNÉRATION DE RAPPORTS ===
        if save_results == 'true':
            report_generator = Node(
                package='drone_navigation',
                executable='test_report_generator',
                name='report_generator',
                namespace=namespace,
                output='screen',
                parameters=[
                    {'output_directory': results_dir},
                    {'generate_plots': True},
                    {'generate_html_report': True},
                    {'generate_pdf_report': False},
                    {'use_sim_time': use_sim_time == 'true'}
                ]
            )
            nodes.append(report_generator)
        
        # === DÉMARRAGE AUTOMATIQUE (avec délai) ===
        if auto_start == 'true':
            auto_start_action = TimerAction(
                period=5.0,  # Attendre 5 secondes
                actions=[
                    ExecuteProcess(
                        cmd=[
                            'ros2', 'service', 'call',
                            f'/{namespace}/test_controller/start_test',
                            'std_srvs/srv/Trigger',
                            '{}'
                        ],
                        output='screen'
                    )
                ]
            )
            nodes.append(auto_start_action)
        
        # === ARRÊT AUTOMATIQUE (après durée) ===
        if auto_start == 'true':
            auto_stop_action = TimerAction(
                period=float(test_duration) + 10.0,  # Durée + marge
                actions=[
                    ExecuteProcess(
                        cmd=[
                            'ros2', 'service', 'call',
                            f'/{namespace}/test_controller/stop_test',
                            'std_srvs/srv/Trigger',
                            '{}'
                        ],
                        output='screen'
                    )
                ]
            )
            nodes.append(auto_stop_action)
        
        return nodes
    
    # Description de lancement
    ld = LaunchDescription()
    
    # Ajout des arguments
    for arg in declared_arguments:
        ld.add_action(arg)
    
    # Ajout de la fonction de setup
    ld.add_action(OpaqueFunction(function=launch_setup))
    
    return ld
